



  Earliest Humans--42 lakh years old; Present Modern Human--50,000 years ago; Oldest Specimen in India(Homo Erectus)--Narmada valley


    		Earliest Stone tool use in India--Shivalik Hills (20 lakh years old)





  Lower Paleolithic(6 lakh-60,000BC) --Handaxes and Cleaver; Tools found all over India except Indus, Ganga and Brahmaputra plains


           Sites:- Pahalgam(Kashmir); Belan Valley(Allahbad); Bhimbetka and Adamgarh in MP; Hunsgi(Karnataka); Attirampakkam(TN)


                                        


   Middle Paleolithic(1,50,000-40,000BC)-- Flake tools(Small and medium sized Handaxes, knives and cleavers made from pebbles); Central India, Deccan, RJ, TN, KN, Orissa


          Sites:- Rohiri Hills of Sindh; Bhimbetka; Nevasa; Pushkar; Samnapur on Narmada





   Upper Paleolithic(45,000-10,000 BC)-- Parallel Sided Blades;Larger Blade tools upto 8cm ;Composite tools ; Ganga and Belan Valley, Central and West India, Gujrat, AP and Karnataka





Mesolithic Culture:-aka Late stone age or Microlithic period( Decrease in size of stone tools)
  
   i)Practised some form of Agriculture( Sites- Mahadah and Dumdum in Ganga plains)
  ii)Were in touch with Harappan People(Bagor in Rj and Langhhanj sites have evidence for this)
 iii)Sheep and Goat domestication
  iv)Rock Painting sites--Bhimbetka, Lakha Juhar(MP), Kupagallu(Kn), Murhana Pahar(UP) 

  iii)Other sites:- Bhimbetka, Adamgarh 


   Meolithic Age: Domestication of Plants , Animals
   
   Rice Domestication----7000BC( Koldihawa, Belan Valley)----------->Oldest in the world
   
   Wheat and Barley Domestication---7000BC(West Asia)
   
   Animal Domestication----16,000BC(Aq Kupruq, Afghanistan)
   
   Pots and Pans; Wheelmade----6000BC(Mehargarh)

Sites:- Gufkral and Burzahom(J and K); Mahagar, Chopani Mando and Koldihawa(Belan Valley, UP); Chirand(Bihar); Sarutaru(Guwahati);
 Kodekal,Utnur,Nagarjunkonda,Palavo(Andhra); Hallur,Maski,Brahmagiri(Karnataka); Paiyampalli(TN) -----Southern  Neolithic Age (2500-800BC); 3 Phases
                                                                                                      i)Phase I- No Metals
                                                                                                     ii)Phase II- Copper and Bronze Tools; DOmestication of Plants and Animals
                                                                                                    iii)Phase III- Use of Iron




Chalcolithic Age: After the Neolithic Age, Civilization arose in West India, but in Central and South India, the level of Urbanisation was not the same and a new Chalcolithic age began
                   Common:- i)Painted Ceramic Ware( Black on Red)
                           ii)Specialised Blade and Flake Industry
                          iii)Economy based on Subsistence agriculture, hunting and fishing
   
   Example:- Ahar; Kayath; Malwa; Savalda; Jorwe; Prabhas; Rangpur--------> All of them made distinct painted pottery
   
   Sites:- Balathal, Navdatoli, Eran, Nagada(Narmada Valley); Rangpur(Gujrat);  Prakash, Demabad and Imangaon (Maharashtra)------------>Almost all in Black Cotton Soil zone
      
          i)Planted crop in rotation , both kharif and Rabi crops
   
         ii)Ochere Colored Potter(OCP)- Ganga Valley; Pottery with Bright red slip and painted in black
 ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Indus valley civilisation 

Cities:- Nageshwar, Rangpur, Amri, Balakot, Ganweriwala, Mitathal, Manda 

Agriculture:- Whear, Barley, Lentis, Chickpea, Sesame                          Rice Evidence found mostly from Gujrat and Haryana    Cotton from Mehargarh
Animals:- Cattle, Sheep, Goat, Buffallo, pig, Boar, dear, gharial.             Terracotta Model of Plough found at mohanjodaro and Kalibanga
	. Terracota Model of plough at Cholistan and Banawali
	. Evidence of 2 different crops grown together. 

Citadel was physically seperated from Lower city by bricked walls( Except Lothal ). Lower town was also walled.

Pit Burials with Jewellery for afterlife for few but not for everyone. Fire Altars were found at Kalibanga and Lothal. 

Channudaro was a small settlement dedicated entirely for craft production. 

Bought Nickle and copper from OMAN(Rasal Janyaz). 

John Marshall in 1924 announced the discovery of this news civilisation of Harappa. Although the 1st Harappan Seal was presented to Alexander Cunningham in 1875 itsef. 

Terracotta Model of Plough found at mohanjodaro and Kalibanga


Geographical Extenet----         1600km East-West; 
 1400 Km North-South;
  Area=12lakh sq km;
   80%sites on River plains between Indus and Ganga 
    
    
    
    West upto Sutkangekor(Balochistan)                         i)Most of 1400 settlements=Small villages (<10Hq)
    
    East upto Alamgirpur(UP)                                  ii)MOhanjodaro(250 hc), Harappa(150), Ganawariwala(80), Kalibangan(100), Rakhigarhi(80), Dholavira(100)
    
    South upto Daimabad(MH)
    
    North upto Manda(J and K)
 

Trade
    
    i)Copper from Khetri Mines in Rajasthan
  
   ii)Chert Blades from Rohri Hills of Sindh
 
  iii)Carnelian Beada from sindh                                                                Early Harappan: 3500-2600 BC
 
   iv)Lead from South India                                                                     Mature haprappan: 2600-1900 BC
 
    v)Lapis-Lazuli from Kashmir and Afghanistan                                                 Late Harappan: 1900-1300 BC
 
   vi)turquoise and Jade from central Asia/Iran
 
  vii)Chalcedony and Carnelian from Saurashtra

Script:- Right to Left
Evidence of Animal Worship.

VEDIC AGE



Kula(Family)-->Gram(Village)-->Vis(Clan)-->Jan(People)-->Rashtra(Country)
(Griha--Home)  (Head=Gramini)  (Vispati)   Ex:-Yadav-jana, Bharat jana-----Ruler of Jan--Rajan(King)------> For his services was paid 'Bali'-A vouluntary donation, even by defeated folks
                                                                                                                                       In Later Vedic became a Regular Tax.
They had spies and Diplomats

In Rig Vedic times on copper was used, which was called "Ayas", Later Iron was also used  and then Copper was called 'Lohit ayas' and Iron as 'Syam Ayas'. Silver--'Rajat'.

Currency=Nishka and Satman [But not standardized]

Indra is referred as Purandar--Lord of Forts

700BC- Northern Blacked Polished Ware came to be manufactured.

Sabha also used to function as a court of Justice.

Daughters could Inherit property if she was the only child.

Guilds of Crafstmen came into existence in Later Vedic Age. Head of Guild was called 'Sreshthi'.

Education of Childern began with 'Upanayan' Ceremony--Was considered the 2nd Birth of child and after it he was considered 'Dvij' (Twice born).

Gargi and Maitreyi were female intellectuals. 

Officials
    i)Suta--Chairoteer

   ii)Sangrahitri--Treasury-wala

  iii)Bhag-Dugh-- Tax-Collector

   iv)Gramini-- Village headmen

    v)Sthapati-- Chief Judge

   vi)Takshan-- Carpenter

  vii)Kshatri-- Chamberlain(Who managed the household of kings)

MAHAJANPADAS


NorthernMost+Westernmost---Kamboj(Peshawar)

Southernmost---Asmak(near Godavari river)

East---ANGA, in Bihar

Important Gan-Sanghs--> i)Mallas ii)Sakyas iii)Koliyas iv)Moriyas v)Bulis vi)Kalams vii)Bhaggas viii)Lichhvis





MAGADH
  i)Bimbisara of Haryanka Dynasty, after overthrowing Brihadrath. 	Attained supremacy under Ajjatashatru.    His son Udayi founded Patliputra. 	
 ii)Haryanks were followed by SISUNAGS.       Mahapadma Nanda then took control. 


Alexander crossed Khyber pass in 326 BC. 


In 1830s James Princed deciphered the Brahmi and Kharoshti script. 

3...2..1 BC, it's the Mauryan Empire Bitch.

 Jain Text Parisisth Parvam describes how CM captured magadh from Nandas.







Ashokan Pillar:
     i)Essance of All religions/Root---> Mulla

    ii)No criticism of other religions--->Vachaguti

   iii)Coming together people of different relion in assemblies--->Samavaya

    iv)Learning texts of different religion--->Bahusruta

 
Ashokan Pillars :- Nigga-Sagar, Rum-dede, Rampurwa, Laundia- Nadan, Sarnath, Arora, Kausambhi, Gujjar,  Meerut, Toprank, Sanchi 

Major Rock Edicts :- Man-Shera, Shah-Bano-Garh, Kandahar, Kalki, Girnar, Supari, Sannata, Juggad, Shishpal-kaka 

Minor Rock Edicts :- Bai-Rat, Maski, Ude-Gollu, Raju-ka-Mandir, Nittu, Brahmagiri, Siddha-pur, Jati-Rameshwar, Palki-Gunndi, Go-do-Math

There were committees for Military activity, including Navy. 
Dhamma Mahamata were appointed to spread Dhamma. 
Transplantaion and Iron tipped Plough increased production in Ganga and Kaveri areas. 


	- Ashoka ne, Rajukas appint kiye the : TO reward and punish people. 
	
	- Rumindel Pillar Inscription : Ashoka
	
	- Adyaksha : suprintendents to regulate economic activities of the state 

Pativedak= Reporter 

King issued Ordinances called 'Sasans'. 

Adhyaksha-->Superintendent 

Yukta--> Subordinate Officers in charge of revenues of the king

Rajjukas---> Officers for land measurement and fixing their boundries---Had powers to punish the guilty. 

Pardeshikas---Either Revenue collector or Governor of a province. 








Slavery did not exist, but Forced and bonded labour was present. The condition of widows was good. 

Rudradaman's Inscription at Junagadh---Tells about repairement of Sudarshan lake by one of Chandragupta's Governor.


Mauryan Art:-
   i)Rock cut caves at Barabar and Nagarjuni Caves.

  ii)Ashokan Pillar with lion Capital at Lauriya-Nandingraha




SATVAHNAS(7-Riders) a.k.a ANDHRAS
  i)Before Satvahnas in Maharashtra and CCP in TN, South India was dominated by Megalithic people.
 ii)Mentioned in Aiteraya Brahmans. and by Pliny as well.  Part of Mauryan empire but declared themselves free. Founder=Simuka(235-213 BC).
iii)3rd Ruler= Satkarni; Performed Asvamedh; Nanaghat Inscription; Conquered Narmada Valley; "lord of dakshinpath"; Name occurs at Sanchi Stupa(Gave donations for restorations)
 Iv)Greatest= Gautmiputra Satkarni; Nasik Inscription---"Destroyer of Sakas, Yavanas and pahalavas" 


INDO-GREEKS
 i)Menandar--Converted to Buddhism by Nagasena. His questions to Nagasena are recorded in book "Milindpanho". 
ii)Greeks- 1st to have portrait of kings on coins + 1st to issue Gold Coins. 




SAKAS(1st CE BC-4th CE AD)---Pushed towars India by the Kushans
  i)Rudradaman--Junagadh Inscription. Repaired Sudarshan lake. Capital=Ujjayani 
 ii)Saka Rule came to end at by defeat of last king by Chandragupta II in 390 AD. 




KUSHANS(Yueh-Chi)--Came to India due to Great wall of China

   i)Kanishka(78AD)--Founded Saka Era; Capital= Peshawar; Held 4th Buddhist Council----Under joint leadership of Vasumitra and Ashvaghosh; Mahayan Buddhism came into being

  ii)His court--i)Vasumitra ii)Ashvaghosh iii)Parsava iv)Charak v)Nagarjun 

 iii)Taxila and Mathura emerged as Great centre of learning. 


    Asvaghosh wrote---BUDHHACHARITA; SAUNDAR-ANANDA; Vajra-Suchi



MAURYAS---> Satvahnas in Deccan, CCP in South and Shakas(200BC)----> Kushans(100BC-100AD)---->Guptas(400AD)---->Chalukyas in Deccan and Pallavas in Tamil Nadu( 500- 600AD). 	



Shaka Ruler Rudradaman Repaired Sudarshan Lake.

Kushans called themselves Devputras( Sons of gods ). They were the 1st to issue Gold coins. ((Yadheyas were a Punjabi tribal Republic who issued siver coins))

Prayag Prashasti a.k.a Allahbad Inscription contains praise of Samundragupta by Harisena(Green-Army). 

Jatakas were written in Pali around 100BC. "Ganda-Tendua Jatka" is a story of wicked king. 




GUPTAS
  i)Allahbad Pillar Inscription(Harishena)- Samundragupta ; Mentions Maharaja Sri-Gupta(Founder) and Ghatotkatch. 

 ii)320 AD--> ChandraGupta--Laid Foundation of Great Gupta Empire.  His son--SamundraGupta (Greatest) a.k.a Kaviraj(King of poets)--His son-> ChandraGupta II( Adopted title of Vikramaditya)

iii)Mehrauli Iron Pillar Inscription--ChandraGupta II. Fa-Hien visited during his time. His Grandson, Skandgupta restored Sudarsan lake. 

 iv)'Atavik-Raja'- Forest Kings; 'Paricharak'- Helpers 


Banna-Bhatta wrote Harscharitta-Manas for Duh, he also wrote a novel called "Kadambari". 


Prabhavati Gupta was daughter of ChandraGupta II, and was married to Vak-Attacks. She was very powerfull and had land rights( uncommon ).

                                                                                 SOUTH-INDIA

Megalithic Period--i)Burrials with abundance of Iron tools and Red and black pottery. 
                  ii)Description not found in any sanskrit or Prakrit text, but in Some tamil texts.





Rock Edict 2 and 13 of ASHOKA mentions southern Kingdoms of Chera, Chola, Pandya, Satyaputra, Keralaputra and Tambapanni. 

Megasthenes also mentions these states. 

Hathigumpha Inscription of Kharvela mentions defeating Tamil kings. 




Sangams were organised by Pandayan Kings. 

Sangam texts---Based on 30,000 lines of Poetry(In praise of their kings) arranged in 2 main groups--i)Pati-Ne-kill-kanakku(My husband killed Kannu) ii)Pattu-Pattu ; i) is older

                i)Describe events upto 4th AD

               ii)Earliest= Ettutogai

              iii)Tirrukural of Tiruvalluvar-- One of the best Didactic Poem

               iv)2 Epics---i)Sillapadikaram ii)Manimekhalai



CHOLAS
   i)Delta of Kaveri region including Kanchi. North-East of Panyan kingdom. Capital=Uraiyar in Trichi, later Puhar(a.k.a Kaveripatnam).

  ii)Greatest Early Chola king= Karaikal--> Invaded Sri-Lanka; Defeated C+P; built Irrigation Channels; Powerful navy; 





PANDYAS

    i)Capital=Madurai; Port city=Korkai; Sent envoys to Roman emperor Augustus and Trojan


CHERAS
   i)Greatest=Sengutturan; 

Landowners=Vellalar

Ploughmen= Uzhavar (As mentioned in Sangam texts)

Slaves= Adimai 

Agrahar=Land granted to Brahman, he could collect taxes from it. ( He sort-off became the Samanta of that land )

Masattu-van= Tamil Merchant

Setthis and Sattavahs= Merchants in prakrit  

KutagraShala= A hut where debates would take place.                


Alvar Saints:- Andal(F), Tondara-Dippodi ----Their poems are compiled in Divyaprabhandhan

Nayanar Saints:- Appar, Sundar, samundar----Their poems are compilled in Tevaram. Karaikkal-AmmaYar(F)

Nayannars opposed Buddhism and Jainism.

        Fa-Hien--5th CE

Inscriptions:--
      i)Junagadh Inscription--Rudradaman---Chaste Sanskrit; 2nd CE AD
     ii)Allahbad Pillar Inscription--Samundragupta--Sanskrit
    iii)Asshole Inscription--Pulkeshin II(chalukya)
     iv)Gwalior Inscription--Raja Bhoj----(Knowledge of Tax-Free land to Brahmins known as Agrahar)

                                                                                                                                                                      ॐ; ॐ;ॐ;ॐ;ॐ;ॐ;ॐ;ॐ;   HINDUISM ॐ;ॐ;ॐ;ॐ;ॐ;ॐ;ॐ;ॐ; 


VEDAS
    i)Rig Veda- Collection of Hymns

   ii)Samveda- Collection of songs mostly taken from Rig Veda

  iii)Yajurveda-Collection of sacrificail formulae

   iv)Atharvaveda- Collection of spells and Charms
 

Brahmans:- Meaning of Vedas; Their applications; Stories of their origins etc  [Rituals and Philosophy]



18 main Purans, 18 subsidiary purans---> Coronation of Parikshit marks the begining of Kalyug 


As per Purans--Subject matters of History---

        i)Evolution of Universe--Sarga

       ii)Involution of Universe--Pratisarga 

      iii)Recurring of Time--Manavtantar  

       iv)Geneological list of kings and sages--Vamsa

        v)Stories of some characters--Vamsanucharita


Narration of Purans was an annual ritual during festivals and rainy seasons. 

     Kalhan's Rajataringini ---Also a work of History. Critical method of Historical Research



6 limbs of Vedas(Vedang)

     i)Phonetics--Shiksha

    ii)Rituals--Kalpa

   iii)Grammer--Vyakran                  Panini's Ashtadhyayi--Book on Grammer

    iv)Etymology--Nirukta

     v)Metrics--Channda

    vi)Astronomy--Jyotish

    Later Vedic Texts
         i)Brahmanss---Elaborate Vedic Rituals                             [Arayank=Ritualistic(Karmakand); Brahmans=Philosophical]

        ii)Arayank and Upanishads---Dicussions of Philosophy and Spirituality; Partly included in the Brahmans, Partly seperate work; Arayanks=Concluding portions of Brahmanas

       iii)Purans---Historical accounts

        iv)Dharmasutras and Smritis--- Rules and regulations for general public   (Compiled bw 600-200BC); Manusmriti and Arthashastra are one of them;   Vishakhadatta's Mudrarkshas also.


               Kalidas' Malvikaginmitra(Friendly-Fire)---Pussymitra Sunga

               Banabhatta's Harshcharitta--- Harsha

               Vakpati's Gaudavaho--- Yasovarman of Kannauj 

               Balhan's Vikramdevcharitta---Chalukyan king Vikramaditya

               Jaysimha's Kumarpalcharitta

               Hemchandra's Mahakavya

               Chandbardai's Prithvirajcharitta


2 most Important and oldest Upanishads are i)Chhandogya and ii)BrihadAryank; Other:- Kathak, Isa, Mundak, Prasan
Sathpath Brahman--Tale of Eastward movement of People from Saraswati to Ganga.


Nastik School of Thought:- i)Charavak ii)Jains iii)Buddhist------->Do not believe in the authority of Vedas and God.
Astik School of Thought:- i)Vaisesika ii)Nyaya iii)Samkhya iv)Yoga v)Mimamsa vi)Vedanta--> Do do
  
                             Mimamsa:- Vedas are the final authority in determinig the duties of a man. Concerned with-'Karmakand' i.e Samhita and Brahmans only.

           Sankara's ------->Vedanta:- Vedas are the final authority in gaining true knowledge about man and Universe. With 'Gyankand' i.e Upanishads only. Non-Dualism(Advaita)

                             Vaisesika:- Realistic, analytica and objective. Classification of world in 5 elements. Atomic. Dualism of Soul and Matter. Salvation-Recognising this Dualism.

                             Nyay:- Vaisesika + Abhav(Negation). 'Law of Karma' does not apply to God. Detailed study on Source of knowledge(Praman)--4 Pramans

                             Samkhya:- Oldest; 25 tattvas---Prakriti, akash, Buddhi etc. Infinite number of Souls. 3 virtues--Rajas,Tamas,Sattva.

                             Yoga:- Self-Control and Self-Mortification


ART AND ARCHITECTURE

Asshole Inscription in Karnatka= Varha Avatar of Vishnu
Deogarh Temple in UP is one the oldest Hindu temple of 5th Century. A vishnu templee

                                                                              JAINISM  and  BUDDHISM



JAINISM
  2 tirthankars are mentioned in Rig-Veda--i)Rishabhnath(1st Tirthankar) and ii)AristhaNemia
  
  Vayu-Puran and Bhagwat-Puran mentions Rishab as avatar of Narayan.
  
  23rd Tirthankar was Parsava--Son of Ikshvaku king. (250 years before Mahavira)
  
  Mahavira
    i)Born in Kundangram, near Vaisali(540 BC), He belonged to Jnatrika Clan. Left home at age of 30. Attained Kevalya(Supreme knowledge and deliverance from bonds) at 42.
  
   ii)His followers were initially called 'Nirgranths', later 	Jainas.
  
  iii)Died at Pawapuri at age of 72(468BC)
  
   iv)Doctrine 
   
         a)Non-Injury b)No property c)Truth d)No stealing e)Celibacy(added by mahavir). 
   
         b)Did not deny existence of God, but ignored him.
   
         c)World runs through cycles of Progress(Utsarpini) and Declines(Avsarpini).
   
         d)World fuctions through interactions of living souls(Jiva) and everything has soul. 
   
         e)Purpose of life is purification of soul. Free soul rises above heavens where it remains in omniscence bliss called Nirvana. 
   
    v)ChandraGupta Maurya and Bhadrabahu went to Karnataka.  He organised a council at Patliputra in which Jain canons were arranged(Later rearranged in 5th CE AD)----Digambars
   
   vi)Shulbhadra stayed and got the charge of jains. They started wearing white clothes(Considered haram by original jains)----Svetambars







BUDDHISM

BUDDHA
   
   i)Born in 566BC. In Sakya Clan. Died at the age of 80 in 486BC at Kushinagar. 
 
  ii)4 Noble truths(Arya-satyas)
 
 iii)8 fold paths--i)Right Speech ii)Right Action iii)Right Livelihood iv)Right Exertion v)Right mindfulness vi)Right Meditation vii)Right Resolution viii)Right View
 
  iv)Sila(Conduct)+Samadhi(Concentration)+Prajna(Knowledge)
 
   v)Gave 1st Sermon at Deer Park at Sarnath. Started the "wheel of Law".
 
  vi)Upasakas--Lay followers, who live with Family; Bhikus--Monks who renounced the world and lived at Sangha(Women were allowed)


Major Buddhist Sites:- Karle, Sarnath, Lumbini, Rajagriha, Kushnagar, Ramban, Kapillavstu, Shravasti, Barabar, BodhGaya, Bharhut, Sanchi, Amravati, Ajanta, Karle, Naskik, Junnar

Empty Seat= Meditation of Buddha 

Stuppa= Mahaparinibban

Wheel=First Sermon

Elephants=Strength and Wisdom(ex- Gajalakshmi)

Shalabhanjikas= Women upon who's touch flowers would blossom 

Nagarjun is responsible for Mahayan Buddhism. Contemporary of Kanishka. 'Madhyamik' school of buddhist philosophy popularly known as 'Sunyavad'.

                                                                                 DARK-AGES

Abdul Razaaq Samarkandi= Discription of Vijayanagar Empire. He was from Herat
Al-Biruni was from Uzbekistan, came in 11th CE. He wrote KITAB-UL-HIND. Translated- i) Patanjali in Arabic ii) Euclid's work in Sanskrit
Ibn Battuta was from Morocco, came in 14th CE(1333 in Sind). He wrote RIHLA. 
Frencois Bernier was a Frenchman who came in 17th CE. 

 

SUFFISM a.k.a Tasawuff


Khanqah= Hospice around which community arose.

Jamat khana= A big hall in khanqah where visitors lived and prayed

Shaikh,Wali(Plral=Auliya), Pir or Murshid= Teacher 

Murids= His disciples 

Khalifa= His successors

Silsila= Chain or Lineage. Ex- Chisti Silsila started in Chisti( Afghanistan)

Ziyarat= Pilgrimage to suffi Dargah(Shrine). 
Barkat= Grace of Allah
Karamat= Miracles that suffis performed 

Zikr= Reciting devine name of Allah
Sama= Singing/Recitation 
Masnavis= Long poems to express divine love. Ex- Padmavat by Malik Md Jassi 

Malfuzat= Conversations of Sufi saints. 
Maktubat= Letters written by Sufi saints
Tazkiras= Biographies of Sufi Saints



Auqaf= Charitable trusts setup by Sultan for Sufis
Inam = Tax-Free land given to them 

Be-Sharia--> Radicals who dicarded khanqas and performed extreme ascetism. Example= Qalandar, Madari, Malang, Haidari.

Abdul Hasan Al Huzwiri a.k.a DATA-GaNJ-BAKSH--Wrote about what tasawuff meant. His Shrine= Data Darbar in Lahore. 

Amir Khusra was a Murid of Nizamuddin Auliya. He started Qual--> Later became Qawwali .

SIKHISM

   Baba Farid's poetry was included in Guru Granth Sahib.

   Kabir got into Bhakti by his teacher Ramananda. 

   5th Guru, Arjun compiled teachings of Guru Nanak and his 4 successors, Kabir, Baba farid, Ravidas into ADI-Granth.

   10th Guru, Gobind Singh included teachings of 9th Guru Tegh bahadur to Adi Granth and created GURU-GRANTH SAHIB. Also created Khalsa panth. 

   Raidas was the teacher of MIRABAI. He was a leather worker caste fellow.

   Shankaradeva= Assamese, Vaishnav, Satra(Monestary for transmission of knowledge), Naam-Kirtan, Naam-Ghar(Prayer hall). Major composition- Kirtan-ghosh 

   Bhakti saints opposed Castism. But they did not condem oppression by Zamindari system. 


VIJAYNAGAR EMPIRE/ HAMPI
     Collin Mackenzie discovered ruins of Hampi in 1800. He was the 1st Surveyor General of India. 

     Harihara and Bukka- Founders in 1336. Rulers- RAYAS( Rulers of men ). 

     Sangam--> Sulvas--> Tulvas--> Aravidu

        Kudirai-Chettis= Horse Merchants  

        Nayaks= Military Chiefs (Amar-Nayak system based on Iqta system of Delhi Sultanate). 

        Nicolo-di-Conti- Italy; Abdul Razzak- Persia; Afanasi Nikitin- Russia (15th CE)

        Duarte Barbosa, Domingo Paes, Fernao Nuniz- Portuguese (16th CE) 

              Kamalapuram Tank was built by Krishna Dev Raya.

              Walled City, No Mortar or cementing agent used in construction. Enclosed Agriculture fields as well as forest areas. 

              Elaborate canal System drawing water from Tungabhadra.

              Indo-Islamic gateway arches. Massive raised platform- "Mahanavmi Dibba", A temple called "Lotus Mahal". Other-"Hazara-ram temple".

              Pampadevi married Virupaksha( Guardian deity of city ) at Virupakh Temple. It has pillarad halls,a surrounding tank and Raya-Gopuram(Royal Gateway).

But, VITTHAL Temple is for Vitthal( A maharashtrian Vishnu deity ) , built by Vijaynagar nayaks. It has a shrine designed as a Chariot. Pillored pavalions where merchants setup shops.

مغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہمغلیہ سلطنت
                                                      MUGHAL EMPIRE( Ming in China; Safavid in Persia; Ottoman in Turkey )

Muzarian, Raiyat, Kisan, Asami= Farmer/Peasant. (Khud-Kasht-> In his own village/ Pahi-Kasht-> In some other village).

Maize(Makka) was bought from Spain and Africa.

Tommatoes, Potatoes and Chillies as well as Fruits like Pineapple and Papaya  came from New World. 

Mandal,Muqqadams= Village Headmen--> Chosen through consensus of Village elders.
Jajmani System(In Bengal)--> Artisans like blacksmiths were paid a small daily allowence for their work.
Peshkash= Form of tribute collected by Mughals. Ex--> Peshkash required from Janglis were Elephants. 


Diwan-> Officer for supervising Fiscal Administration of state.

Jama-> Amount assessed. Hasil-> Amount actually collected.

Amil-Gulzar= Tax-Collector ( Pay in Cash or Kind ), but cash was always prefered.

Polaj= Land which is always cultivated(Never fallow). 
Parauti= Land which is left uncultivated to revive fertility.
Chachar= Left fallow for 3-4 years. 
Banjar= uncultivated for more than 5 years.

Giovani Careri(Italian)--> Gave graphic account of how Silver travelled around the world and reached India.

Persian was the court language. Babur's autobiography was written in Turkish. 

Mahabharat was translated to Persian as "Razmanama-The book of wars"

Nastaliq= Caligraphy, Muhammad Husayan of kashmir was one such Caligrapher of Nastaliq.

Bihzad was a painter at Safavid Court. Very famous.



Humayun bought 2 painters with him from Iran- Mir Sayyid Ali and Abdus Samad. 

Ramdas was Akbar's painter. 

Jahangir's Painters--> Abul Hasan 

Payag was a painter of Shah Jahan , his work is found in Badshah nama. 





Humayun Nama was written by Gulbadun Begum( Daughter of Babur and sister of Humayun). 

Abdul Hamid Lahori wrote the biography of Shah Jahan--> Badshah Nama. He was a student of Abul-Fazl who wrote Akbar-Nama. 
(Asiatic Socity of Bengal founded in 1784 by Willian jones, translated these 2 books in English).


Kornish--> A form of greeting/Salutation in Akbar's court. Jharokha darshan was started by him where he would stand in balcony. 

Chahr Taslim and Zaminbos(Kissing ground)--> Salutations in Shah Jahan's court.

Dagh--> Branding of Horses.

Waqi-Nawis= Court Writers, suppervised by Mir-Bakshi 

Asaf Khan and Mirza Raja(Aurangzeb gave to 2-Jai singh and jaswant Singh) were Senior titles offered by the king.

مغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہ سلطنتمغلیہمغلیہ سلطنت

BRITISH INDIA

Permanent Settlement came in 1793 under Cornwallis. 75% Zamindaris changed hands after PS. PS was made with the rajas and Taluqdars of Bengal. Now they became Zamindars( Revenue collector of state ). 
"Sunset Law"- Payment had to be made by the Sunset of fixed date. Rate was fixed for perpetuity. 
Zamindar's other powers( Troops, judicial duty etc) were abolished or taken up by Collector. 

Jotedars- Rich Ryots; Mandals- Village headmen; Adhiyars or Bargadars = Thekedaar 
( The FIFTH REPORT was submitted to British parliament in 1813 which gave a detailed discription of Company rule in Bengal and Tamil Nadu). 

Damin-I-Koh--> Land alloted to Santhals to cultivate in Rajmahal hills, between the plains and the Hills( where Paharis lived and practiced Jhum).
But they revolted in 1855 because of High rent, and Interest rate from Dikus(Moneylenders). So Santhal Prargana was created to pacify them.

In Deccan and Western India, Ryotwari System---> Settlement with Ryot itslef. Rent changed after every 30 years. 1st in 1820s.

    Other Facts about these Fags : 
    
      1) Ye log, lakdi ka plough, apni gaand me lete the. 
      
      2) Ye irrigation ko bhi apni gand me lete the. 
      
      3) Ye log--> Cattle, Sheep, GOat and Buffalo, ko bhi apni gand me ddaaalte the. 
      
      4) Ye log, ber khaate the, or Antelope jaise prani ka shikar karte the. 
      
      5) Dholavira ke log, special chutiye the, kyunki baaki logo se alag, ye apni city ko 2 nahi balki 3 parts me divide karte the : 
      
           i) Har part ke charo taraf massive stone walls thi, or enterances the. 
           
          ii) Settlement me open area tha, jaha public ceremonies hua karti thi---> Raja ki beti, nangi naachti thi. 
          
         iii) Bade se white stone par harappan script likhi mili---> Aisa baaki jagah nahi milta0----> baaki sab chota chota hai.
         
         
         
      6) Lotha, Sabarmati ke sant ki gaand se bahane wali pani pe bana tha. 
      
          i) Ye gulf of khambat ke close hai. 
          
         ii) yaha semi-precious stone kaafi aasani se mil jata tha, or ye unke badle me rande uthake late the, sindh ki, isliye, is jagah ko Mutthal kehte the, lekin dhere dheere, naam bas Lothal reh gaya. 
         
        iii) City me ek store house tha. ----> Isme bohot saari seals or sealings mili hai.----< Matlab, jarur ye koi workshop type kuch hoga.  
        
         iv) Yaha pe ek dockyard bhi mila hai.    
        

       7) 3900 years ago, Harappa-Mohanjodaro ki maa chudne lagi---> Trade ruk gayi,  Ghatiya ghar,  Kachra hi kachra, Nadiya sukh gayi,,    Deforestation,    Baadh bhi aa gayi,    .

      11) Lower town bhi walled tha. 
      
      12) Sun-dried or baked bricks ka use, sab ki sab sites par hua hai. Or bricks ke size bhi standardized hain. 

      
      13) Citadel me hai ye sab :--> Great bath,  Warehouse,  Wheeler and Marshall's deep trenches,  Stupa Area, Great courtyard, 
      
      
      14) Dead log ko pits me daal ke gaad dete the.   Bas kuch ke saath hi saman waman mila hai---> Overall, aisa lagta hai, ki ye log, yakeen nahi karte the precious things ko saath me leke marne me. 
      
      
      15) Faience se bane, miniature pots bas, Harappa and Mohenjodaro me mile hain---> Kaliban jaisi choti sites pe nahi.

       Vedic age
       
        
        
  
        
         1) Rig veda : 
         
               - Ise, 3500 saal purana
               
               - 1000 hyms hai, jinhe "Sukta" kehte hain. Matlab---> "well said", wah bc. 
               
               - 3 gods important hain---> Agni, Indra  and   Soma
               
               - Kuch verses ko aurat log ne bhi compose kiya hai. 
               
               - Vedic/Old sanskrit me hai, jo hamare wali se alag hai. 
               
               - Rigveda ko likha nahi, bas recite kiya jata tha. 
               
               - BC, ise, pehli baar bas 200 saal pehle hi likha gaya tha. WTF. 
               
               
               - Isme----> Vishvamitra bhand hoke, 2 nadiyo se baate karta hai---> Beas or Sutlej se ---> Kya BS hai ye. 
               
               - Kashmir, se jo manuscript mila hai, 150 saal pehle, uska use karke, modern version likha gaya tha. 
               
               - Ganga or Yamuna ko bas 1 hi baar mention kiya gaya hai--> Kat gaya. 
               
               
               - Cattle or Sons ki pray karte hain---> Based ? 
               
               - Or GHode----Ummm Ghodeeee
               
               - Jo wealth capture hoti thi, use, Leader rakh leta tha, or baaki baant deta tha--> Priests or logo me. 
               
               - Kuch wealth se Yajnas yani Sacrifices karwate the. 
               
               - Koi regular army nahi thi, Assemblies me milte the, log, or jaake lad marte the sab ke sab, chutiye saale cow-premi. 
               
               - Yahi par wo apna leader chose karte the---> ye brave or skillfull warrior hota tha---> based/
              
              
               - 2 tarah ke log bataye gaye hain--> Pehle --> i)Priests ko 'Brahmin' bolte the----> Ye rituals perform karte the. 
               
               - Dusre ii) 'Raja'--> Ye modern raja jaisa nahi tha---< Ye na tax collect karta tha, na iska mahal or foj thi. Log iske lund pe tafri maar ke bhaad jaate the, aise hi maje maje me. 
               
               - Na ye post hereditary thi. 
               
               - Community ko describe karne ke liye 2 words use kiye hain--- Jan   and   vish( Vaishya, isi word se aata hai) 
               
               - Ex : Puru Jan,  Bharat jan or vish    ,    Yadu jan or vish  etc
               
               - Khudke arya kehte the, or baaki log ko--> Dasas or Dasyus. ---> Ye log wo the, jo sacrifices nahi karte the ot different languages bolte the--. based. 
               
               - Slaves capture karte the, ladai me. 
               
               - Inhe property ki tarah rakha jata tha. ---> Jo man me aaye wo karo=----> based
               
               - Jab, north-west me Rig-veda likhi ja rahi thi, tab, Elentra log, Megaliths bana rahe the. 
               
               
               
               
                    
     Megaliths : 
     
        1) Literally : bade Bade pathhar
        
        2) Carrefully arrange kar, burial sites banai jaati thi
        
        3) Ye practice 3000 saal paehle start hui. 
        
        4) Deccan, SOuth India or North east, even Kashmir me bhi chalta tha ye sab 
        
        5) Kuch surface pe thi, kuch underground. 
        
        6) Sabke andar, Mare hue chode ke saath-----> Red and Black ware bhi burry kiya jata tha---> Kyunki ye sab Usher ke fan the, matlab inhe R and B music pasand tha. 
        
        7) Saath me Iron tools and weapons , even ghoda...haan bc...ghoda tak dafna dete the, sone chandi ke saath.  BC ghoda kon dafnata hai. 
        
        8) Brahmagiri me to ek grave mili hai, wo BC kisi seth ki lagti hai---> Sala 33 gold beads, 2 stone beads, 4 copper bangles, 1 shankh---> ye sab leke mara tha betichod.
        
        9) Kuch me lash ke saath tatti padi hoti thi----> matlab, Class difference tha, us time waha. 
        
       10) Kabhi kabhi puri family ko ek hi pit me gaad dete the---> Family burial bhi tha matlab. 
       
       11) Inamgaon(Bhim river pe)---> Yaha adult ko north me sir karke gaadte the. ---> ye site, 3600 se 2700 saal pehle thi. 
       
       12) Yaha ek choode ko, khane ke pot ke saath gada hua tha. LoL pettu sala.            
                
               
  


               Back to Vedic Society : 
               
               
                  - 3000 saal pehle tak to Raja log ko choose kiya jata tha. 
                  
                  - Baad me choda aake, bade bade sacrifice karke, bolne laga---> Main hoon raja. 
                  
                  - Ashvamedh a.k.a Horse sacrifice bhi aisi hi ritual thi. 
                  
                  - Isme, Raja ka jo saarthi hota tha, wo badi badi fenkta tha. 
                  
                  - Raja ki wife or bete bhi kuch choti moti rituals perform karti thi. 
                  
                  - Baaki, cucked rajas aake specte karte the. 
                  
                  - Preist log, khub chutiya banate the sabko, raja ke upar muth mara hua paani chidak dete the. 
                  
                  - Ordinary log, yani Vish type,bhi gift leke aate the. 
                  
                  - Lekin, Priest log, Shudra log ko nahi aane dete the. 
                  
                  - Kshatriyas or Vaishyas, dono ko allowed tha---> Sacrifices perform karne ka. 
                  
                  - Shudra or Aurat, dono ko Vedas padhna allowed nahi tha------ Based. 
                  
             
             
             
        Janapadas : 
        
         
             - Raja jo ye bade bade sacrifices karta tha----> Ab inhe, Rajas of Janapada ki tarah recognise kiya jane laga, rather than jan. 
             
             - Janapada---'land where jana set its foot and settled down'.
             
             - Archeologist log ne kai dhuund liye hain settlements---> Delhi ka Purana Qila,   Meerut ke paas Hastinapur,    Etah ke paas Atrnjikhera .
             
             - Log, jhonpdi me rehte the. Or gaaye paalte the. 
             
             - Rice, Wheat, Barley, Pulses, Sugarcane, Sesame or Mustard ugaate the. 
             
             - Ye pots banate the----> Kuch grey color ke the, kuch laal.      EK Special pot banate the ye log-----> Painted Grey Ware. ----> Inpar painted design hote the, Hot bandio ki shape ke, --> Ye kaafi fine the. 
             
        
        
        
       Mahajanpadas : 
       
        
        
        
        
           Sabse North or West wala ----> Pushkalavati in Gandhara
           
           
           Southernmost ---> Ashmika --> Maharashtra
           
           
           Easternmost---> Champa
        
        
        
        
        
        
            
               - 2500 saal pehle, kuch janapadas, baaki ki janpadao se jyada important ho gayi. 
               
               - Inhe Mahajanpadas bulane lage log---> In sabke capital city the, jyadatar jo fortified the. 
               
               - Wood or Brick ki huge deeware banai jaati thi, unke chaaro or. 
               
               - Forts--> Protection or dekho dekho mera lund kitna bada hai. 
               
               - Fortified cities ke andar rehne wale logo ko bhi aasani se control me rakha jaa sakta hai. 
               
               - Ab ye naye raja, Army rakhne lage.    Soldiers ko regular salary dete the.   Pure saal rakhte the.  
               
               - Payments ke liye, Punch-marked sikke use karte the. 
               
               - Regular taxation start kar diya inhone. 
               
                   i) Most important ---> Crops par tax---> 1/6th of jo bhi ugaya. ---> Ise 'Bhag' aka share kehte the
                   
                  ii) Craft wale log pe tax tha. Inse kaam waam bhi karwa lete the, tax ke naam pe. 
                  
                 iii) Herders se bhi animal produce ki shakal me tax wasoolte the. 
                 
                  iv) Trade hone wale goods par tax
                  
                   v) BC, yaha tak ki Hunter-gathere tak se tax wasoolta tha raja gandu
                   
              
              - IS time, Agriculture me 2 major changes hue : 
              
                 i) Iron Ploughshare use hone laga. 
                 
                ii) Paddy ka transplantation start ho gaya. 
                
                   - In dono se production kaafi jyada ho gayi. 
                   
                   - Lekin, ye kamar todu kaam tha---> Isliye, Slave log ka use hone laga ye sab ke liye----> Dasas/dasis   and Landless agri labourers--> 'kammakaras' ---> Mare kol kammakaras, trai khante ho ge ne saanu, 
                   
                             




      MAGADH : 
      
       
         - Ise bas 200 saal lage, sabse important Mahajanpad ban ne me. 
         
         - Bohot saari important river, Ganga or Son, isme se behti thi. 
         
         - Is se a) Transport  b) Water supplies   c) Fertile land   ka jugad ho gaya
         
         - FOrested land--Haathi
         
         - Iron ore mines thi region me
         
         - 2 powerful rulers the : Bimbisara and Ajatasattu  --> Inke baad Mahapadma Nanda---> Ye bhai ne to NW tak pe kabja kar liya. 
         
         - BIhar ka Rajagriha---> Inka capital tha kaafi saal. 
         
         - Baad me capital shift kar, Patliputra kar liya. 
         
         
       Some other points about these fags.   
         
        1) 6th or 4th CE BCE ke time, ye sabse important mahajanpad ban gaya tha. 
        
        2) Mauryan Empire : 
        
             - C M ne 321 BC me start kiya. 
             
             - Inke baare me, jain, buddhist or baad ke puranic literatures me bhi milta hai .
             
             - Empire ke 5 major political centre the :--> Patliputra Caital + 4 provincial centre : Ujjain ke liye, Taxi lene gaya, Tosali bolti, Suvar nikal yaha se ---->   Ujjain, Taxila, Tosali,  Suvarnagiri(karnataka)
             
             - Major Rock edicts mile hain :-->  Kandahar(kandh/deewar) Shahbazgarhi(shabash) Mansehra(Manse)  Kalsi(Kalse)  Girnar   Sopara(sapera)   Sannati(sannata)   Shishupalgarh(shsihpal kaka)   Jaugada(Jugad)  
             
                                                   
                                                    
       Sapere ko dekhne ke liye, Shishpal kaka, jugad se bani deewar pe chade , or Gir gaye, jis se waha sannata cha gaya .   Fir bolte,  Kalse daru peeni band, ye manse decide kiya h.Ye dekh ke Ashok ne bola-   Shabash
                
                
         3)  Ye Empire bas 150 saal chala.         
                  
                
        
        Vajji : 
        
        
             - Magadh ke time, ye log---> sangha bana ke baithe the. 
             
             - Isme, ek nahi balki, gayi rulers hote the. 
             
             - Har kisi ko raja bulate the. 
             
             - Ye saare rajas, rituals bhi saath me karte the, even ek dusre ki wife ko sab sabha me milke chusa karte the. 
             
             - Lekin, Women, Dasas or Kammakaras ko assemblies me nahi aane dete the. 
             
             
             - Buddha or Mahavira, dono Sangh/gana ke memeber hi the. 
             
             - Digha Nikaya(2300 saal purani), naam ki famous buddhist book me, Ajatasattu or Vajji ke baare me likha hai -----> Alexander ne isi time ke aaspass, invasion kiya tha. 
             
                 i) Ajatasattu ne apne bande, Vassakara ko Buddha ke pass bheja, advice lene ke liye. 
                 
                ii) Buddha ne bola, jab tak vajji ke log : Public assemblies karte rahe,  Met and acted together,  rules follow karte rahe,  Bado ko sunte rahe,   Chaitya maintain karte rahe,  Tab tak vajji prosper karta rahega. 
                
             
             
             - Ye sangh se bohot saare log, lode lete rahe. Finally, Gupta Makichu logo ne inpar kabja kar liya, 1500 saal pehle. 
             
             
             
             
             
             
        Buddha : 
        
          1) 2500 saal pehle janam hua bhai ka. 
          
          2) Sakya gana, was a kshatriya. 
          
          3) Gave first lecture at---> Sarnath, jo varanasi ke paas hai. 
          
          4) desire--- tanha---> iski wajah se ham kabhi khush nahi hote. 
          
          5) Bhai, prakrit bolta tha. 
          
         
         
         
         
         Upanishads : 
         
         
            - Same time, jab buddha gyan chod raha tha, tab baaki log bhi--- > Kon hu main, kya hu main.type baate kar rahe the. 
            
            - Inhe lagta tha, duniya me kuch aisa bhi hai, jo marne ke baad bhi rehti hai---> atman a.k.a individual soul. 
            
            - ultimately dono atman or brahman--> 1 hi hain. 
            
            - In sab ideas ko Upanishads me record kiya gaya hai. 
            
            - Ye Later Vedic texts ka part hain. 
            
            - inka literally matlab hota hai--Pas aake baithna----> ye conversations hain, teachers or students ke beech.   Idead ko simple dialogues ki tarah present kiya jata hai. 
            
            - Ex :--> Wo wise begger wali kahani in, Chhandogya Upanishad. ---> Ek beggar ne, Shaunaka or Abhipratarin naam ke sages ka chutiya kaise kata, badi badi bate kar ke. 
            
            - Jyadatar, upanishad thinkers--> mard the---> brahmins and rajas. 
            
            - Lekin, Ek hot bandi thi--> Gargi naam ki---> Ye royal courts me debates jeet leti thi, apne 34 DDs ke dam pe. 
            
            - Baad me, Shankracharya naam ke bande, ye Upanishads ke ideas ko further develop kiya. 
                 
             
             
             
             
    JAINS : 
    
    
       1) Vardhaman Mahavir--> same time, 2500 saal pehle
       
       2) Vajji sangh ke Lichchhavi group ka kshatriya prince tha. 
       
       3) 30 saal ka hone par, ghar chor, jungle me chala gaya. 
       
       4) 12 saal akela raha. 42 ka hone par, chudai ka man hua, to wapas aa gaya. 
       
       5) MArd ya admi, sach jaan na hai--- to ghar chor do. ---> Ahimsa ka rule follow karo-----> Har janwar jinda rahna chahta hai-----Or ye sab PRakrit me bola, Buddha ki tarah. 
       
       6) Magadh me jo prakrit bolte the---> Magadhi kahalati thi. 
       
       7) Is bhai ne logo ko convince kar liya, nange bhikari ki tarah jeene ke liye. 
       
       8) Mainly baniyo ne support kiya, kyunki kisan log ko to insect marne padte the. 
       
       9) Kai centuries tak, Mahavir ki teachings ko orally transmit karte rahe. 
       
      10) Lekin, 1500 saal pehle, Gujrat ke Valabhi naam ke shahar me, Muth marte marte kisi ko khayal aaya, ki bC valabhi hot hai, or ye bhi ki, Jain teachings ko compile kar lete hain. 
      
      
      
      
      SANGHA : 
      
        1) Dono mahavir or Buddha, logo ko ghar se bhaga ke le gaye, apne saath SANGHA me.. 
        
        2) Vinaya Pitaka me Buddhist sangh ke rules likhe gaye
        
        3) Men or Women ki seperate branches thi. 
        
        4) Har aadmi Sangha join kar sakta tha. 
        
        5) Children or Slaves bhi, lekin pehle permision slip pe sign karwake lani padti thi. 
        
        6) Aurat log ko bhi apne husband ki permision leni hoti thi----> Based
        
        7) Ye saara din meditation karte the, fir shaam ko shahar jaake bheek mangte the. 
        
        8) Isliye, inhe bhukkhus kehte the. 
        
        9) Ye meetings karte the--> Sangha ki, to settle any quarrels. 
        
       10) Rainy season ke alawa, ye saara time, idhar se udhar travel karke, logo ko teachings dete the. 
       
       11) Gufa wagarah me so jaate the jake. 
       
       12) Baad me permanent shelter ban gaye inke liye---> Viharas. 
       
       13) Sabse purane vihar lakdi ke the.   kuch ko caves me banaya tha. 
       
       
       
       
       
       ASHRAMS : 
       
       
          - Jains or Buddh logo se jalan ke maare, Brahmin chodo ne ashram bana liye. 
          
          - Lekin, ye koi jagah nahi---> balki stage of life thi. 
          
          - 4 ashrams ---> brahmacharya,  grihasta,   vanaprastha   and    samnyasa
          
          - Brahmacharya ke time, simple life jiyo or Veda padho
          
          - Baad me chudai karo---> Grihasta
          
          - Baad me jungle me jake raho or meditate karo---> Vanaprashta
          
          - Baad me sab kuch tyag do---> Samnyasi ban jao. 
          
          - Aurat log ko to ved padhne hi nahi diye jaate the---> based.

             i) Narmada Cross karte time, Chalukya king, pulakeshin II ne lullakesin chakha diya

           Admininstration of these kingdoms : 
           
             1) Pehle ki tarah, Village hi basic unit of admin tha. 
             
             2) Lekin, ab kings, ko naye logo ki jarurat hi, jo loyal ho---> Isliy, isne bohot saare posts hereditary kar diye 
             
                - Ex : Poet Greenarmy--> Ye maha-danda-nayaka or chief judicial officer tha, apne baap ki tarah
                
                - Kai baar, ek hi banda kai saare offices sambhalta tha : Green army, judiciary ke saath saath, Kumar-amatya--- yani important minister bhi tha, or sala Sandhi-vigrahika, yani War/peace ka minister bhi yahi tha
                
                - Important log, local admin me bhi lode lete the--> Jaise, Nagar-shresti---> City ka chief banker/ merchant.    or   prathama-kulika---> Chief craftsman    or    head of kayasthas or Scribes
                
             3) ye sab thode time ke liye to effective tha, lekin baad me yahi log, rulers ki gand lene lage. 
             
             
             4) Army ke liye, King, Samanta naam ke bando pe dependent hone laga---> Inhe salary nahi balki, grants ya lands di jaati thi---> Ye us land se revenue collect kar lete the, or badle me log supply karte the ladai ke liye
             
                 - Jaise, hi ruler weak hota, Samanta loda dikha dete

         Purans wagarh sab, Guptas ke time likhi gayi thi :
         
            - Inme bhagwan wagarh ki kahaniya, or baaki sab philosophical chutiyaap bhi hai.    Saath saath purane raja maharajao ka bhi mentioned hai. 
            
            - Purans or Mahabharat dono ko Vyas ne likha tha. 
            
            - Purans ko Shudra or Aurat log ko bhi allowed tha sun na. 
            
            - Pandit banda, mandir me padha karta tha puran. 
            
            
            
       Yahi time tha, jab Aryabhatta ne Aryabhattiya likhi : 
       
       
            i) Day or night hoti hai, kyunki earth apni axis pe rotate karti hai. 
            
           ii) Eclipse kyu hote hain, ye bataya
           
          iii) Circle ki circumference kaise calclulate karte hain, ye bataya
          
       
       
       
     
       700 - 1750 ke beech ke kaand  :

            1) Persian wheel aa gaya tha---> irrigation ke liye

            3) COmbat me firearms use hone lage the
            
            4) Naye food and beverages aa gaye the :---> Aalu,  Corn,  Chillies,  Chai and coffee. 
            
            5) Forest clear hue, or agriculture expand hua. 
     
     
       
       
       
     7th CE, tak samantas bade hote gaye---> ye khudko Maha-samanta  or even maha-mandaleshavar (great lord of a region) kehne lage. 
     
       - Baad me rulers ko hi loda dikhane lage. 
       
       - Ex : RASHTRAKUTAS : 
       
                 1) Ye saale, ek time pe, Chalukyas ke subordinates the, in kArnataka 
                 
                 2) Mid 8th CE me, Dantidurga ne, apne chalukya raja ki gand me danti de maari---> Fir bhai ne--> Hiranya-Garbha(Golden-womb) naam ki ritaul perform ki----> Is se wo Kshatriya ban gaya, even though ye koi chuda bh
                 
       
       - Kabhi kabhi, ameer log, apni military skills ka use kar, khudke liye, kingdom bana lete the . 
       
          Ex :-- Kadamba Mayursharma    or   Gurjara Pratihar Harichandra---> Ye saale, baaman the--->   Karnataka or rajashtan me kingdom bana liye, talwar uthake. 
          
          
          
        - Ye saale khudko--> Maharaja-adhiraj,   Tribhuvan-chakravartin  etc jaise bade bade title free me hi de diya karte the. 
        
        - Lekin, inhe bhi Samantas ka lemon chusna padta tha.     
       
       
       
       
       
       
     Vetti :---> Tamil Nadu me, CHola log, ye tax lagate the---> Or ye koi cash wash nahi lete the---> BC, forced labour karwate the
     
     Kadamai :--> Ye chola log, Land-revenue ko kehte the.

    PRITHVIRAJ III 
    
    
       - Ye bhai, ek chahaman Ruler tha, jisne Muhammad Ghori ko 1191 me haraya, lekin agle saal haar gaya.

       IMPERIAL CHOLA : 
       
          1) Kaveri Delta par, Muttartiyar naam ki famila ka kabja tha. Ye saale bhi , pehle Pallav kings ke subordinate the. Kanchipuram wale
          
          2) Purane, chola, under VijayYala, bolote YOLO or , Muttaraiyar ka mut nikal diya. 9th CE ke middle me. 
          
          3) FIr bhai, ne Thanjavur me Nishambhsudhini mata ka mandir banwaya. 
          
          4) Vijayyala ke beta log ne, fir, pandayan or Pallav territories par kabja kar liya. 
          
          5) Most Powerful chola bhai the :-- RajaRaja I --> Ye 985 me raja bane. 
          
                                                   - Admin ko reorganise kiya, empire ke
                                                   
                                                   - Iske bete Rajendra I ne to Ganga Valley, or Sri-lanka, choro, bhai ne south-east asia tak pe kabja kar liya. 
                                                   
                                                   
          6) Inhone jo bade bade mandir banwaye, uske aaju baaju hi settlements develop ho gayi.   Yaha mast bronze wale bhi the, ---> Chola bronze image, duniya me best. 
          
          7) Ye sab isliye ho paya, kyunki, 5th-6th CE me, Tamil Nadu ke area me large scale irrigation start ho gaya tha.       
          
         
       
       
       Chola Admin : 
       
         - UR---> Ye peasants ki settlements hua karti thi. 
         
         - Irrigation ki wajah se ye kaafi prosporous ho gayi thi. 
         
         - Ur + Ur + Ur..... = Nadu
         
         - Nadu ki village councils---> Justice dispense karti thi, or tax collect karti thi. 
         
         - Nadu ke upar, Vellela chaoudhary log ka kabja tha---> Lekin, all under the supervision of Central Chola Government. 
         
         - Chola king, ameer landowners ko bade bade titles deta tha---> Jaise Muvendavelam---> Ek velan/peasant jo 3 rajao ko serve karta hai. ,    Ariyar---> Chief 
         
         - Chola inscriptions se kai tarah ki lands ke baare me pata chalta hai : 
         
            1) Vellanvagai----> Non-brahmin Choudhary peasant propeietors
            
            
            2) Brahmadeya----> Land jo, brahmins ko gift ki gayi ho
            
            
            3) Shalaboga------> School chalane ke liye, di gayi jameen
            
           
            4) Devadana, Triunamattukkani ---> Temples ko jo land gift ki jaati thi   -----> Tero naam tukkani,   Tu mere temple ke lund ki rani
            
           
            5) Pallichchhandam ---> Jain, institutions ko jo land donate ki jaati thi. -----> Palli chandam , Palli chandam,   Santhara leloo naaa,   Palli Chandam Palli chandam, Santahara leloooooo
            
            
            
            
         
         - Kaveri valley me bohot saare, Brahmadey land, par kai saari brahman log ki settlements thi. 
         
         - Inhe mangae karne ke liye, Sabha or assembly hua karti thi---> Or prominent landholders. 
         
         - Temples ki stone walls par inke decisions ko inscribe kiya jata tha. 
         
         
         
         
         - Baniyo ki associations, jinhe Nagarm kehte the----> Ye bhi kabhi kabhi admin. functions perform kar liya karti thi, town me. 
         
         
         
         
         
       Periya-puranam ---> Ye ek 12th CE, tamil work hai---> Jisme ordinary man ki life ke baare me details hai. 
       
          - Isme bataya hai : 
          
               i) Pulaiyas :--->   Neech jaat

      Minhaj-i Siraj ---> Ye book hai, jisme likha hai, ki Raziyya Sultan ke boobs kaafi gore the, or jab wo ghode pe chalti thi, to log, ka wahi nikal jata tha, or haan wo apne bhaiyo ka lund bhi khada kar deti thi. 
      
      
                           - Ye randi, 1236 me, Sultan Iltutmish, yani apne baap ka lund kaat ke sultan ban gayi thi. 
                           
                           - Ise, fir 1240 me hata diya gaya, jab ye ek bande se, ghode ka lund lete hue pakdi gayi thi. 
       
       
         Ye darpok, apne sikko pe, apne aap ko Iltutmish ki beti batati thi. 
         
         
              - Iske ulat, Rani Rudramadevi(1262 - 1289)---> Warangal ke Kakatiya dyansty ki----< Khudko aadmi batati thi
         
         
              - Kashmir ki rani Didda(980 - 1003) ---> Ye khudko didi bulati thi. 
         
       
       
          
       Delhi Sultanate ka expansion bas 3 hi logo ke under hua : 
       
          1) Balban   2) Alauddin Khilji   3) Muhammad Tughluq -----> Paisa kamane wale
          
          
          
       
       
       
         Qutub Minar par 3 hi sultans ne kaam karwaya hai : 
         
           1) Aybak   2) Iltutmish  3) Firuz Shah Tughluq  ----> Paisa udane wale
       
       
       
       
       
       Ye log slaves khareed te the :--> Bandagans   --> Inse lund chuswata tha sultan. 
       
          - Inhe fir ye governor wagarh bhi laga diya karte the. 
       
       
          - Persian tawarikh ke log bhi, Sultans ko criticize karte the is cheez ke liye. Wo nahi chahte the, ki sultan la lund, unke alawa koi or chuse. 
          
          
            - Ziauddin Barani ne, Muhammad Tughlaq ko khub gaaliya baki hai, jab use, wine distiller or 1 cook or 2 gardners ko admin. post de di. 
            
            
            
       
       
       - Purane rulers ki tarah, ye bhi, Military commanders ko Territories ka governor bana dete the----> In lands ko-> Iqtas bulaya jata tha. 
       
            - Inke holders ko--> Iqtadars
            
            - or Muqti bhi kehte the. 
            
            - Ye wo same Samantas wala randaap tha. 
            
            - Ye lekin, tabhi effective hota jab koi hereditary chutiyaap start na hota, or aisa karne ke liye 10-15 saal se jyada kisi ko ek iqte par tikna allow nahi karna chahiye tha. 
            
                - Aisa karne wale the :-->  Alauddin Khalki or Muhammad Tughlaq
                
                - Lekin, inhe kaafi oppose kiya jata tha. 
                
                - Lekin, Sultan fir bhi accountants appoint kar deta tha---> Muqtis jo amount collect kar rahe hain, wo check karne ke liye. 
                
             - Khalji ke under, State ne--> Assessment and collection of land revenue ko apne under le liya.  Or local samanta logo ko bola, ki koi khudka tax lagate hue dikh gaya to uske, aand kaat diye jayenge. 
             
             - Carefully land measure karte, or account rakha karte. 
             
             - 3 tarah ke taxes the : 
             
                 i) Khraaj ---> Produce ka aadha, BC 50%.
                 
                ii) Cattle pe
                
               iii) Ghar pe
            
       
       
       
     - Ibn-Battuta, jo morocco se aaya tha, or us Dilbar wali randi ka chacha lagta hai----> 14th CE me---->> Batata hai, ki kaise, local samanta log, bamboo ke piche chip jaya karte the, fir sultan ke jate hi, use lund dikha diya karte the.         
       
       
       
         
         - Jab, mOngols se ladne ke liye badi army chahiye the, Tab Allaudin ne tax 50% kar diya, or soldiers ko iqta ki jagah cash dene laga---> Or jab use laga, ki ye sunkar, baniye price badha denge to usne, price fix kar diye. 
         
         
       
       - Baad me, Sher Shah Suri ne, Alauddin ke admin. ki copy kar, khudka admin banaya----> From 1540 to 1555.

     MUGHAL DYNASTY : 
     
     
        1) BABUR (1526 -1530) 
        
           - Ferghana ka ruler bana, 1494 me, jab bas 12 saal ka tha. 
           
           - Uzbegs ne gand pe laat mar ke bhaga diya
           
           - Kabul pe kabja kiya in 1504. 
           
           - 1525 me, Ibrahim Lodi ka loda kaat diya, Panipat me, or Delhi / Agra par kabja kar liya. 
           
           - 1527 me Rana Sanga ko haraya, at Khanua
           
             
        2) Humayun 1530 - 1540,  1555 - 1556
        
             - Sher khan se gand marwa lim at Chausa, in 1539 or fir agle saal at kanauj. 
             
             - Iran bhag gaya. 
             
             - Safavid Shah ki madad se, 1555 me dobara Delhi pe kabja kar liya
             
             - Agle saal, library me seedhiyo se girke mar gaya
             
             
        3) Akbar  1556 - 1605
        
        
             -  13 saal ka tha, bhai tabhi emperor bana diay. 
             
             - Suris or baki afghans ko lode diye,   Malwa or Golconda ko koda kiya,   Mirza-Hakim ka revolt suppress kiya
             
             - 1568 me Chittor pe kabja kiya
             
             - 1569 me Ranthambor pe
             
             - Safavids se Qandahar cheen liya. 
             
             - Kashmir or Kabul cheen liye
             
             - Deccan or Berar or Ahmadnagar pe kabja
             
             - Last year me chodu bete, Jahangir ne rebellion kar diya. 
             
             
                 
       
         4) Jahangir (1605 - 1627)
         
             - Sikh, Ahoms or Ahmadnagar se lode liye
             
             - Shah jahan ne rebell kar diya.        
       
       
       
        5) Shah Jahan (1627 - 1658 )
        
            - Deccan me continue kiya
            
            - Bundela ko pela
            
            - Uzbegs se balkh lene ki koshish naakam
            
            - Safavids ne qandahar wapas cheen liya
            
            - 1632 me Ahmadnagar par finally kabja
            
            - Last saalo me, iske bete Aurangzeb ne 3 bhaio ka sir utar diya. 
            
            
        6) Aurangzeb (1658 - 1707)
        
             - Ahoms ko haraya, in 1663. 
             
             - Sikhs or Yusufshahi se lode liye
             
             - Rajputs ki internal politics me lund fasaya
             
             - Shivaji se lode liye
             
             - Prince Akbar ne rebell kiya
             
             - 1685 me Bijapur ko annex kiya
             
             - 1687 me Golconda ko
             
             - MArathas ne Gurrilla warfare start kar diya
             
             - Nort me Jat, Sikh or Satnami revolts. 
             
             - Marne ke baad , bete lad pade aapas me.            
       
       
       
       
       
       
       
       Mansabdari System : 
       
         1) Mansabdari===> Term refers to an individual who holds a mansab--> Meaning a position or rank
         
         2) Ye ek grading system tha, jis se fix karte the :---> Rank,  Salary  or  Military Responsibility
         
         3) Military ke liye, ek specific number of sawar ya cavalary wale rakhne padte the. 
         
         4) Horses ki branding hoti thi, or unhe rupey milte the, as salary. 
         
         5) Jagirs ki form me salaries milti thi-----> Wahi jaise iqtas thi. 
         
         6) Lekin, Muqtis/walis ki tarah, mansabdar, jagirs me rehte nahi the. 
         
         7) Ye apne nokar log, bhejke bas waha se revenue collect karte the. 
       
       
       
       
       
       Akbar ke time pe, Jagirs ko carefully assess kiya jata tha, taaki waha se hone wali revenue laghbhag utni hi ho, jitni mansabdar ki salary hai. 
       
       Aurangzeb ke time sab system chud gaya tha. 
       
       
       
       
         Akbar ke revenure Minister, Todar Mal ne----> Crop yields, prices and Area under cultivation ka survey kiya tha, for a 10-year period. 1570 - 1580. 
         
          - Is basis pe, Tax fix kar diya gaya tha, har crop ke liye, in Cash. 
          
          - Har province ko revenue circles me divide kiya gaya tha---> har kisa ka alag rate tha. 
          
          - Is system ko 'Zabt' kehte the. 
          
          - Ye bas unhi areas me ho sakta tha, jaha ache se survey kiya gaya ho---> matlab gujrat or bengal me nahi hua ye sab. 
          
          -  Mughal log, peasants se nahi, balki zamindars se collect kiya karte the. 
          
          - Ye zamindar, kabhi kabhi, apne jaat wale, peasants ke saath milke, revolt wagarh kar dete the, agar inhe lagta ki tax kafi jyada wasoola ja raha hai, to. 
          
          
          
          
      AKBAR NAMA : 
      
      
          1) Abul Fazl ne likhi
          
          2) 1st volume ---> Muh Ancestors
          
             2nd volume ---> Akbar ke reign wale events
             
             3rd volume----> Ain-i-akbari ---> Akbar ka admin, household, army, revenues, geography, tradition , culture, crops, yields, prices, wages, revenues, loda, lahsoon. 
             
             
          3) Empire ko provinces me divide kiya hua tha---> Jinhe Subas kehte the. ----> Inhe subadar govern karta tha. ----> Iske pass, dono military or political functions the. 
          
          4) Har province ka ek financiacl officer hua karta tha, jise Diwan kehte the. 
          
          5) Peace and order ke liye, Subadar ki madad ke liye. or bhi officers the :
          
              i) baskhi -----> Military paymaster
              
             ii)  sadr ------> Min. in charge of religious and charitable patronage
             
            iii) faujdar ----> Military commanders
            
             iv) kotwal -----> Town police commander
             
             
                
          6) Akbar---> Ibadar khana me baaki religious logo se discussion karta tha : Yahi se hi sulh-i-kul ka idea develop hua :
           
                         i) System of ethics pe focus karo ---> Honesty,  Justice  and peace --. Jo universally applicabel hain, 
                         
                        ii) Abul Fazl ne is idea ke around ek governance ka vision frame karne me madad ki
                        
                       iii) Is principle ko Jahangir or Shahjahn ne bhi follow kiya tha. 
                       
                       
         
         
         
         - 17th CE ke end tak, provinces waale chode, inte takatwar ho gaye the, ki Mughal authority to bas naam ki hi bachi thi. 
         
      
      
      
      
      
      2 vertical columns par ek horizontal beam tika ke, Roofs, doors or windows banate hain----> Is style of architecture ko-----> "Trabeate" ya "corbelled" style bolte hain. 
      
       - 8th or 13th CE ke beech, is style ka use hota tha----> Temples, mosque, tombs or bolis se attached buildings me. 
       
       
       
       
       
       - Iltutmish ne---> Delhi ke just bahar ek resrvoir banaya tha----> Hauz-i-Sultani   a.k.a King's reservoir. 
                      
                      
                      
      - Mughal Architecture me jo central, Towerin Dome hota hai----> Ise, Pishtaq kehte hai -----> Ye Timur ke time se chal raha hai---> Lekin India me sabe pehle---> Humayun's Tomb me visisble tha. 
      
                      
          
       'Hasht-bihisht' ----> "Eight paradise"---> Central Hall, surrounded by 8 rooms. 
       
         - Is building ko, red sandstone, se banaya gaya tha, and edged with white marble. 
         
         
         
         
         
      - Shah Jahan ke time, ye saare elemenets ek hi bade se construction project me use hone lage. 
      
      - Isne Agra or Delhi me bade bade projects start karwaye. 
      
      - Ceremonial halls for public and private audience ---> Diwan-i khas and or am
      
      - In courts ko---> Chihil sutun kehte hain--->   40-pillarad halls---> Place within a large courtyard. 
      
      - Shah jahan ke audience hall ko specially mosque jaisa design diya gaya tha, or jo uska throne tha, wo qibla ki direction me tha. 
      - Is se ye suggest hota hai ki ye saale khudko, god ka representative samajhthe the.    
         
         
       - Red fort me---> Greek god, orpheus ko dikhaya gaya hai, music bajate hue---> Pietra dura se. 
       -  
         
         
         Mughal log, regional styles Jaise Bangla Dome, deccan wale, or even Gujrat and Malwa ke styles ko adopt kar lete the.

          1) Capital of Cholas
          
          2) Parennial kaveri flow through

          5) Places with mandaps the---> Jinme se king, orders diya karta tha, apne subordinates ko.
          
          6) Thanjavur ke Saliya weavers
          
          7) Uraiyur ke log, flags banate the temple ke liye
          
          8) Kuch door, svamimalai me----> sthapathis a.k.a sculpturs ----> exquisite bronze idols bana rahe hain.

         11) Tiruvannamalai ---> Tamil Nadu  
         
         
         
         
         
         
         
         
         Mandapika :---> Mandi ko hi bolte the.

         South India ki 2 sabse famous gilds thi :---> Manigramam and Nandesi.    [ money-gram  tu mandesi ke kon desi ]
         
           - Ye India me to trade karte the hi, saath hi saath, Southeast Asia or China se bhi trade karte the. 
           
           
           
         
         
         
         
      Bidar ke craftsmen, itne famous the apne, copper or silver ke inlay work ke liye, ki ise Bidri kaha jane laga.
