      HAMPI : 
      
      
         1) Krishna-Tungabhadra basin par located hai---> Vijaynagar empire ka nucleus tha-----> Ise, 1336 me found kiya gaya tha. 
         
         2) Ye well-fortified city thi. 
         
         3) Ise banane me koi mortar, ya cementing agent ka use nahi kiya gaya tha 
         
         4) Interlocking se, wedge kar dete the  aapas me deewaro ko. 
         
         5) Ek portuguese traveller, Domingo Paes ne Hampi ko describe kiya hai : 
         
              i) Walls and towers se fortified
              
             ii) Bohot hi strong walls
             
            iii) Sundar buildings, jinke flat roofs hain. 
            
         6) Roayl complex ki buildings me, kaafi splendid arches the, domes or pillard halls bhi the, jime sculptures bane hote the. 
         
         7) Well-planned orchards or pleasure gardens bhi banaye hue the, jinpar, lotus jaise motifs bane hote the. 
         
         8) Iska Hayday tha 15th-16th CE ke beech---> Commercial or Cultural activities se chamakta tha shahar. ---> Moors, Chettis, European traders
         
         9) Temple cultural activities ka hub tha, jaha devdasis nacha karti thi. 
         
        10) Virupaksha temple me raja or public ke saamen
        
        11) Sabse important festival tha---> Mahanavmi festival, jise South me Navaratri kehte hain---> Hampi me celbrate hota tha. 
        
        12) Mahanavmi platform par---> Kings, guests se milta tha---> Subordinate chiefs se tribute accept karta tha---> Wrsetling bouts, dance, music wagarh hote the. 
        
        13) 1565 me , sab mulla deccan sultans---> Golconda, Bijapur , ahmadnagar, Berar or Bidar  ne milke, hampi jala daala.   
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
      1668 me English ne apna Headquarter, Surat se shift kar, Mumbai kar liya, jis se Surat ki maa chud gayi.    Is se pehle, ye Mughal Empire ke time, sabse important cities me se ek tha. 
      
      
      
      Masulipatnam : 
      
        1) Kaveri river ke delta pe tha
        
        2) English or Dutch, aapas me kutto ki tarah ladte the, ispar control karne ke liye
        
        3) yaha pe, Dutch logo ne fort banaya. 
        
        4) Ye Golconda ka chief port tha.   Telugu komati chettis,  Persian Merchants,  Golconda nobles,  European traders ---> Sab lode lene aate the. 
        
        5) Golconda ke Qutb Shahi Rulers---> Royal monopolies impose kar dete the--> Textiles, Spices or baaki items par, Taaki trading, Gore East India companies ke under na chali jaye puri tarah se. 
        
        6) 1686 me Aurangzeb ne Golconda annex kar liya. 
      
        7) Btitish ne fir apna, Cnentra Calutta or Madras me shift kar liya, to Masulipatnam ki bhi maa chud gayi. 
      
      
      
      
         GONDS : 
         
            - Ye Gondwan me rehte the--> vast forested region tha. 
            
            - Shifting cultivation practice karte the. 
            
            - Clans me bati hui thi tribes, Har clan ka apne ek Raja/rai hota tha. 
            
            - Jab Delhi Sultanate ki power decline ho rahi thi, tab, Gond kingdoms bhi ban ne lage. 
            
            - Akbar nama me --> Garha Katanga ke Gond kingdom ke baare me likha hai---> Jiske under, 70,000 gaanv the. 
            
                 - Centralised admin tha
                 
                 - Kingdom ko 'garhs' me divide kiya hua tha. 
                 
                 - Har Garh ko ek particular Gond clan, controll karti thi. 
                 
                 - In units ko further subdivide kiya hua tha, 84 villages me--- Chaurasi
                 
                 - Or BC, Chaurasis ko fir, subdivide kiya hua tha---> 12 villages each wali, barhots me. 
                 
                 
             - Pehle ye equal society hua karti thi, fir bade kingdoms emerge hone ke baad yaha bhi unequal social classes ban gayi. 
             
             - Gond raja khud ko rajput samjhne lage 
             
             - Aman Das---> Sangram Shah ban gaya. 
             
             - Iske bete ne, Ssalbahan ke chandel rajput raja ki beti, Durgawati ki chut mari. 
             
             - Lekin, Durgwai ne Dalpat, ka lund pat diya, suhagrat pe hi, or khud rani ban gayi. 
             
             - Lekin, tab tak Dalpat apna kaam kar chuka tha, or rane ke Bir Narain naam ka beta, pet me daal chuka tha. 
             
             - 1565 me, Mulla Mughal Forces under, Asaf Khan tatuulun, ke under Rani ki chut lene.
             
             - Dono maa bete mar gaye ladte ladte. 
             
             - Mullo ne adhe pe kabja kar liya, or baaki ki Bir Narain ke chacha, Chandra Shah ko de diya. 
             
             
             
             
        AHOMS : 
        
          1) Ye myanmar se aaye, the 13t CE me. 
          
          2) Inhone, yaha ke locak landlords, bhuiyans ko apna lemon taste karwaya, or ruler ban gaye. 
          
          3) 1523 me Chhutiuas or 1581 me koch-hajo ka kingdom annex kiya
          
          4) Ye bhai log, 1530s me hi Firearms use karne lage the. 
          
          5) 1660s tak ye khudke, Gunpowder or cannons banane lage the--- based. 
          
          6) 1662 me, Mughlas ka mulla Mir Jumla attack karne aa gaya, Ahoms haar gaye. 
          
          7) Lekin, direct mughal rule establish nahi hua. 
          
          8) Ahom state forced labour pe based thi---> 'Paiks' kehte the inhe.    Har gaanv ka census karte the, or sabko 'paik' bhejne hote the, rotation se. 
          
          9) 17th CE tak, admin kaafi centralised ho gaya tha. 
          
         10) War ke time, sab adult male lada karte the. 
         
         11) Ahoms ne , rice cultivation ka ek naya method introduce kiya tha
         
         12) Ahom society--> Divided thi, khels or clans me. -----> Artisans ki bohot thodi si hi castes hua karti thi. 
         
         13) khel, kai saare villages ko control karta tha. 
         
         14) Peasant ko land, village community diya karti thi----> Sala , even king bhi, community ki marji ke bina, land nahi cheen sakta tha. 
         
         15) 17th CE ke 1st half se hi, Brahman influence badhne laga tha. 
         
         16) Sib Singh (1714-1744) ke under, Hinduism prominent religion ban gaya tha. 
         
         17) Lekin, inhone apne traditional beliefs kabhi give up nahi kiya. 
         
         18) 'Buranjis'----> Ye historical works the, jinhe pehle  Ahom language me likha gaya tha, fir Assamese me. 
                   
       
      
      
      
      
      Nayanars and Alvars : 
      
      
        1) 63 nayanars the----> Different castes ke the. 
        
             - Most famous :-->   Appar, Sambandar,  Sundarar  and Manikkavasagar
             
             - Inhone 2 sets of songs complile kiye :---> Tevaram   and   Tiruvacakam 
             
             
         2) 12 Alvars the ----> Ye sab bhi diff background ke the
         
             
               - Most famous :--> Periyalvar, or uski beti Andal,    Tondaradippodi Alvar     and    Nammalvar
               
               - Songs--> Divya Prabhandham
               
               
               
               
         BHAKTI HI SHAKTI HAI : 
         
         
           1) SHANKARA : 
            
                - India ke sabse influential philosophers me se ek-
                
                - Aap kerala me paida hua
                
                - Aapne Advaita ko advocate kiya----> Doctrine of oneness of individual soul and Supreme God
                
                - Brahman -- Ultimate reality-----> Ye formless hai and without attributes. 
                
                - Hamare around jo world hai, wo illusion hai, a.k.a maya
                
                - Renunciation ko preach kiya, or knowledge ke path ko adopt karne ka
                
           
           
          2) RAMANUJAN : 
          
               - Tamil Nadu me janam hua, 11th CE me. 
               
               - Aap, ALvar logo ke palle pad gaye the. 
               
               - Aapke hisab se, Salvation ka best method, Vishnu ki intense devotion hi hai. 
                     (Knowledge gayi maa chudane, vishnu ka naam japo)
               
               - Is se, devotee jaake, Vishu se mil jata hai. 
               
               - Aapne, 'Vishist-advaita' naamm ki alag se feki, jise qualified oneness kaha gaya hai. 
               
               - Matlab, Soul saali, jab, Supreme God se mil bhi jaati hai, to bhi wo distinct rehti hai. 
               
               - Is bhai ki wajah se, Bhakti ki ek nai chingari spark hui ---> Yahi aage North India me develop hui. 
               
               
           
          3) BASAVANNA ka VIRSHAIVISM : 
          
          
                 - Basavanna, or iske mitra log, Allama Prabhu   and   Akkamahadevi, type log ne start kiya
                 
                 - Karnataka, in mid-12th CE
                 
                 - Equality of human beings and against, idea of caste and treatment of women. 
                 
                 - Also, Ritual worship is a sucks. 
                 
          
          
          
          
          4) Maharashtra ke Saints : 
          
          
               - 13th se 17th CE ke beech---> Saint-poets se bhar gaya, maharashtra
               
               - Simple Marathi me gaane
               
               - Most important----> Janeshwar,   Namdev,   Eknath  and   Tukaram
               
               - Women bhi jaise----> Succki succki, Sukkubai    or    Chokhamela ki family---> Ye Komal Mahar ki family ke hai
               
               - Inka focus tha----> Vitthal par--- Vishnu ki ek form. ---> Pandhrapur me ek vishnu mandir tha, iske around. 
               
               - Inhone, bhi, sab forms of ritualism ko reject kiya, or Birth pe based social difference ko. 
               
               - In saalo ne to renunciation ke idea ko hi reject kar diya, or chudai ko theek mana
               
               - Inke hisabs se, Bhakti to thi---> Dusre ke dukh kam karna.
               
               - Ex : Famous Gujju Saint Narsi Mehta---> " Vaishnavas wo hai, jo dusre ki jeb kaatn....err I mean, jo dusro ka pain samajh sake "
               
                     
                          
      
      
      
      
      
      NathPanthis,  Siddhas  and   YOGIS :
      
      
         1) Ye bhi conventional religion ke rituals wagarh ko criticize karte the. ---> Simple, logical arguments ke basis pe. 
         
         2) Aise log the : Nathpanthis,   Sidddhacharas    or    Yogis
         
         3) Rennounciation ko advocate karte the
         
         4) Formless ultimate reality par meditate karo
         
         5) Yoga or breathing exercise karo, meditaiton bhi. 
         
         6) Low caste me famous ho gaye
         
       
       
       
       
       13th CE BHAKTI MOVEMENTS IN NORTH INDIA :
       
         
         - Kabir, Baba Nanak -----> Rejected existing beliefs
         
         - Tulsidas and Surdas ---> Accepted existing beliefs ----> Lekin, chahte the, ki ye sabko accessible ho
         
         - Tulsidas, Rambhakt ban gaya. 
         - Surdas ko Krishna se pyar ho gaya. 
            
              Surdas ke compilations :--> Sursagar,    Surasaravali     and     Sahitya Lahari
              
              
              
         - Assam me Shankaradeva, Namghar establish karne laga, jaha wo assamese babes ko letake, unhe apna Vishnu dikhata tha.      
              
              
         - Mirabai, ko Ravidas ne apna lemon taste karwa diya tha, jo khud ek neech jaat ka tha. 
         
         
         
         
         
         
      - KABIR : 
      
          
              - 15th-16th CE me raha. 
              
              - Mulle, Julahas/Weavers ke ghar bada hua. ----> Benaras ke pass
              
              - Isne, 'sakhis'  and ' pads' likhe
              
              - Iske baaki, milte hain---> Guru Granth sahib,   Panch Vani  or  Bijak me. 
              
              
              
              
              
              
              
      'Lilatilakam'---->  14th CE text hai----> Grammer or Pertics par. 
      
          - Ise, Manipravalam ne likha tha.           
              
              
              
         Bhanudatta ne Rasamanjari painting banai thi.

                       Buddhism once again, kyunki har pre me lode lage hain. 
                       
                       
                       
                       
      1) Paramitas :----> Perfection or Virtues
      
      
      2) Impermanence :---> Anicca
      
      
      3) Vajrayan Buddhism, Mahayan Buddhism ka part hai.                  
       
          
         
      4) Bhai ke 2 sabse pehle teachers :  Alara Kalama --> Sphere of Nothingness     and      Eddaka Ramaputta  --> Sphere of neither perception nor non-perception
      
      
      
      5) Awekaning : Bhai ko samajh aa gaya ki karma kaise kaam karta hai and  purani saari lifes ke baare me
      
      
      6) Saath hi saath bhai ke saare mental defilements (Asavas) bhi khatm ho gaye or    Samsara me rebirth ki bhi ending ho gayi. 
      
      
      
      7) Middle way ke baare me bhi samajh aa gaya----> Right path of spiritual practice to end suffering. 
      
      
      8) Final Nirvana---> At the age of 80 in Kushinagar, India. 
      
      
      9) Buddhism me existence ke 3 mark hote hain ----> Dukha,    Impermanence    and    Anatta(non-self)
      
      
     10) tanha --> Craving. --. Iski wajah se dukkha aata hai. 
     
     
     11) craving ki wajah se ham karma karte hain, jis se hum samsar se jud jaate hain---> Cycle of death and rebirth. 
     
     
     12) Craving bhi 3 tarah ki hoti hai :--> Kama-tanha ---> sense pleasure ke liye,    Bhava-tanha ---> Life-Death ki cycle continue karne ke liye ,  Vibhava-tanha ---> world or painful feelings ko na experience karne ki craving
     
     
     13) Vijnana ;----> Person's consciousness
     
     
     14) Pratitya-samutpada :----> dependent arisisng : Kuch bhi inpedendent nahi ho raha hai, sab kuch pehel kuch hua, uske basis pe hota hai.    Basically : Causality
     
     
     15) Sadayatan : 6 senses ,   Namarupa : Sentient body ,    Vedana : Feeling    
     
     
     16) Early buddhism ya Theravada buddhism me----> Arhat wo banda hota tha, jo same awekening paa chuka hai, buddha jaisi. 
     
     
     17) Theravad me Future Buddha ko Maitreya kehete hain----> ye abhi ek bodhisatta hota hai, working for future buddhahood. 
     
     
     18) Mahayan Buddhism me, Arhat ke attain karne ko inferior samjha jata hai, kyunki ye banda bas khud ke liye kaam kar raha hota hai. 
     
     
     19) Mahayan ke hisab se Bodhisaata sabse mast hai ----- Ex Maitreya    and     Avalokiteshvara
     
     
     20) Mahayan ki khudki ek unique interpretation hai, emptiness ki -----> Jo Madhyamak School me milti hai. 
     
         Yogara School bhi inhi ka hai---> Vasubandhu ko lagta tha ki sab kuch bas hamare dimaag me hi hai, or is se bahar kuch bhi nahi hai. ---->  Vijnapti-matrata Vada    and    Vijnanavada (doctrine of consciousness)
         
                          
     21) Mahayan buddhism wale ye bhi mante hain ki, ---> saare sentinet beings ke andar ek tathagata hota hai, as their 'essance, core inner nature, self'. ----> Ye Tathagatgarbha Sutra me mentioned hai. 
     
     
     22) 8 Fold Path : 
     
      
             Sila   ---> Ethics
             
             
             Samadhi  -> Meditation 
             
             
             Prajna ---> Wisdom            
             
                
      23) Buddha ke marne ke 400 saal baad, Pehli baar, Sri-Lanka me Triptika likhe gaye.  
       
       
      
      24) Sutras ko nikyas or agamas me organise kiya gaya tha. 
      
      
      25) Abhidharma---> Ye pehle ke 2 sutras ko explain karne ke liye likha gaya tha, lekin time ke saath saath, BC independent system of doctrinal exposition ban gaya. 
      
      
      26) Theravada or Sarvastivada school ka is par sabse jyada vishwas hai. 
      
      
           
      27) Isis THeravada abhidhamma or Pali commentaries(Atthakatha) se infleunce hokar, Buddhahosha ne Visuddhimarg===> 5th CE ka ek important text likha tha. 
      
      
      28) North India me, Sarvastivada school, bhi yahi sab, Abhidharma tradition se influenced tha. 
      
      
      29) Isi school ne, Kashmir me Kanishka II ke time, Mahabhasha (Great Commentary) likhi (158 - 176).      Vasubandhu ka abhidhamakosha---> Ye bhi ek important work hai. 
      
      
      30) Mahayan Sutras/Texts ko 1st CE BCE- AD ke beech likha gaya tha.
      
      31) Mahayan wale, hinayan literature ko kam weightage dete hain. ---> Inhe wo Sravakayan ya Hinayan kehte hain.    Ye sochte hain, ki hinayan wale texts chutiye logo ke liiye hai. 
      
      
      32) Mahayan walo ko lagta hai, ki jo log Bodhisatta ban na chahte hain, bas unke liye hai Mahayan school. 
      
      
      33) Inki alag hi cosmology or mythologies hain---> jisme powerful bodhisattvas hain---> nayi spiritual practices or ideas bhi. 
      
      
      
      34) Lekin, Theravada wale, ye sab bakwas mante hain.    
      
      
      35) Mahasamghika community ka split bhi BC yahi Mahayan wale randaap ke karan hua tha. 
      
      
      36) Mahayan ka sabse important hai-----> Mula-Madhyamika-karika ---> root verse of the middle way ----> Ise Nagarjun ne likha tha-----> Ye Madhyamik school ka foundational text hai.  
      
      
      
      37) 2nd Buddhist council me pehli baar, Schism aa gaya Sangha me----> Ye hua jab, kuch Sthavir("elders") ne, Vinaya monastic rules ko change karne ki baat ki. 
       
                   - Is se, jo conservatives the, unhe gussa aa gaya----> Ye khudko, Mahasamghika kehte the. 
                   
                   
      38) Ashoka ne expanision.exe file pe double click kiya---> Stupas , Mahabodhi Temple, Missionaries
      
      
      39) Sthavir--> Reformist chode---> Ye bhi expand hue, jis se naye schools bane--- Ex : Theravada School---> Ye south me congregate hua. 
      
          Dusra school tha : -- North India ka Sravastivada
          
       
       40) Mahasamghika school bhi fir, split ho gaya. 
       
       
       41) Pehle to ye splits, monsatary codes ki wajah se hote the, lekin, baad me ye splits, sala doctrinal differences ki wajah se aane lag gaye. 
       
       
       42) Iske, Baad, BC har koi khudki Triptika accumulate karne laga. 
       
       
       43) MilindPanho :---> 2nd CE BC me Greek Raja Menander or ek buddhist monk ke beech ki conversation pe based hai. 
       
       
       44) Kushans ne bhi khub paise diye inhe---> Particularly, Sravastivad walo ko(Reformist walo ki north Indian branch)  
       
       
       45) Kanishk ne expanision2.exe  pe double click kar diya. 
       
       
       46) Gandharan Buddhist Text, sabse purane written buddhist docs me se ek hai---> Or ye saare,   Dharmaguptaka school se related hain 
       
       
       
       47) Kumargupta I wali 5th CE me bani Nalanda,    Dharmapal wali 780 CE wali Vikramshila  ---> Dono hi Mahayan school ki thi. 
       
       
       48) Isi period me 4 tarah ke mahayan school develop hue :--> Yogacara,    Mahyamaka,   Tathagatagarhbha     and    Dingaga-Dharmakirti           Madhyamaka or Yagacara ek jaise hi hain, or purane buddhism se milte hain
       
       
       49) Guptas or Palas ke under, Tantric Buddhism a.k.a Vajrayan buddhism develop hua ---> Mantra,  Dharanis,  Mudras   and   Mandal jaise chutiyaap the isme.   Inhe Mahasiddhas naam ke yogi log ne develop kiya tha. 
       
       
       
       50 Isipatan----> Ye sarnath ka purana naam hai. Ya shayad naya. Anyway, Ishipatan, Sartnath se related hai. 
       
       
       
       
       51) Alexander Cunningham or Joseph David Beglar ne Mahabodi Temple, at Bodh Gaya ko restore karwaya. 
       
       
       
       
       
          Other Facts, jo mujhe shayad kaam aa jaye : 
          
          
          
          1) James Princep, Founding editor tha---> Asiatic Society of Bengal ke journal ka. 
          
               - Bhai, ne Kharoshti or Brahmi scripts ko decipher kiya tha. 
               
               - Ye waise, sikke-vikke ka kaam karta tha, err mera matlab, numismatic wala. 
               
               - Bhai ne Benaras ka detailed map tayyar kiya tha. 
               
               - Bhai 31 ka tha, tabhi mar gaya tha, kaam karte karte. 
       
          
       
          
          2) Archiological Survey of India, bana tha, 1861 me, by Alexander Cunningham---> Ye bhai mota tha. 
       
                     - Ye Princep ka chela tha. 
                     
                     - Ye, Sikh army ke gerail, Jean-Baptise Ventura se inspired tha---> Or isne, bohot saare Buddhist monuments ke survey kiye
                     
                     - Lord Canning ke time bai thi ye---> BC, wo bhi ek law se. 
                     
                     - Lekin, 1865 or 1871 ke beech, fund na hone ki wajah se suspend kar di gayi thi. 
                     
                     - Lekin, 1871 me Lawrence of Inida, jo Viceory tha, ne restore kar diya.  Jai ho Lawrence bhai, 1871 wale----> BC, iske theek 100 saal baad, Pakistan ki maa chodi gayi thi. 
                     
                     - Lekin, 1892 me 'Buck Crisis' start ho gaya tha, Jab Buck naam ke chode, ne announce kiya ki saala 3 saal me kuch naya nahi dhoonda to ASI band karke, uski jagah randi khana khol denge. 
                     
                     - 'Nigali Sagar' inscription se Buck Crisis kahtam hua.   Nigga   and   Buck.    Sometimes the memes just write themselves. 
       
                     -  1896 me Lumbini Pillar inscription mil gaya inhe. ---> Wo bhi Fuhrer naam ke bande ne dhunda.   
                     
                     -  1902 me Curzon ne, 24 saal ke Cambradige ke professor ko Director banaya----> Wo banda tha---> John Marshall.   Ye, 25 saal tak raha director. 
                     
                     -  Bhai ke time, pe Pehli baar ASI ne Museums khole---> Sarnath(1904),  Agra(1906),  Ajmer(1908),   Delhi Fort(1909),   Bijapur(1912),   Nalanda(1917),   Sanchi(1919). 
                     
                     -  Bhai ne, 1921 me Indus Valley Civilisation discovered. 
                     
                     -  Pehla, Indian Banda tha----> Daya Ram Sahni. 
                     
                     -  1944 me wheeler ko lagaya gaya----> Ye bhai ne, Arikamedu ke Iron Age sites,   or    Brahmagiri, Chadravalli or Maski ke Stone Age sites discover ki. ---> Wheeler ne hi, Ancient India naam ka journal start. 
                     
                     -  Amalananda Ghosh ke tenure me, Kalibangan, Lothal or Dholavira dundhe gaye, before 1968. 
                     
                     -  1958 me, Antiquities and Art Treasures Act, 1972 ko pass kiya gaya----> Is se monuments ko central protection milne lagi. 
                     
                     -  Pehli lugai thi---> 1981 me Indira Gandhi ke time, appoint ki gayi-----> Debala Mitra
                     
                     -  Abhi, ye Ministry of Culture ka attached office hai. 
       
          3) Is se pehle, Asiatic Society bani thi, 1784 me, by Willam Jones.
          
              - Iska sabse important kaam tha, wo princep bhaiiya wala----> 1837 me Brahmi script ka decipherment karna
              
              - Or ye Charles Wilkins bhaiyya the, jinhone, Bhagavat gita ko English me translate kar diya tha, BC 1785 me hi. 
              
              - Isme, Warren Hasing ne kaafi saath diya tha. 
       
       
        
         
        
           4) Hindu Schools : 
           
            
                1) Samkhya : --> Strongly dualistic --> Ek purus hota hai, ek prakriti.    Bas 6 praman hi reliable mean hai, knowledge gain karne ke.   ----> Ye kaafi rationalist school hai.  
                
                                    - Kapil bhai ne shuru kiya tha, sankhya gin na.     'Kapil Sharma show dekhne wale type logo ki sankhya badhti jaa rahi'. 
                                     
                                    - Jiv--> Ek state, jisme purus, prakriti se bonded hota hai, kisi form me.
                                    
                                     
                
                2) Yoga :---> Monotheisci---> Ye sankhya se hi emerge hua hai----> Samkhya theory ka practicle use ----> Meditation, Contemplation and liberation 
                
                                - Patanjali
                
                
                3) Nyay/Logic :----> Epistemology---> Source of knowledge ko explore karta hai : Saare nahi, 6 me se bas 4 hi reliable hain.    Right knowledge se moksha milta hai. 
                
                                       - Gautam ka Nyay Sutra
                
                4) Vaisesika :---->  Atomism ka empiricist school
                
                                      - Phyisical universe ke saare objects, jo Paramanu me convert kar sakte hain. 
                                      
                                      - Kandana Kashyap
                
                5) Mimamsa :---> Orthodox---> Correct interpretationns kya hai vedas ki
                                
                                   - Jaimini
                                 
                
                6) Vedanta :--> Ise, Uttara-Mimamsas bhi kehte hain
                
                                  - Dvaitavad ---> Nimbarka in 7th CE
                                  
                                  - Advait ------> Gaudapada (500 CE)   and    Adi Shankaracharya (8th CE)
                                  
                                  - Vishishtadvaita ----->   Nathamuni,  Yamuna   and   Ramanuja  (1017 - 1137 CE)
                                  
                                  - Dvait ---------------> Madhavacharya (1199 - 1278)
                                  
                                  - Suddhadvaita --------> Vallabha (1479 - 1531 CE) 
                
                
                
                     Charavak :---> Free Will hoti hai
                     
                     
                     Ajivika :-----> Koi Free will nahi hoti
                     
                     
                     Buddhism :----> Koi Atman nahi hoti
                     
                     
                     Jainism :-----> Atman hoti hai, lekin ham batayenge kya hoti hai.

       		India ke Old Stone age wali jagah : 
       		
       		  1) MP : Soan River ka bank,   BhimBetka,   Mageshwa

       					Old Stone Manush : Hunter-Gather,   Aag jalani aati thi, lekin, farming nahi aati thi.     Dono aadmi aurat, hunting karte the(bhimbetka caves me aurat ko shikar karte dikhaya hai). 
       
       
       
       
       		New Stone Age yani, Neo-lithic Manush ne, Wheel bana liya, or apna transportation businees start kar liya. 
       		
       		
       		Bhai ne sabse pehle, Copper metal use karna start kiya. 
       
       
       
       
       
       
       
       
       1856 me, jab brit log, Ravi river par, Railway lines bicha rahe the, tab unhe waha Sand ki mound mili.   Yaha unhe, burnt-brichs or ruins mile. 
       
       
       Chutiyo ne, unhi eento se railway lines bana li. 
       
       
       1921 me archeologist log ne aake dekh, to bole, "Bahan ke lodo, ye to Harappa naam ki ancient city hai". 
       
       
       
       			- Inhe houses me koi windows nahi thi. 
       			
       			- Har ghar me ek Well, or bathroom tha. 
       			
       			- Ghar ke aage, dustbins hote the. 
       
       
       
       
       
       Aryan Women Poets : Lopamudra,  Abala, Kosa,  Vishwara
       
       	Early Vedic Time me, Caste System nahi tha, or Women Remarriage allowed tha.

       Later Vedic me ye sab likha gaya tha : SamVeda,  Yajur Veda   Atharva Veda
       
       		Brahmans,  Upanishads,  Aranyakas bhi isi time likhe gaye the. 
       
       
       			Vajpaya :  Pooja ke time
       			
       			Rajasuya or Ashvamedha : Apni authority establish karne ke liye. 
       			
       			
       			
        	Later Vedic Age ki aurato log : Gargi,  Maitreyi

       	National Emblem :  Ashokan Pillar se liya hai. 
       	
       		           National Flag ka Chakra hai : Dharma Chakra --> Ashoka Pillar se hai.

			Kapadapuram or Madurai : Pandyas ki capital
			
				i) Madurai me 1st Sangam hua tha. 
				
			       ii) Kapdapuram me 2nd--- YE Tsunami me dub gaya tha. 
			       
			      iii) Madurai me hi 3rd Sangam hua tha	
			      
			      			Sangam ko kudal bhi kehte hain. 
 
 
 			Abhi jo Sangam Lit available hai : Ettuthogai   and   Pathupattu. 
 			
 				Inme Poem likhne wale log : Kapilar,  Paranar,   Avvayyar  and  Nakkeerar
 				
 				
 				
 				
 			Pride of Tamil lit : Tolkappium ---> 3rd Sangam se pehle ki hai. 		
 			
 			
 			
 			Ye saare Tamil Port Cities hain :  Puhar,  Kaveri,  Korkai,   Musiri,   Thondi,   Vanchi
 			
 			
 			
 					Tamil poets ne 5-fold division kiya tha land ka : 
 					
 						a) Mountains : Kuinchi
 						
 						b) Forest :  Mullai
 						
 						c) Coastal : Neidal 
 						
 						d) Sandy : Palai
 						
 						
 						
 						
 						
 						
 						
 				T.N History 
 				
 				
 	2nd Highest Peak in the world : Mt Godwin Austen--- > Kashmir ke north wale Karakoram range me. 
 	
 	
 	Assam ke mountains : Pat Koi,   Nagai  and  Lushai Ranges. 
 	
 		- Ye hills, thick forest se covered hain. 			
 				
 				
 				
 	Western Ghats wali coast : Konkan upto Goa,     Kannara     and   Malabar
 	
 		- Western Ghats ke passes : Junnar,  Kanheri   and   Karle			
 				
 				
 	Palghat pass : Kaveri Valley to Malabar Coast. 
 	
 		- Indo-Roman trade hoti thi iske through. 
 		
 	
 	Southern peninsula ki highest peak : Anaimudi. 
 	
 		- 2nd Highest --> Dodabetta
 		
 
 	Eastern Ghats--> Ek to ye jyada bade nahi hai, dusra, inke through bohot saari nadiya pass hoti hain. 
 	
 	
 	Coromandal Coast par located cities : Arikkamedu,  Mamallapuram  and  Kaveripattanam			
 				
 				
 	Godavari, Krishna, Tungabhadra and Kaveri ---> West to East
 	
 		K and T ke beech ka area---> Raichur doab---> Fertile soil
 		
 		
 		
 		
 	Paleolithic : Old Stone Age ---> 10 k BC se pehle ka period. 
 	
 		- Soan Valley and Potwar Plateau on NW India
 		
 		- Siwalik Hills on North India
 		
 		- Bhimpetka
 		
 		- Adamgarh Hill in Narmada valley
 		
 		- Kurnool in Andhra Pradesh
 		
 		- Attiramakkam near Chennai
 		
 			
 			1) Hunted animals and Gathered edible plants and tubers. ----> Hunter-Gatherers. 
 			
 			2) Stone Tools ka use karte the hunting ke liye. 
 			
 				- Quartzite naam ka stone. 				
 						
 						
 			3) Thodi bohot koshish ki- --> Pot banane ki, Domestication and Agri ki. 
 			
 		
 		
 	
 	Mesolithic or Middle Stone Age : 10 k to 6 k BC
 	
 		- Langhanj in Gujrat
 		
 		- Adamgarh in Madhya Pradesh
 		
 		- Some places in Rajasthan, UP and Bihar
 		
 		
 			1) Ek naye tarah ka stone tool : Chota sa, 5cm se kam size ke---> Microliths
 			
 			2) Baaki saale ye bhi Hunter-Gatherers hi the. 
 			
 			3) Lekin, bade bade janwaro ki jagah, small animal hunting and Fishing par dhyan dete the. 
 			
 			4) In saalo ne, Bow and Arrow ka use karna shuru kar diya. 
 			
 			5) Ye saale, ek jagah par kafi time rukne lage---> Inhone hi, Domestication of animals ,   Horticulture and primitive cultivation start kiya. 
 			
 			
 			
 	
 	Neolihic Age : 6 k to 4 K BCE
 	
 		- Kashmir Valley
 		
 		- CHirand in Bihar
 		
 		- Belan Valley in UP
 		
 		- Maski, Brahmagiri, Hallur and Kodekal in Karnataka. 
 		
 		- Paiyampalli in Tamil Nadu
 		
 		- Utnur in Andhra Pradesh
 		
 		
 			1) Practice of Agri, Domestication of Animals
 			
 			2) Polishing of stone tools---> Hunting and Cutting Trees. 
 			
 			3) Manufacture of Pottery
 			
 			4) Emergence of villages and Communities based on sedantary life. 
 			
 			5) Mud Bricks se ghar banate the, ghass ki jhompdi ki jagah. 
 			
 			6) Wheels ka use karke, pottery banate the. 
 			
 			7) Pottery ka use karte the, cooking and for storage of food grains
 			
 			8) Large urns ka use karte the, as coffins and for the burial of the dead. 
 			
 			9) Wheat, Barley, Rice and Millet ugate the.     Eastern India me Rice cultivation was extensive
 			
 		       10) Used Clothes made of Cotton and Wool. 
 		       
 		       
 		       
 		       
 	Chalcolithic(Copper-stone) period
 	
 		- Copper and bronze was used. 
 		
 		- New Tech : Smelting metal ore and Crafting metal artifacts.      But the use of stone tool was not given up. 
 		
 		- Log, metal ores ki talash me door door tak travel karne lage---> Led to the development of Chalcolithic cultures. 
 		
 			1) Harappan Culture
 			
 			2) South India ki river valleys par
 			
 			
 	
 	Iron-Age : Vedas me mentioned hai. 
 	
 		- South India me related with : Megalithic Burials
 		
 			- Burial pits were covered with large stones. 
 			
 			- Hallur and Maski in Karnataka
 			
 			- Nagarkonda in AP
 			
 			- Adichchanallur in Tamil Nadu
 			
 		
 		- Black and Red Pottery
 		
 		- Iron Artifacts such as hoes, Sickles and Small weapons---< Ye sab burial pits me mile hain. 
 		
 		
 		
 	
 	Harappan Civilization : 2300 to 1750 BC
 	
 		- 1st Discovered sithe thi, Harappa. 
 		
 		- Major Sites : 
 		
 			1) Kot Diji in Sindh
 			
 			2) Kalibangan in Rajasthan
 			
 			3) Rupar in Punjab
 			
 			4) Banawali in Haryana
 			
 			5) Lothal, Surkotada and dholavira in Gujrat
 			
 			
 		- Mohanjodaro--> Largest city--> 200 Hectares
 		
 		
 		
 		- Pre-Harappan : Eastern Baluchistan : Mehragarh---> 150 miles NW of Mohanjodaro
 		
 			- Nomadic people began to lead a settled agri life. 
 			
 		
 		- Early-Harappan : Amri and Kot DIji
 		
 			- People lived in large villages in the plains
 			
 			- Gradual growth of towns in Indus Valley. 
 			
 		
 		- Mature-Harappan : Kalibangan
 		
 			- Great Cities emerged---> Elaborate town planning and urban features
 			
 		
 		- Late-Harappan : Lothal
 		
 			- Decline started
 			
 			- Lothal was surronded by a massive brick wall as flood protection
 			
 			- It was an emporium of trade between the Harappa civ and Remaining parts of India as well as Mesopotamia
 			
 			
 			
 		
 		- Harappa, Mohanjodaro, Kalibangan--> Teeno jagah, Citadel bana tha, on a high podium of mud brick
 		
 		- Burnt Bricks ka large scale use hota tha. 
 		
 		- Stone Buildings ka absence
 		
 		- Mohanjodaro ki largest building hai : Granary ---> 150 feet length and 50 feet breadth. 
 		
 		- Harappa ke citadel me to sala 6 grannaries thi. 
 		
 		- Wheat and Barley main crop thi. 
 		
 		- Sheeps, Goats and Buffalos ko domesticate karte the. 
 		
 		- Pottery---> Plain and in some places, Red and Black Pottery is found. 
 		
 		- Semi-precious stones ke beads banate the. 
 		
 		- Gold, COpper and Tin, import karte the. 
 		
 		- Dancing Girl : MOhanjodaro made in Bronze
 		
 		- Harappan Script : Right To left
 		
 		- Pashupati Seal : 4 animals : Elephant, Tiger, Rhino and Buffalo  and  2 deers. 
 		
 		- Mohanjodaro me, complete burial and post-cremation burial karte the. 
 		
 		- Harappa me wodden coffins mile hain. 
 		
 		- Lothal me pot burial ke evidence mile hain. 				
 			       				
 			       				
 			       				
 	Vedic Culture : 
 	
 	 		- 6th CE BCE tak whole of North India ko occupy kar liya tha. ---> Aryavarta
 	 		
 	 		- 1500 se 1000 BC ---> Early Vedic Period
 	 		
 	 		- 1000 to 600 BC  ---> Later Vedic Period
 			       				
 			      
 			      
	Vedic Lit : 
	
		1) Earliest Veda : Rig  Veda---> 1028 hymns and Mantras and Stuff
		
		2) Yajur Veda ---> Rules to be observed at the time of sacrifices
		
		3) Sama Veda --_> Chanting during sacrifices
		
		4) Atharva Veda :--> Details of Rituals
		
		
		
		5) Brahmanas : Treatises relating to prayer and Sacrificial ceremony
		
		6) Upanishads ---> Pholosophical texts dealing with topics like, soul, the absolute, origin of the world and the mysteries of nature. 
		
		7) Aranyaks : Forest Books----> Mysticism, Rites, Rituals and Sacrifices
		
		
		8) Ramayan : Valmiki
		
		9) Mahabharat : Vedavyas 			       				
 			       	
 			       	
 			       	
	Leader of village : Gramani
	
	Group of Villages : Visu---> Headed by Vishaypati
	
	Highest political unit---> Jana or Tribe
	
	Rig-Vedic period ke tribal kingdoms : Bharatas, Matsayas, Yadus and Purus. 
	
	Head of Kingdom --> Rajan or King. 
	
	King was assisted by Purohit a.k.a Priests  and   Senani or Commander
	
	2 popular bodies--> Sabha and Samiti 
	
		Sabha : Council of Elders
		
		Samiti : General assembly of entire people
		
	
	
	Basic unit of society--> Family or Graham
	
	Head of Family ---> Grahapati
	
	Generally Monogomous, but polygamy was prevelant among royal and noble families. 
	
	- Women were given equal opp for spiritual and intellectual dvpt. 
	
	- WOmen poets : Apala,  Ghosa,  Viswavara  and  Lopamudra
	
	- Women attended popular assemblies. 
	
	- No child marriage and practice of sati was absent
	
	
	
	Social divisions were not rigid. 
	
	Rig Vedic society-- Pastoral people :---> Main occupation was cattle rearing. 
	
	North India me settle hone ke baad---> Started practicing agriculture
	
	Carpenters produced chariots and ploughs
	
	Trade was conducted on barter system 
	
		- Baad me, Nishka naam ke gold coins became medium of exchange. 
		
		
	Important Rig Vedic Gods : Prithvi, Agni, Vayu, Varuna and Indra		
	
	
	
	
	Later Vedic Period (1000 to 600 BC)
	
		- Satapatha Brahmana : Mentions , expansion of Aryans to the eastern Gangetic Plains
		
		- Growth of Large kingdoms--> Kuru and Panchala
		
		- Parikshat and Janamejaya--> Famous rulers of Kuru
		
		- Pravahan Jaivali---> Famous king of Panchalas---> Patron of learning. 
		
		- In dono kingdoms ke fall ke baad---> Kosala, Kasi and Videha aa gaye. 
		
		- Kasi ka ruler tha, Ajatasatru
		
		 - Janak, tha, Videha ka. ---> Iski capital thi, mihila me. 
		 
		 	- Yajnavalkya naam ka scholor isi bhai ke court me rehta tha. 
		 	
		 - Magadha, Anga and Vanga--> easternmost kingdoms
		 
		 
		 
		 
		- Later vedic period me larger kingdoms form hue. ---> Bohot saare janas milke, Janapads or Rashtras bane. 
		
		- King bohot saare ceremonies karne laga
		
			- Rajasuya --> Consecration ceremony
			
			- Asvamedha --> Horse Sacrifice
			
			- Vajpeya ---> Chariot Race
			
		
		- Kings title adopt karne laga jaise ki : Rajavisvajana,  Ahilabhuvanapathi,  Ekrat and Samrat
		
		- Sabha and Samiti ki importance kam ho gayi. 
		
		- Vaisyas log organised themselves into guilds known as Ganas. 
		
		- Rig Vedic nishka ke alawa, Satamana naam ka gold coin and  Krishnala naam ka silver coins were used. 
		
		
		
		- Women lost their righs to attend assemblies. 
		
		- Child marriage became common. 
		
		- Aitreya Brahaman --> Daughter = Source of misery
		
		- Importance of prayer declined,  and that of sacrifice increased. 
		
		- Priesthood became a hereditary profession. 
		
		
		
	
	Jainism : Vardhaman Mahavira--> 539 BC to 467 BC
	
		- Ye banda, Jain tradition ka 24th Tirthankar tha. 
		
		- Vaisali ke paas, Kundangram me bhai ka janam hua tha. 
		
		- 30 saal ki umar me ghar se nikal gaya. Bhai ki wife ka naam Yasoda tha. 
		
		- 43 saal ki umar me---> Achieved higher spiritual knowledge--> "kevala Gnana". ---> Became Mahavira and JIna
		
		- Agle 30 saal gyan choda.  Died at the age of 72 at Pava near Rajagriha
		
		
		
			3 Principles of Jainisms : Triratnas
			
				1) Right Faith ---> Belief in the teaching of Mahavira
				
				2) Right Knowledge ---> There is no god and world has been existing without a creator and all objects posses a soul 
				
				3) Right Conduct ---> 5 great Vows : 
				
							1) Not to injure life
							
							2) Not to lie
							
							3) Not to steal
							
							4) Not to acquire property
							
							5) Not to lead immoral life
							
			- Mahavira--> Rejected authority of vedas and objected to vedic rituals. 
			
			- Bhai ne sangha organise kiye, to spread the teachings.     Admi, Aurat dono ko include kiya.     Monks and Lay followers, dono included the sangha me. 
			
			- Western India and Karnataka me rapidly spread hua. 
			
			- CM, Kharavela of Kalinga,  Gangas, Kadambas, Chalukyas and Rashtrkutas---> Patronized jainism
			
			
			
			
			- 4th CE BCE  ke end me ek famine aaya===> Bhadrabagu, bhag liya, CM ko leke, Sravana Belgola ki taraf. 
			
				- Sthulabahu wahi ruk gaya----> Changed the code of conduct for monks. 
				
				- Sthulbahu Digambar logo ka leader ban gaya, or ye log nange ho gaye. 
				
				- Isi chode ne, Pataliputra me, 3rd BC ki begining me 1st Jain Council organise ki. 
				
				- 5th CE AD me, 2nd Jain council organise hui---> In Valabhi. 
				
					- 12 Angas ki final compilation kiya gaya		 
	
	
	
	
	Buddhism : 567 to 487 BC
	
		- Bhai ke papa ka naam tha - Sudhhodhana of the sakya clan.   Maa thi Mayadevi. 
		
		- 16 ki age me, Yasodhara naam ki bandi se shadi kar li, or Rahula naam ka beta hua. 
		
		- 35 ki age me enlightenment mili. 
		
		- 1st sermon, Benaras me dia. 
		
		- 80 ki age me shaheed at Kusinagara. 
		
		- Most important desciples : Sariputta, Moggallana,  Ananda, Kassapa  and Upali. 
		
		- Kosala ka Prasenajit and Magadh ke Ajatasatru became his imoortant desciples. 
		
		- Visited places like, Benaras, Rajagriha, Sravasti, Vaisali , Nalanda and Patligrama.
		
		- God, Soul, Karma , rebirth par jyada dhyan nahi diya. 
		
		- 4 noble truths : 
		
			1) World is full of suffering
			
			2) Cause of suffering is desire
			
			3) Get rid of desires to get rid of sufferings. 
			
			4) Can be done by following the 8-fold path
			
		- Law of Karma par great emphasis. 
		
		- Soul does not exist. 
		
		- Emphasized Ahimsa. 
		
		- Againt social districtions but caste system ke baare me kuch nahi bola hai. 
		
		- 2 kind of disciples the : Monks and Lay worshipers(Upasikas). 
		
		- Famous monks : Sariputta, Moggallana and Ananda. 
		
		- Ashoka ne, West Asia and Ceylon me spread  kiya. 
		
		
			1)  1st buddhist COuncil : Rajagraha, under Mahakasapa ----> Immediately after the death of Buddha.
			
					- To maintain the purity of Teachings. 
					
			
			2) 2nd Buddhist COuncil : Vaisali around 383 BC. 
			
			
			3) 3rd Buddhist COuncil : Pataliputra under Asoka. ----> Moggaliputta Tissa presided over it. 
			
				- Final Version of triptika were completed. 
				
			
			4) 4th Buddhist Council : kanishka under Vasumitra
			
				- Asvaghosh ne participate kiya tha. 
				
				- Mahayan school of buddhism came into existence. 
				
					
	 		5) Buddha ke marne ke 500 saal baad---> Buddhist texts were compiled ---> Triptikas, namely Sutta, Vinaya and Abhidhamma
	 		
	 		
	 		
	 		
	 
	 4 Epic Mahajanpadas : Vatsa, Avanti, Kosala and Magadha
	 
	 
	 	1) Vatsa : Yamuna River ke bank par tha. 
	 	
	 		- Kausambi capital thi, Allahbad ke pass. 
	 		
	 		- Most popular ruler : Udayana. 
	 		
	 		- Avanti, Anga and Magadha ke saath metrimonial alliances the. 
	 		
	 		- Baad me Avanti ne hi annex kar liya. 
	 		
	 		
	 	
	 	2) Avanti : Iski capital thi, Ujjain
	 	
	 		- Most imp ruler : Pradyota
	 		
	 		- Is bhai ne, Udayan ki beti chodi thi, jo Vasavadatta kahalati thi. 
	 		
	 		- Is bhai ko bhi buddha ki baat pasand thi. 
	 		
	 		- Iske baad ke log, muth maru the, isliye, Magadha ne lambe lund ka fayda utha, Avanti par kabja kar liya. 
	 		
	 	
	 	3) Kosala : Ayodhya capital tha iska. 
	 	
	 		- Sabse famous ruler - Prasenjit
	 		
	 		- Ye bhai, bhayanak padha likha tha. 
	 		
	 		- Isne bhi Magadha ka lemon chus liya tha, matrimonial allaiance karke. 
	 		
	 		- Isne, Bimbisara ko kasi or apni bahan ki choot marne di. 
	 		
	 		- Or badle me, isne Bimbisara ki beti chod di. 
	 		
	 		- Is bhai ke marne ke baad, Magadh ne Kosala par bhi kabja kar liya. 
	 		
	 		
	 	4) Magadh : Stratec position between the upper and lower part of Gangetic valley
	 	
	 		- Fertile soil thi. 
	 		
	 		- Rajgi ke pass Iron ore hills the, or Gaya ke pass, Iron and copper ke deposits the. 
	 		
	 		- Trade highway ke centre me location thi. 
	 		
	 		- Magadh ki capital thi, Rajagriha. 
	 		
	 		- Bimbisara and Ajatasatru ke reign ke time, power reached its zenith. 
	 		
	 		
	 
	 
	 Bimbisara (546 to 494 BC)
	 
	 	- Ye Haryanka Dynasty ka tha. 
	 	
	 	- Vaisali or Kosala ki rande chodi, for matrimonial alliance. 
	 	
	 	- Ye contamporary tha, Mahavir or Buddha ke. 
	 	
	 	
	 	
	 Ajatasatru (494 to 462 BC)
	 
	 	- Kosala and Vaisali se lode liye. 
	 	
	 	- Vaisali se lode lete time, Patna ki importance samajh aayi.   Shuru me bhai Jain tha, Fir buddhist ban gaya tha. 
	 	
	 	- Barhut Sculpture me ise, Buddha se milte hue dikhaya gaya hai. 
	 	
	 	- Mahavamsa ke hisab se bhai ne khub saare Chaiyas and Viharas banaye the. 
	 	
	 	- Rajagraha me 1st Buddhist Council setup ki thi, Buddha ke marte hi. 
	 	
	 	
	 	- Iske baad aaya, UDAYIN---> Isne finally, capital shift ki, Patna ki or---> On confluence of Ganga and Son. 
	 	
	 	
	 
	 
	 Saisunaga Dynasty : Haryanka se magadh capture kiya.
	 
	 	- Saisunaga ---> Kakavarman or Kalasoka
	 	
	 	- 2nd Buddhist council was held at Vaisali. 
	 	
	 	- Kalasoka ko, Nanda Dynasty ke founder ne maar diya.
	 	
	 	
	 Nanda Dynasty :Mahapadma Nanda was the ruler
	 
	 			- Bhai khudko, ekrat kehta tha. Ek Rat. 
	 			
	 			- Purans me bhai ki conquests mentioned hai. 
	 			
	 			- Kalinga ke Kharavela ki Hathigumpha inscription----> Mentions, conquest of Kalinga by Nandas. 
	 			
	 			- Isne, 10 saal rule kiya tha. 
	 			
	 			- Last Nanda ruler tha --> Dhana Nanda
	 			
	 			- Ye gandu, logo se bohot oppresively tax collect kiya karta tha. 
	 			
	 			- Isi ke time, Alexander aaya tha India me. 
	 			
	 		
	 		1) Tamil Sangam work : Ahananuru :---> enormous wealth of Nandas
	 		
	 		2) CM ne, kautilya ki madad se, Dhana Nanda ki maa chod di. 
	 		
	 		
	 
	 Haryanka ---> Sisunaga ----> Nanda ----> Mauryas
	 
	 
	 
	 
	 
	 
	 
	 Cyrus the Great ki invasion : Achaemenian Empire ka tha. 
	 
	 	- Gandhara region capture kiya. 
	 	
	 	- Indus River ke west ki saari Indian tribes ne, apni gaand jhuka li thi iske saamne. 
	 	
	 	- 530 BC ke aaspass ke time
	 	
	 	
	 
	 Darius I : Ye Cyrus ka pota tha. 
	 
	 	- Isne, 518 BC me, Punjab and Sindh capture kiya. 
	 	
	 	- Ye, Acharemeniaan Empire ka 20th Satrapy bana or sabse fertile area tha. 
	 	
	 	- Skylas ke under, Darius ne naval expedition bheja to explore the indus. 
	 	
	 
	 Xerxes : 465 BC ke aspass. 
	 
	 	- Ye choda, Greek logo se ladne ke liye, Punjabi ikathe karke le gaya tha. 
	 	
	 	
	 	
	 - In invasions ki wajah se, North West me, Kharoshti script famous ho gayi thi. 
	 
	 - Mauryan art bhi kafi inspired hai inse. ---> Monolithic pillars of Ashoka and the sculptures found on them. 
	 
	 
	 
	 Alexander the Great : 
	 
	 	1) Persia capture kiya, by defeating Darius 3.0 in the Battle of Arbela in 330 BC. 
	 	
	 	2) Crossed Indus in 326 BC 
	 	
	 	3) Died in Babylon in 323 BC	
	 
	 
	 		
	 		
	 									       	
 			       	
 			       	
 	Arthasastra : Kautilya ne likha tha
 	
 		- 15 books  and  180 chapters. 
 		
 		- 3 parts : i) Kings and his council + Depts of Govs     ii) Civil  and  Criminal laws     iii) Diplomacy and War
 		
 		
 		
 	Mudrarakshas : Visakadatta ne likha tha. 
 	
 		- Drama in Sanskrit. 
 		
 		- Describes how, CP +Kautilya overthrew Nandas 				       	
 			       	
 			    
 			    
 	Ashokan Edicts : 
 	
 		1) Asokan Inscription : 1st deciphered by James Princep in 1837. ---> written in Pali language, and sometime in Prakrit. 
 		
 		2) Brahmi Script was used.   In NW India---> Karoshti script. 
 		
 		3) 14 major rock edicts ---> Major cities me thi. 
 		
 		4) XIII Rock Edict ---> War with Kalinga
 		
 		5) Pillar Edict VII ---> Effort to promote Dhamma within his kingdom		    
 			       				
 			       				
 			       				
 			       				
	CM : FOunder of Mauryan Empire
	
		- 25 saal ki umar me, Patliputra capture kiya, Dhanananda se. 
		
		- 305 BC me, Selukas Niketar ko lode diye. 
		
		- Reign ke end me Jainism accept kar liya, went to Sravan Belgola and starved himself to death. 
		
		
		
	Bindusara (298 to 273 BC)
	
		- Mysore tak bhai, ne Deccan fatah kiya. 
		
		- Syrian king Antiochus I ne ambassador bheja. 
		
		- Isne, Ajivikas ko support kiya tha. 
		
		- Ashoka ko, Ujjain ka governor lagaya. 
		
		
	
	Asoka the Great (273 to 232 AD)
	
		- Ujjain ka Governor tha.    Bindusara ke time, Taxila me revolt face kiya tha. 
		
		- 261 BC me, Kalinga capture kiya. 
		
		- Upagupta naam ke buddhist monk ki baato me aa gaya bhai. 
		
		- Dharma Mahamatras naam ke specail officers appoint kiye, to speed up the progress of Dhama. 
		
		- 241 BC me, Lumbini Garden visit kiye. 
		
		- Sarnath, Sravasi and Kusinagar bhi visit kiya. 
		
		- Mahendra and Sangamitra ke under, Sri-Lanka me bhi ek mission bheja---> Planted a bodhi tree there. 
		
		- 3rd Buddhist council convene ki at Patliputra in 240 BC---> sangha ko strengthen karne ke liye. 
		
		- Moggaliputta Tissa ne preside kiya tha. 
		
		- Died in 232 BC --> Jiske baad, Mauryan Empire got split into 2 parts - Western and Eastern. 
		
		- Western part was ruled by Kunala ==> Son of Ashoka
		
		- Eastern part was ruled by Dasaratha ==> Grandson Of AShoka
		
		- Bactrian invasion ki wajah se western part collapsed. 
		
		- Last Mauryan king tha---> Brihatratha ---> Assassinated by Pushyamitra Sunga. 
		
		
		
	Inke day to day admin ke liye hota tha ---> Amatyas ---> Civil servants, like IAS officers.
	
		- Kautilya ne elaborate method of selection diya tha. 
		
	
	Dhamma Mahamatras ---> To supervise the spread of Dhamma
	

	Samharta : Chief of Revenue Department---> Charge of Collection of all revenues of the empire. 
	
		- FIxed at 1/6th of the produce. 
		
		
	Mauryan Armies : Under senapati 
	
		- Soldiers paid in Cash. 
		
		- Military officers ko ranks ke hisab se salary milti thi. 
		
		- Pliny ke hisab se, inke paas 6 lakh infantry thi.    Inke pass Navy, Transport and Supply wings bhi the. 
		
		- Har wing, 'Adhyaksha' ke under tha. 
		
		
		
	
	Civil or Criminal, dono tarah ki courts exist karti thi
	
		- Chief Justice of the Supreme court ==> Dharmathikarin
		
		
	Ye saale regular census bhi conduct karte the. 
	
	

	- Inke empire ki 4 provinces thi : Taxila,    Ujjain,    Suvarnagiri   and  Kalinga
	
		- Royal family ke members ko governor laga dete the. ---> Maintain Law and order and collect taxes. 
		
		- Rajukas ke under, District Admin tha. 
		
		- Ise assist karne ke liye hote the : Yuktas or subordinate officials. 
		
		- Gramani ke haath me village admin tha. 
		
		- 10 or jyada villages, Gopa, naam ke official ke under aate the. 
		
		- Nagarika : City Superintendent
		
		- 6 committes thi, to help admin : 1) Industries  2) Foreigners  3) Registration of Birth   4) Trade   5) Mfg + Sale of goods   6) Collection of Sales tax
		
		
		
	- Ashoka ke pehle, bas wooden monuments bante the---> jo saale sab jal gaye. 
	
	- Ashoka ke time se, Stones se monuments ban na shuru hue. 
	
	- Is bhai ka bhi bas ek Sanchi Stupa hi bacha hai. 
	



	Asokan Pillars : Finest specimen of Mauryan Art 
	
		- Delhi, Allahabad, Rummindai,  Sanchi and Sarnath
		
		- Sarnath pillar par---> 4 lions standing back to back
		
		
	- Sanchi Stupa ashoka se pehle ka bana hua tha--> using bricks---> But Ashoka ne, use enlarge karwaya tha. 
	
	
	- Ashoka ne or iske bete, Dasaratha ne, Ajivikas ko caves donate ki thi. ---> Interior walls polished like mirrors. 
	
		- FOr residence of monks. 
		
		- Bodh Gaya ke pass ki Barabar Hills---> Wonderful pieces of mauryan Architecture. 
		
		
	- Ashoka ki pro-buddhist policies ne---> Brahmins ko antagonize kar diya---> Bought a revolution led by Pushyamitra Sunga. 
	
	
	
	
	
	
	
	
	Ashoka ke jane ke baad, Mauryan Empire disintegrate ho gaya 	:
	
		- Foreign Invasion  ki wajah se NW India gaya. 
		
		- Kalinga declares independence
		
		- Satavahanas establish independent rule
		
		- RUle confined to gangetic valley----> Soon replaced by Sunga Dynasty 
		
		
	
	Sungas : Founder ==> Pussymitra Sunga
	
		1) Ye sala, Mauryas ka Commander in CHief hua karta tha. 
		
		2) Iske ane se, greeks ki gand me bhi guda aa gaya, or un saalo ne, Patliputra tak occupy kar liya. 
		
		3) Lekin, Pussy mitra ne territories regain kar li. 
		
		4) Isne, Kalinga ke Kharavela ke against campaing kiya. 
		
		5) Pussymitra ko Brahmanism pasand tha. ---> Saale ne 2 sacrifices kiye the.    Lekin, Isne Buddhist art bhi patronize ki thi. 
		
		6) Iske time me, Bharhut and Sanchi ke monuments ko renovate kiya gaya. 
		
	
	
	- Inka last ruler tha----> Devabhuti ----> Murdered by his Minister--> Vasudev Kanva---> Jis bhai ne, Kanva Dynasty found kar li. 
	
		- Ye dynasty chali agle 45 saal. 
		
		- Inke saath hi, Magadh ki story khatam hui. 
		
		
		
	Satvahanas : Mauryas ke baad, 450 saalo tak rule kiya. 
	
		- Inhe 'Andhras' bhi bulate the. 
		
		- Purans me mentioned hain. 
		
		- Nasik and Nanagadh inscriptions bhi ----> Reign of Gautamiputra Satakarni
		
	
	1) Inka FOunder - Simuka Simuka Ray Mysterio
	
		- Iske baad aaya Krishna ---> Ye bhai kingdom ko Nasik tak le gaya. 
		
		
		- 3rd bari aai---> Sri Satkarni ===> Western malwa and Berar par kabja kiya
		
		- 17th King tha--> Hala ---> Is bhai ne, Gathasaptasati naam ki book likhi, jise, 'Sattasai' bhi kahte hain---> 700 verses in Prakrit language. 
		
	
		- Greatest Ruler : Gautamiputra Satakarni : 106 se 130 AD ke beech, 24 saal rule kiya. 
		
				- Isi bhai ki achievements- Nasik Inscriptions par hai. 
				
				- Is bhai ne, pure Deccan par kabja kar liya. 
				
				- Malwa ke Nagapana ruler ko apna lund chuswaya. 
				
				- Brahmanism ko patronize kiya, lekin, Buddhists ko bhi donate kiye. 
				
		
		- Ye log, Naval power bhi the, Kyunki Gautamiputra ke bete, Vashishtaputra Pulamayi ke coins par, ships bane hue the. 
		
		- Last Great ruler ---> Yajna Sri Satakarni
		
		
		Admin and Economy : 
		
			1) Karshapanas naam ke Silver coins. 
			
			2) Deccan me, Kalyani naam ki port city. 
			
			3) East COst me, Gandakasela and Ganjam naam ke seaports			
			
			
			
	Bactrian Invasion : 
	
		1) Bactria and Partha,3rd CE BC me, Syrian Empire se alag ho gaye the. 
		
		2) Demetrius naam ke Greek ruler of Bactria ne, Punjab and Afghansitan par kabja kar liya. 
		
		3) Fir is bhai ne, Appolodotus and Menander ko bheja aage ke conquest karne keliye. 
		
		4) Menandar bhai ne,MAthura tak sab capture kar liya.    Appolo ne Sindh. 
		
		5) Menandar patliputra par bhi kabja karna chahta tha, lekin, Pussymitra sunga ke pote, Vasumitra ne, iske mu me apna lemon daal diya. 
		
		6) Menandar ko hi Milind kahte the, or iska capital tha, Sialkot. 
		
		7) Is bhai ka, Nagasena se sawal jawab ---> "Milindpanho".
		
		
	
	Sakas : 
	
		1) In saalo ne, Bactria or Partha par kabja kar liya, waha ke Greeks ko lemon chuswake. 
		
		2) Fir in salo ne, NW India par bhi kabja kar liya. 
		
		3) 2 groups the : Northern Satraps : Ruling from Taxila      ,    Western Satraps : Ruling from Maharashtra
		
		4) Founder of Saka Ruler tha--> Maues in 1st BC ---> Iska beta tha, Azes I ---> Founder of Vikram Era.
		
		5) Taxila wale Saka logo ko, Parthians ne lemon chuswa diya. 
		
		
		
	
	Kushans : 
	
		1) Yuchi Tribe ki ek branch thi---> Central ASia se aaye the. 
		
		2) Pehle inhone Bactria se, Shaka logo ko bhagaya.   Baad me, Kabul valley and Gandhara region par kabja kiya. 
		
		3) Founder of Kushan Dynasty : Kujula Kadphises or Kadphises I.  ---> Occupied Kabul valley and issued coins in his name. 
		
		4) Iska beta tha--> Kadphises II ---> Ye bhai Mathura tak aa gaya tha.  ---> Ye shiva bhakt tha. 
		
		5) Last important ruler : Vasudeva ---> Shiva Bhakt
	
			Kanishka (78 to 120 AD)
			
				1) Founder of Saka Era, from 78 AD
				
				2) Is bhai ne, Magadh par kabja kar liya.   Kalhana ke hisab se, isne Kashmir par bhi kabja kar liya tha.
				
				3) Lekin, ek Chinese general, Pancho ne iske Pan cho di. 
				
				4) Iski capital thi- --.> peshawar	
	
				5) Iske time me Mahayan Buddhism start hua--> Image worship of buddha. 
				
				6) Patronised : Vasumitra,  Asvagosh and Nagarjuna
				
				7) Kashmir me, Vasumitra ke under 4th Buddhist council karwai. ----> Authoritive commentary on the tripitakas and Mahayana doctrine was given final shape. 
				
				8) Asvaghosha ne : Buddhacharitra likhi. 
				
				9) Nagarjun, South India se aaya tha. 
				
				10) Charak bhi isi ka lemon chusta tha.
				
				
		
	Gandhara Art : 1st and 2nd CE AD around Peshawar in NW India. 
	
		- Indo-Greek rulers ke time orginate hua, lekin, Sakas and Kushans ne patronize kiya. 
		
		- Buddha ke sculptures banaye in various shapes and sizes. 
		
		- Describes his Birth, renunciation and his preaching. 
		
		- Muscles, Moustache and curly hair jaise minute features par minute attention diya gaya hai. 
		
		- Thick drapery hai, with bold fold lines. 
		
		- Rich carving, Elaborate Ornamentationa and Symbolic expressions. 
		
		- Main theme : Mahayan Buddhism
		
		
		
	Mathura School : Indigenous 
	
		- Buddha ke face par spiritual feelings hain, jo Gandhara School me absent thi. 
		
		- Siva- Parvati    and    Vishnu-Lakshmi    ki bhi images carve out ki gai hain. 
		
		- Female Yakshinis				
	
	
	
	
	Sangam : a.k.a Muchchangam ---> 3 in total.  : 3rd BC to 3rd AD
	
		1) Pandyas ke under. 
		
		2) 1st hua tha Madurai me : Gods and Legends ne attend kiya tha. ---> Therefor no lit work available
		
		3) 2nd Sangam : Held in Kapadapuram : Bas Tolkappiyam bacha hai. 
		
		4) 3rd Sangam : Madurai : Isme bohot saare works compile hue---> Used to reconstruct the history of Sangam Age
		
			Sangam Lit : 
			
				1) 2 epics : Silappathigaram (By Elango Adigal) and  Manimegalai (By Sittalai Sattanar)
				
				2)  Tolkappiyam : By Tolkappiyar : Earliest work in tamil lit. ---> Tamil Grammer + Political + Social conditions
				
				3) Ettutogai : Eight Anthologies
				
				4) Pattuppattu : 10 idylls
				
				5) Ye dono divided the into : Aham(love)  and  Puran (Valour)
				
				6) Pathinenkilkanakku : 18 works--> Ethics and Morals
				
				7) In 18 me se sabse important hai --> Tirukkural jise Thiruvalluvar ne likha tha. 
	
	
		Chera : Modern Kerala : Vanavaramban, Vanavan, Kuttuvan, Irumporai and Villavar (Titles)      Symbol = Bow
		
			1) Capital : Vanji --> Important Seats : Tondi and Musiri
			
			2) Inka garland tha : Palmyra flowers
			
			3) 1st AD ke Pugalur Inscriptions : Mentions 3 generations of Chera kings.    Padirruppattu me bhi inke baare me likha gaya hai.
			
			4) Cheran Senguttuvan : 2nd AD---> Chota bhai tha, Elango Adigal---> Author of Silappathigaram.
			
			5) Ye bhai ne, Himalayas me expidition kiya tha---> Defeated many Narthies. 
			
			6) Is bhai ne hi, Pattni Cult introduce kiya tha : Worship of Kannagi as the ideal wife. 
			
			
			
		Chola : South Andhra Pradesh ki Tiruchi district se :  Senni, Valavan and Killi (Titles)     Tiger - Symbol
		
			1) Capital hua karti thi : Uraiyar---> Later shifted to Puhar
			
			2) Karikala ek famous chola kinga tha.    Pattinappalai ---> Portrays early life and military conquests. 
			
			3) Battle of Venni me---> Chera + Pandyas + 11 baki thullo, ko haraya akele. ---> Ye event kai Sangam Poems me mentioned hai. 
			
		
		
		Pandyas : South Tamil Nadu : Thennavar  and  Minavar (Titles),    Carp = Symbol
		
			1) Capital tha, Madurai
			
			2) Neduncheliyan : Is chode ne hi, Kovalan ko execute kiya tha, jiske wajah se, Kannagi ne, Madurai jala diya tha. 
			
			3) "Maduraikkanji" ---> Mangudi maruthanar ne likhi thi---> Describes the socio-economic conditions of the Pandya Country---> Including the flourishing seaport of Korkai. 
			
			4) Last famous king : Peruvaludhi 
			
			5) Kalabhras ki invasion se inki maa chud gai thi.
					
	
	
		Terms : 
		
			1) Imperial Court : Avai
			
			2) Ministers : Amaichar
			
			3) Priests : Anthanar
			
			4) Military commander : Senapathi
			
			5) Envoys : Thuthar
			
			6) Spies : Orrar			
			
	
	
	5 fold divisions of land : Tolkappiyam me mentioned hai
	
		1) Kurinju : Murugan : Hunting and Honey Collection : Hilly Tracks
		
		2) Mullai : Mayon(Vishnu) : Cattle rearing and Dairy : Pastoral
		
		3) Marudam : Indira : Agriculture : Khet
		
		4) Neydal : Varunan : Fishing and Salt mfg : COastal
		
		5) Palai : Korravai : Robbery : Desert
		
		
	
	4 casts bhi mentined hai isi book me : 
	
		1) Arasar : Ruling class
		
		2) Anthanar : Polity and Religion
		
		3) Vanigars : Trade and COmmerce
		
		4) Vellalas : Agriculturists
		
		
	Tribal Groups : Parathavar,  Panar,   Eyinar,  Kadambar, Maravar  and  Pulaiyar
	
	
	Primitive Tribes : Thodas,  Irulas,  Nagas   and  Vedars
	
	
	Nadu kal : Hero Stone-Worship
	
	
	Panar  and  Viraliyar : Singing Bards
	
	
	Kanigaiyar : Dancers
	
	
	Koothu : Most popular entertainment of the people
	
	
	Women Poets : Avvaiyar,  Nachchellaiyar  and  Kakkaipadiniyar
	
			
			
		
	Uraiyar me cotton clothes bante the, jinki west me bhi kaafi demand thi. 
	
	
	Port Cities : Tondi,  Musiri,  Korkai,  Arikkamedu   and   Marakkanam
	
	
	
	
	
	
	Gupta Empire : 
	
		1) Purans me mentioned hai inki Genealogy. 
		
		2) Vishakhadatta ke, Devichandraguptam  and  Mudhrakshasam
		
		3) Fa-Hien aaya tha Chandragupta II ke time. 
		
		4) Meherauli Iron Pillar Inscription : CP -I
		
		5) Allahbad Pillar Inscription: Samundargupta ---> Ye Ashokan Pillar par hi engraved hai. ---> Classic Sanskrit in Nagari script.   Ise, Harisena ne likha tha. 
		
		6) Founder = Sri Gupta---> Iska beta- Ghatotkacha 
		
		
	
	Chandragupta I : Maharajadhiraja 320 - 330 AD 
	
		- Licchavis ki beti chodi, jiska naam tha Kumardevi.  
		
		- Meherauli Iron Pillar inscription
		
		- Iska Accession hua tha, 320 AD me---> Considered to be the founder of Gupta Era
		
		
	
	
	SamundraGupta : 330 to 380 AD
	
		- Greatest ruler. 
		
		- Allahbad pillar inscription : North India ---> Dakshinapatha---> North India dobara
		
		- Is bhai ne, Asvamedha dobara se shuru kiya. 
		
		- Isi ko 'Indian Napolean' kehte hain.
		
		- Ye sala Kaviraj bhi tha.   Veena bhi bajata tha.     Greenarmy isi ke court me tha. 
	
		- Ye vaishnavite tha, lekin, Buddhist Scholor, Vasubandu ko patronize kiya
		
		
		
	CP-II (380 to 415 AD)
	
		- Samudra ka beta. 
		
		- Kuberanaga ki choot mari. 
		
		- Vakatak Prince, Rudrasena II ko apni beti chodne ko di---> Prabhavati 
		
		- Rudrasimha III naam ke Saka ko koda kiya ---> Becama Shaka-Hari. ---> Bhai khudko, Vikramaditya bulane laga. 
		
		- Iske time me, Ujjain became an important city 
		
		- Bhai ne, Sindh cross karke, Bactria par kabja kiya. ---> Kushan ruler ko koda kiya. 
		
		- Isi ke time me, Fa-Hien ne visit kiya. ---> Ye bhai 9 saal ruka India me, or Land ke through aaya tha, or Sea ke through wapas gaya. 
		
		- Patronized Kalidas
		
		- Isi ke time period ko--> Golden Age of India kehte hain. 
		
		
	
	
	CP-II ka beta tha : Kumargupta
	
		- Is bhai ne hi, Nalanda Universit shuru ki thi. 										
		
		- Lekin, Pushyamitras naam ki wealthy tribe ne iski gand mar di.  Jiske baad, Huns ke invasion bhi shuru ho gaye the. 
		
	
	Kumargupta ka beta tha==> Skandagupta---> Isne, Huns ko haraya, lekin, Empire ki maa chud chuki thi. 
	
	
	
	Gupta Terminologies : 
	
		1) Min. of Foreign Affairs : Sandivigraha
		
		2) Province ke admin ke liye, Officials ki ek class thi : Kumaramatyas and Ayuktas. 
		
		3) Gupta Provinces ko Bhuktis kehte the---> Under, "Upariks". --> ye sale generally prince log hote the. 
		
		4) Bhuktis, subdivided thi, into --> Vishyas or DIstricts ---> Inhe Vishyapatis govern karta tha. 
		
		5) City admin ke liye the---> Nagar Shrestis
		
		6) Villages were under the control of --> Gramikas
		
		
	Gupta Admin : 
	
		1) Mild and benevolent
		
		2) Logo ki movement par koi restrictions nahi thi. ---> State intefere bhi nahi karti thi logo ki life me. 
		
		3) Punishments bhi jyada severe nahi thi. 
		
		4) Koi spy system bhi nahi tha.  
		
		5) lOGO KE PASS khub paisa tha, isliye, crime bhi nahi tha. 
		
		
		
	- Caste system rigid ho gaya. 
	
	- Untouchability shuru ho gayi.
	
	- Swayamwar ki practice giveup kar di gayi---> Manusmriti kahti thi child marriage karwao.
	
	- Purans bhi isi period me compose ki gayi. 
	
	- Mahabharatta and Ramayan ke final versions bhi yahi aaye. 
	
	- Isi period me, Valabhi me Jain council organise hui---> Swetambars ki jain canon likhi gayi. 
	
	- Both Nagara and Dravidian style of architecture started. ---> Lekin, Huns ki invasion ki wajah se sab kuch gayab ho chuka hai. 
	
		1) Jhansi ke pass --> Temple at Deogarh
		
		2) Allahbad ke pass --> Temple at Garhwas
		
		3) Sarnath me Budda statue-> Unique peace of Gupta Art
		
		4) Bhitari monolithic pillar of Skandagupta
		
		5) Sultangaj Buddha
		
		6) Delhi Iron pillar
		
		7) Bagh Caves me Gupta paintings, near Gwalior
	
	- Inpar, Gandhara style ka koi influence nahi hai.
	
	
	
	- Gupta Lit : 
	
		1) Samundragupta ke pass GreenArmy tha
		
		2) CP-II ke pass---> Navratn the. --> Most famous = Kalidas
		
		3) Kalidas ne likhe : 
		
			- Shakuntala --> Sanskrit Drama --- > Most famous work
			
			- Malavikanimitra  and  Vikramorvasiya ---> 2 other plays
			
			- Raghuvamsa and Kumarsambhava ---> 2 epics
			
			- Ritusamhara  and  Meghaduta ---> 2 lyrics
			
		
		4) Vishakhadatta ne likhe : 
		
			- Mudrarakshasa
			
			- Devichandraguptam
			
		
		5) Sudraka ---> Poet
		
			- Mrichchakatika
			
		
		6) Bharavi ---> Isne likhi--> Kritarjuniya---> Conflict between Shiva and Arjun
		
		7) Dandin ---> Author of Kavyadarsa  and  Dasakumaracharita
		
		8) Shubhandu ne likha---> Vasavadatta
		
		9) Vishnusharma ===> Panchatantra
		
		10) Amarasimha ---> Buddhist Author--- 'Amarakosa'. 	
		
		
		
		
	Science and Tech : 
	
		1) Aryabhatta -
		
			- Wrote Aryabhatiya in 499 AD --> Maths and Astronomy
			
			- Describes, solar and lunar eclipses
			
			- Earth was spherical in shape and rotates on its own axis --> 1st to do so. 
			
			- Lekin, Varhamihira and Brahmagupta jaise baad ke bhadwo ne bola ki Aryabhatta to bhaat jaati ka hai, isliye iski kisi bhi baat par yakeen mat karna. 
			
			
		
		2) Varahamihira : 	 
		
			- Wrote, 'Pancha Siddhantika' ---> 5 astronomical systems
			
				- Great authority on astrology
				
			- Also wrote, 'Brihadsamhita' ===> Great work in Sanskrit literature
			
				- Astronomy, Weather, Animals, marriage etc
				
			- Also wrote, 'Brihadjataka'---> Standard work on Astrology. 
			
			
		3) Vagbhata ---> Medicine guy. 
		
			- Wrote 'Ashtangasamgraha' --> "Summary of the 8 branches of medicine". 
			
			
			
			
			
			
			
			
			
	Harshavardhana (606 to 647 AD)
	
		1) North India me bada sa kingdom banaya. 
		
		2) Hiuen Tsang + Bana wali, Hashcharita .
		
		3) Harsha ke likhe Drame : Ratnavali,   Naganda  and  Priyardarsika
		
		4) Madhuben plate inscription and Sonpat inscription ---> Chronology of Harsha
		
		5) Banskhera inscription --> Signature of harsha
		
		
		- Harsha ka baap, Pushyabhuti---> Guptas ka feudatories tha. ----> Ye saale apne aap ko, 'Vardhans' kehte the. 
		
		- Huns ki invasion ke baad ye saale independent ho gaye. 
		
		- Inki capital thi, Thaneswar.   Lekin, Harsha ne, Kanauj kar di baad me. 
		
		- Harsha ki , Pulakesin II naam ke Chalukyan king ne, Narmada River par, lakho logo ke saamen, Ass fuck kiya tha----> "Asshole Inscription". 
		
		- 'Nilopitu' ---> Archives of Harsha's period. 
		
		- Harsha ke period me==> Sharp economic decline, as compared to Gupta period===> 'led to rise of self-sufficient villages'. 
		
		- Banabhatta--> Cort poet--> 'Harscharita'  and   'Kadambari'. 
		
		- Harsha ke court me ye log bhi the : Barthrihari (Poet, philosopher)    and    Matanga Divakara
		
		- Harsha ne khud likhi : Ratnavali,  Priyadarsika  and  Nagananda
		
		- Harsha ne, Nalanda Uni ko bhi patronize kiya. 
		
		
		
		Ye saare Nalanda Uni ke heads the : 1) Dinganga  2) Dharmapa(Kanchi wale)  3) Sthiramati  4) Silabadhra
		
			- No fee
			
			- Means of Instruction - Sanskrit
			
			
	
	
	
			
	Pallavas Dobara : 
	
		1) Mahendravarman I (600 AD)
		
			- Isne, Chalukyas se lode lene shuru kiye the. 
			
			- Pullakesin II ne aake, kabja kar liya. 
			
			- Ye bhai pehle jain hua karta tha, lekin, baad me, Appar naam ke Saiva saint ki baato me aa gaya. 
			
			- Is bhai ko Cave temples ka bohot shok tha.   
			
			- 'Vichitra-Chitta' yahi bhai hai---> Mandagappattu inscription ----> Constructed temples without use of bricks, Timber, Metal and Mortar
			
			- Sanskrit work : Mattavilasa Prahasanam
			
			
		
		2) Narasimhavarman I (630 AD)
		
			- Ise, Mamalla bhi kehte the. 
			
			- Isne, Pulakein II ko lulla de diya tha, at the Battle of Manimangalam near Kanchi. 
			
			- Iski army ne bhi, Vatapi ki maa chod di. ----> " He became Vatapikonda ". 
			
			- Is chode ne, Sri-Lanka me naval expidtion bheja, or apen dost, Manavarma ko throne par baithaya. 
			
			- Hiuen Tsang bhi Kanchipuram aaya tha isi time. 
			
				"Ghatikas of Kanchi" --> Great centres of learning. 
				
			
			- Isi bhai ne, Mamallpuram found kiya tha---> waha ke monolithic raths isi ke kaand hain. 
			
			
		
		3) Narsimhavarman II or Rajasimha(695 AD)
		
			- Shore Temple at Mamallapuram 
			
			- Kailasnath Temple at Kanchipuram
			
			- Dandin naam ka famous Sanskrit scholor isika lemon chusta tha. 
			
			
		
		Pallava ruler, 9th CE tak chala.  Last Pallava Ruler tha---> "Aparajita" ---> Chola king, Aditya I ne ise parajit kar, Pallava Dynasty ko ram ram bol diya. 
		
		
		
		Pallav Admin : 
		
			- State was divided into--> Kottams ---> Ise, Admin karta tha ek officer, jise King appoint karta tha. 
			
			- Devadhana ==> Grants to temples
								---> In dono par, koi tax nahi lagta tha. 
			- Brahmadeya ==> Grants to Brahmans
			
			- Ye logo ne, bohot saare irrigation tanks banwaye. 				
		
			- Sabhas naam ki village assemblies hoti thi. 
			
			
		- Pallavas ke time, Brahmanism ka rise hua---> Buddhism and Jainisms ka decline hua. 
		
		- Saiva Nayanmar and Vaishnava Alwars ---> Growth of Saivism and Vaishnavism ---> Bhakti Movement
		
			
			
		- Kanchi--> Ancient Centre of learning 
		
		- Ghatikas was popular---> Attracted students from all parts of India and Abroad. 
		
			- Kadamba Dynasty ke founder Mayursarman ne, Kanchi me hi Ved padhe the
			
		
	
	Pallava Art and Architecture : 
	
		- Introduced the art of excavating temples from the rock. 
		
		- Dravidian style of temple architecture began with pallava rule. 
		
		- Pehle, Cave temples ---> Fir aaye, Monolithic Rathas ---> Fir aaye, Structural Temples
		
		- Mahendravarman ne, Rock-Cut temples shuru kiye the
		
		- Narsimhavarman ne, 2nd stage me---> Monolithic Rathas and Mandaps banwaye the, Mamallapuram me. 
		
			- 5 rathas --> Panchapanadava Rathas ---> Signifies, 5 different styles of temple architecture
			
		- 3rd Stage me---> Rajasimga--> Introduced Structural Temples---> built using, soft sand rocks
		
			- Kaislashnath Temple at Kanchi---> Greatest architectural master piece of Pallava Art
			
			- Shore Temple at Mamallapuram	
			
			
		- 4th and Last stage : Structural Temples
		
			- Vaikundaperumal Temple 
			
			- Muktheeswara Temple
			
			- Matagenswara Temples at Kanchipuram
			
		
		- Open Art Gallery at the Shore Temple : 
		
			- Descent of Ganga
			 			---> 'Fresco Painting in stone'
			- Penance of arjuna 	
			
			- Ascetic cat
			
		
		- Sittannavasal Paintings
		
		- Mahendravarman I ne, 'Dakshinchitra' naam ki commentary likhi
			
			
			
	
	Chalukyas(543 to 755 AD)
	
		1) Western Chalukya log ne, Deccan par 200 saal rule kiya, jiske baad,Rashtrakutas powerful ho gaye. 
		
		2) Pulakesin I founder tha is dynasty ka. 
		
		3) Vatapi a.k.a Badami iski capital thi. 
		
		
	
			Pulakesin II (608 AD to 642 AD)
			
				1) Most important ruler. 
				
				2) Asshole inscription isi bhai ki hai :  Gangas of Mysore se lada,  and Kadambas of Banavasi
				
				3) Narmada river par, Harsha ko lulakesin kiya. 
				
				4) Pallavas se pehle ladai me jeet mili. 
				
				5) Lekin Narasimhavarman I ne Kanchi ke pass, pula bhai gand me apna lulla de diya. 
				
				6) Hieun Tsang ne visit kiya bhai ke kingdom ko. 
				
				7) Iska beta tha, Vikramaditya, jisne fir baap ki haar ka badla liya,Kanchi raid karke. 
				
			
		Aakhri Chalukya tha, Kirtivarman, jisi Danti se gaand kutar di thi, Dantidurga ne---> Founder of Rashtrakuta Dynasty. 		
	
	
	
	
	- Chalukyan Admin, highly centralised---> Unlike Pallavas and CHolas. 
	
	- Koi village autonomy nahi thi 
	
	- Great maritime power-=---> Pula I ke pass 100 ships thi. 
	
	- Ravikirti--> Ye Pula II ka court poet tha, isi ne hi Asshole inscription compose ki thi, or ye sala ek Jain tha. 
	
	
	- Chalukya Art and Craft : 
	
		1) Vesara style develop kiya, LEkin this style reached its zenith under Rashtrakutas and Hoysalas
		
		2) Aihole, Baddami and Pattadakal me, Chalukyas ke structural temples exist karte hain. 
		
		3) Ajanta, Ellora and Nasik me inke Cave temples milte hain.
		
		4) Reception given to Persian Embassy by Pula II is depicted in a painting at Ajantha. 
		
		5) Aihole ke 4 most important Temples : 
		
			i) Ladh Khan Temple : Low, flat-roofed structure consisting of a pillared hall. 
			
			ii) Durga tmeple --> Resembles a buddha Chaitya
			
			iii) Huchimalligudi Temple
			
			iv) Jain temple at Meguti.  	
	

		6) Badami ke 4 most important temples : Was and Pillared halls are adorned by beautiful images of Gods and Human Beings. 
		
			i) Mukhteeswara Temple
			
			ii) Melagutti Sivalaya
	
		
		7) Ye upar wale to saare 1st Stage ke hain. 
			
			2nd Stage is represented by ---> Temples at Pattadakal. 
			
				1) Papanatha Temple -> In Northern Style. 
				
				2) Sangamesvara Temple and Virupakhsa Temple ---> In Dravidian Style. 
				
						- Virupaksha temple--> Ye,Kailasnath Temple ke style me bana hai, at Kanchipuram. 
						
									- Ise, Vikramaditya II ki rani ne banwaya tha, apni chut se paise kamake. 
									
									
	
	
	Rashtrakutas (755 to 975 AD)
	
		1) Ye kannada log the.    Dantidurga founder tha-->Captured Malwa from Gurjars
		
		2) Isne fir, Chalukya kingdom annex kar liya, Kirtivarman II ko harake.----> Became a paramount power in Deccan.
		
		3) Krishna I --> Iska successor--> Defeated Gangas aned Eastern Chalukyas of Vengi. 
		
			- Isi bhai ne, Ellora me, monolithic Roch-cut, Kailasa Temple banwaya tha. 
			
		
		4) Next important king---> Govinda III. 
		
		5) Iske baad--> Amoghvarsha (815 to 880 AD) ---> Ruled for 64 years. 
		
			- Ye bhai ek Jain tha. 
			
			- Ye bhai ne, Kannada work, Kavirajamarga likha. 
			
			- Isne Rashtrakutas ka capital bhi banwaya---> City of Malkhed or Manyakheda
			
		
		6) Iske baad aaya Krishna III (936 to 968 AD) ---> Famous for his expiditions. 
		
			- Isne, Cholas ke against march karke, unhe Takkolam me haraya. 
			
			- Isne fir, Tanjore par kabja kar liya. 
			
			- Betichod yaha bhi nahi ruka, or Rameswaram tak chala gaya. 
			
			- Isne, Rameswaram me, Krishnewara Temple banwaya. 
			
			
		Rashtrakuta admin 
		
			1) Rashtras naam ke provinces me empire divided tha, under the control of rashtrapatis
			
			2) Jo further divided the, into vishayas or districts, governed by vishayapatis
			
			3) Next Subdivision--> Bhukti, consisting of 50 to 70 villages, under the  control of Bhogapatis. 
			
				- In officers ko , central Goc directly appoint karti thi. 
			
			4) Village admin--> Carried out by village headman.    Village assembly also played a significant role. 
											
	
		- Salatogi(in Bijapur) me ek collage bhi hota tha. 
		
		- 1/3rd population jain thi. 
	
		- Ye sanskrit lit ko patronize karte the. 
			
			1) Trivikrama ne likha--> Nalachampu
			
			2) Halayudha ne likha --> Kavirahasya (Krishna III ke time)
			
			3) Amoghvarsha I ke jain teacher, Jinasena===> COmposed --> Parsvabhudaya--> Biography of Parsva in verse. 
			
			4) Gunabhadra ne likha---> Adipurana ---> Life story of various jain saints. 
			
			5) Sakatayan ne, grammer work likha--> Amogavritti. 
			
			6) Viracharya ---> Great maths guy===> Wrote, Ganitasaram
			
			
			
		Isi period me, Kannada lit ki shuruat hui. 
					
		Kavirajmaraga---> 1st poetic work in Kannada. 
		
		Pampa==> Greatest kannada poet---> Wrote 'Vikramsenavijay'. 
		
		Ponna--> Also a poet, wrote, Santipurana. 		
		
		
	Rashtrakuta Art : 
	
		1) Ellora and Elephanta me milta hai. 
		
		2) Kailasa temple at Ellora----> Krishna I ke time excavate kiya tha. 
		
		3) Ellephants ke sculptures, ellora jaise hi hain, isliye, shayad dono ko ek hi bande ne banaya hoga. 
		
			- Most important figure --> Trimurti	
		
		
		
		
		
		
	Cholas : Sangam period ke baad inki maa chud gayi thi, and they became feudatories in Uraiyur. 
	
		- Lekin, 9th CE me ye firse prominence me aa gaye. 
		
		- Capital = Tanjore.     Sri-Lanka and Malay Peninsula me bhi inka lemon chuste the log----> So they are known as Imperial Chola. 
		
		- Founder - Vijayalaya --> Captured Tanjore from Muttariyars in 815 AD---> Built a temple for Durga. 
		
		- Iske bete ne hi, Aparajita ko defeat karke, Tondaimandalam ko annex kiya tha, Pallavas se. 
		
		- Parantaka I---> Defeated Pandyas and ruler of Ceylon--> But Rashtrakutas ne, Battle of Takkolam me iske Pran nikal diye. 
		
			Is bhai ko mandiro ka bada shok tha. 
			
			Provided Vimana of famous Nataraja temple at Chidambaram with a golden roof. 	
		
		- Uttiramerur Inscriptions --> Detailed account of village admin under Cholas.
		
		
		
		Rajaraja I (985 to 1014 AD)
		
			1) Is bhai or iske bete, ke under, CHola power Reached its highest point of glory. 
			
			2) Chera ruler, Bhaskararavivarman ko, Kandalursalai ki naval battle me haraya. 
			
			3) Mysore region ko conquer kiya 
			
			4) Sri-Lanka ko invade kiya, iske bete Rajendra I ne. 
			
			5) Western Chalukyas of Kalyani ki maa chodi.    Raichur doab par kabja kiya.  Tungabhadra river tak, Chola power was extended. 	
		
			6) Telugu CHodas ko bhi haraya, or Vengi thore ko, Saktivarman and Vimaladitya ko lotaya.  Vimal ko apni beti, Kundavai bhi di chodne ko. 
			
			7) Last expidition to maldive Islands. 
			
			
				- Bhai was a devout follower of Saivism. 
				
				- COmpleted the construction of Rajarajeswara Temple   or  Brihadeeswara Temple at Tanjore in 1010 AD. 
				
				- Nagapattinam me ek buddhist monastery bhi banwai. 
				
				
			
		Rajendra I (1012 - 1044 AD)
		
			1) Pure Sri-Lanka par kabja kar liya.
			
			2) Jayasimha II naam ke western Chalukya king ko choda---> Tungabhadra river was recognised as a boundry between Cholas and Chalukyas. 
			
			3) North India me military expidition. ---> Crossed Ganga and Defeated Mahipal I of Bengal. 
			
				- Founded Gangaikondacholapuram  and  constructed famous Rajesvaram Temple their. 
				
				- Cholagangam naam ka bada sa irrigation tank bhi banwaya waha pe. 
				
			
			4) Naval expedition to Sri Vijaya
			
			5) Chidambaram me Lord nataraj temple ko donation diye. 
			
			6) Vaishanava and Buddhist sects ke towards bhai tolerant tha.
