		Rajendra III--> Last Chola king ---> Defeated by Jatavarman Sundarpandya II ---> CHola country was absorbed into the Pandya Empire. 
		
		
		
		Central Gov : 
		
			1) Various officials such as : Perundanam   and   Sirudanam
			
			2) Land Revenue department ---> Puravuvarithinaikkalam
			
			3) Sab land ko carefully survey and classify kiya gaya tha, for assessment of Revenue. 
			
			4) Residential portion of Village was called 'Ur Nattam'. 
			
			5) Velaikkarar : Persenol security troops of king. 
			
			6) Military Cantonments : Kadagams
			
		
		
		Chola empire---> Divided into--> Mandalams ----> Further divided into, Valanadus and Nadus. 
		
		- In each Nadu---> Number of Autonomous villages. 
		
		- Mandalam ka charge, royal princes ko diya gaya tha. 
		
		- Valanadu was under, Pariyanattar    and    Nadu was under, nattar. 
		
		- Town was known as nagaram---> Under the admin of a council called nagarattar. 
		
		
			Villag assemblies : Village autonomy with sabhas and their committees. 
			
			
		Qualifications to become a ward member : 
		
			1) Ownership of at least 1/4th Veli of land. 
			
			2) Own Residence
			
			3) 30 se 70 ke beech ki age. 
			
			4) Vedas ki knowledge. 
			
			
		Kudavolai system : election of ward members. 
		
		Devadasi system inhe ke time emerge hua. 
		
		'Peruvazhis' or Trunk Roads. 
		
		
			Tamil Lit : 
			
				1) Thiruthakkadevar ne Sivakasintamani likhi. 
				
				2) Kundalakesi bhi isi time likhi gayi. 
				
				3) Kamban ne Ramayan likhi. 
				
				4) Periyapuranam or  Tiruttondarpuranam  by Sekkilar
				
				5) Ottakuthar ne, Moovarula likhi-0---> Describe the life of 3 chola kings. 
				
				6) Pugalendi ne, Nalavenba likhi. 
				
				7) Tamil Grammer par, Kalladanar ne, Kalladam likhi. 
				
				8) Buddhamitra ne, Virasoliyam likhi.
				
				
				
	Ye saare Buddhist Monks, China Gaye the : 1) Gunabhadra    2) Vajrabothi   3) Dharmadeva    4) Dharmagupta
	
	
	5th CE me, Buddha Ghosha visited Sri Lanka and Consolidated there the Hinayan Buddhism. 
	
		- Famous paintings of Sigiriya were modeled on Ajantha Paintings. 
		
		
	East Asia : Suvarnabhumi
	
		- Indians began to colonize it, since the Gupta era
		
		- Further encouraged by Pallavas. 					
		
		
		
										Medieval India
										
										
		
		- Harsha ke marne ke 500 saal baad bhi, North India me koi unity nahi thi. 
		
		- 8th CE me Kashmir Kingdom dominant tha. 
		
		- Baad me, Bengal ke Pala khade ho gaye. 
		
		- Unke baad, Pratihar log.
		
		- BC, 10th CE me, Rashtrakutas of Deccan tried to extend their power in North India  but failed in their attempt. 
		
		
		
	Rajputs : 
	
		1) Gurjar-Pratihars--> Earliest .     1st ruler---> Harishchandra---> Capital at Bhinmal. 
		
		2) Chauhans ruled ajmer. ---> Vigraharaj was their most important king---> Occupied Delhi. 
		
		3) Paramars bhi thi----> Most important king--> Bhoja. 
		
		
		
		
	Arabs : COnquest of Sindh in 712 AD.		
	
		- Under Qasim---> COmmander of Umayyad Kingdom. 
		
		- Started Indo-Arabic Contacts. 
		
		
	Turks : 9th CE tak, Abbasid Caliphate declined  and  Turks established independent kingdoms. 
	
		- Alptigin ----> Iski capital thi, Ghazni me----> Captured Peshawar. 
		
		- Iska beta tha, Mahmud of Ghazni (997 to 1030)
		
			1024 AD me, Solanki Raja ko harake, Somanath ko loota. 	
			
			Firdausi isi ka court poet tha. ---> Author of Shah-Namah. 
			
			Alberuni bhi isi ke court me ruka tha---> Kitab-i-Hind likhi.
			
			Hindu-Shahi kingdom--> Jo front pe the--> Ko haraya---->Allowed the Afghans and Turks,  to invade India in future. 
			
		
	Mulla Ghori : 
	
		1) Ghazni par kabja kiya. 
		
		2) 1175 me Multan capture kiya. 
		
		3) 1186 tak, pura punjab and Sindh capture kar chuka tha. 
		
		4) 1191 me, Tarain me har gaya by PC. 
		
		5) 1192 me, 2nd Tarain me jeet gaya. 
		
		6) 2-3 saal baad, Jaychandra ko, battle of Chandawar me haraya.
		
		
		
	Delhi Sultanate : 1206 AD to 1526 AD	
	
		1) Slave Dynasty : a.k.a Mamluk Dynasty.    1209 se 1290 tak.
		
			- Qutubi Dynasty ---> By Aibak
			
			
				- Isne Delhi par kabja karke, Ghori ko FUck off bol diya. 
				
				- Patronized, Hasan Nizami
				
				- Sufi Saint Khwaja Qutubuddin Bakhtiyar ke naam pe kuch banwaya, mujhe exactly nahi pata kya, but kya farq padta hai BC, exam me thodi puchenge. 
				
				- Ye lola, Polo khelte hue mar gaya tha. BC, Polo khelte hue. 
				
				- Iske, bete Aram Baksh ko, Iltutmish ne lemon chuswa dia.
				
				
				
			
			- Ilbari Dynasty ---> By Iltutmish
			
				
				- Ye Aibak ka Son-in-law tha.    Aibak ne ise, Gwalior ka Iqtadar appoint kiya tha. 
				
				- Isne, Capital ko Lahore se Delhi Shift kiya. 
				
				- Isi time par, Chengiz khan bhi josh me aa gaya tha.  Kwarizam ka ruler, Jalaluddin Mangabarni, Iske pass asylum mangne aaya tha, lekin iski gand fat gayi . 
				
				- Received 'Mansur' --> letter of recognition from Abbasid Caliph in 1229. ---> Became legal sovereign ruler of India. Gajab BC. 
				
				- Apni beti, Raziya ko ruler nominate karke mar gaya.  ---> Hereditary succession of Delhi Sultanate was initiated by Iltutmish. 
				
				- Qutb Minar ki construction puri ki===> Tallest stone tower in India. 
				
				- Introduced Arabic Coinage in Indi=----> Silver Tanka, became standard coin in Medieval India. ----> Remained basis for Modern Rupee. 
				
				
				
		Raziya (1236 to 1240 AD)
		
			- Bhatinda ke Governor, Altunia ne revolt kar diya 1240 me.    Raziya ke baby, Yakuth ko mar ke, Raziya ko prisoner bana liya. 
			
			- Lekin, Raziya randibaaz nikli or usne, Altunia se hi shaadi kar li.  Or Delhi par chadh dodi. or In dono ki maa chud gayi. 
			
			- 1246 me, Balban ne, Ilututmish ke ek bete, Nasiruddin Mahmud ko Sultan banwa diya. 
			
		
			
	 2nd Ilbari -------> By Balban (1246 to 1287 AD)
			
			
		- Ise, Ulugh Khan bhi kehte the. ---> Isne, apni beti de di, Sultan ko chodne ke liye. 
		
		- 1266 me Nasiruddin expire ho gaya, to Balban bola ki abse main Sultan. 
		
		- Iske hisab se, Sultan was God's Shaddow on Earth.    Isne, prostration introduce kiya + Kissing the feet of sultan .
		
		- Isne, Nauroz bhi introduce kiya. 
		
		- Nobels ki activity monitor karne ke liye, Spies appoinnt kiye. 
		
		- Isne apne empire ko exapnd karne se jyada, internal matters sudharne par dhyan diya. 
		
		- Eastablished a separate, military Department ---> Diwan-i-arz  and  reorganized the army. 
		
		- 1279 me, Mongols ne iske bete ki gardan utar di.     
		
		- 1287 me Balban bhi expire ho gaya.  Iske marne ke baad, iske ek pote ko Sultan banaya gaya, lekin, 4 saal baad hi, Jalaluddin Khalji ne Throne capture kar liya, in 1290. 
		
	
	
	Khalji Dynasty (1290 - 1320)
	
		- Jalaluddin loda, 70 saal ka tha, jab power me aaya. 
		
		- Iske, nephew Alauddin Khalji ne, 1296 me iski gardan utar di. 
		
		
			Alauddin Khalji (1296 - 1316 AD)
			
				- 4 ordinances pass kiye : i) Nobels ki properties cheen li ii) Spy system reorganize kiya    iii) Daru band      iv)Sultan ki permission ke bina saare festivals band kar diye. 
				
				- In harsh measures ki wajah se, iske reign me koi rebellion nahi hua. 
				
				- Dagh system intro kiya---> Branding of Horses     and    Huliya --> Descriptive list of soldiers. 
				
				- Market Reforms : Delhi me 4 seperate markets--> 1 for Grains,  1 for Cloths,Sugar, Dry fruits   1 for horses, slaves and cattle    and 4th for Randibaazi. 
		  
		  		- Sare markets,  'Shahna-i-Mandi' ke control me the. 
		  		
		  		- Saare commoditites ke prices fixed the. 
		  		
		  		- Diwani Riyasat naam ka separate department banaya---> Under, 'Naib-i-Riyasat' 
		  		
		  		- Saare merchants ko is department ke under register karwaya. 
		  		
		  		- 'Munhiyans' naam ke secret agents appoint kiye, in markets ke liye. 
		  		
		  		- 1st Sultan to order the measurement of land. ---> Land revenue was collected in Cash.  
		  		
		  		- 6 baar Mongols ne lode liye. 
		  		
		  		- Gujrat, Mewar and Deccan par kabja kiya. 
		  		
		  		- 1303 me, Chittor par kabja kiya. 
		  		
		  		- Patronized Saints like, Amir Khusrau and Amir Hasan. 
		  		
		  		- Alai Darwaza banaya and constructed a new capital at Siri. 
		  		
		  		
		  		
		  		
	Tughaq Dynasty (1320 to 1414)
	
		1) Is Dynasty ka founder tha, Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq
		
		
		2) Muhammad Bin TUghlaq : 1325 to 1351
		
			- Religious matters me bhai kafi tolerant tha. 
			
			- Egypt, CHina, Iran se diplomatic relations the. 	  
			
			- Iske contemporary writers : Isami,  Barani and Ibn Batuta
			
			- Transfer of capital to Devagiri : 1327 AD
			
			- Introduced TOken Currency : 1329-30. --> Kyunki Silver ki shortage thi. 
			
			- Doab region me taxation badha diya, pehle 2 reforms fail ho jane ke baad. 
			
			- Takkavi loans(for cultivation) dena shuru kiye. ---> To buy seeds and extend cultivation.
			
			- Seperate Dept of Agri ---> Diwan-i- Kohi banaya.
			
			- State ke imder. 64 Sq miles me model farm banaya. 
			
				- Hasan Shah ne revolt karke, Madurai Sultanate bana liya. 
				
				- 1336 me, Vijaynagar kingdom bana tha.   
				
				- 1347 me Bhamini kingdom bana tha. 
				
			- Sultan died in 1351. 
			
			
	
	3) Firoz Tughlaq (1351 to 1388 AD)
	
		- Is bhai ne, 'khan-i-jahab' Maqbal ---> Ek Telugu Brahmin convert ko wazir appoint kar diya. 
		
		- Iske time me, Bengal became free from Delhi Sultanate. 
		
		- Nagarkot campaign ke time, bhai ne, Jwalamukhi Temple se 1300 sanskrit manuscripts jabt kiye or unhe, Farsi me translate karwaya. 		
		  		
		- Is chode ne, Iqta system ko revive to kiya hi, saath hi saath use Hereditary bhi bana diya. 
		
		- Non-Muzz logo par, Jizz-oh-yeah impose kar diya. 
		
		- 1st Sultan to impose irrigation tax. 
		
		- Sutlej to Hansi tak, 200km lambi canal khodi. 
		
		- Karkhanas naam ki royal factories shuru ki. 
		
		- Buddo or Widhwao ki madad ke liye, Diwan-i-khairat kholi. 
		
		- Patronized--> Barani and Afif. 
		
		
	
	
	- Iske baad, Delhi Sultanae disintegrate ho gayi. 
	
	- Malwa and Gujrat declared their independence. 
	
	- 1398 me Timur ne invade kiya. ---> Delivered a death blow to Tughlaq Dynasty. 
	
	


		Sayyids(1414 to 1451)
		
			1) Timur ne, Khizir Khan ko Multan ka governor appoint kiya, is chode ne, Delhi capture kar liya, and Started the Sayyid dynasty in 1414. 
			
			2) Iske marne ke baad, iske bete ne bola ki bhai mujhse na ho payega and handed over the throne to Baklol Lodi. 
			
			
		
		Lodis (1451 to 1526)
		
			1) Ye lode, Afghan the.--> Infact 1st Afghan rulers.  
			
			2) Baklol, 1489 me expire ho gaya to, uska beta, Sikandar Lodi aa gaya. ---> Greates of the 3 lodas. 
			
			3) Bengal, Bihar, Punjab me empire extand kiya. 
			
			4) Ye bhi Firoz bhai ki tarah ek Bigot tha, isliye, us time ke woke log, iske againt global campaigns launch kiya karte the. 
			
			5) Iske baad aaya, Ibrahim Lodi. --> Ye saala Arogant banda tha, isliye, Daulat Khan Lodi ne, Babar ko Facebook par invitation bheja, Cancel-Ibrahim party ka. 	  		
		  		
		  		
		  		
		  		
		  		
	In 3 sultans ko, Caliph ne 'Mansur' yani ki permission di thi : 
	
		1) Iltutmish     2) Muhammad Bin Tughlaq     3) Firoz Tughlaq
		
		
			  		
	Sultanate Admin : 
	
		1) Post of Naib---> Most powerful. 
		
		2) Iske baad tha---> Wazir----> Headed the finance department called Diwani-Wizarat. 
		
		3) Military Department tha---> Diwani Ariz ----> Headed by Ariz-i-mumalik. 
		
				- Recruted soldiers   and  Administered the military department. 
				
				- But ye sala koi Commander-in-chief nahi tha. ---> Wo to khud sultan hua karta tha. 
				
		
		4) Military Dept ko sabse pehle, Balban ne setup kiya tha, lekin Aladuddin Khilji ne ise further imporve kiya. 
		
		5) Diwani Rasalat---> Department of Religious Affairs. ----> headed by CHif Sadr ----> Mosques, Tombs and Madrasas ko banane ke liye grants milte the. 
		
		6) Judicial Department ka head---> Chief Qazi.
		
		7) Criminal law was based on rules and regulations made by the Sultans. 
		
		8) Dept of Correspondence---> Diwani Insha
		
		9) Provinces under Delhi Sultanate ---> Iqtas ---> Initially ye nobels ke control me thi. 
		
			- Governors of the provinces ---> Muqtis or Walis ---> Maintian Law and Order and collect Revenue. 
			
			- Provinces ko Shiqs me divide kiya gaya tha--> Jo further divided the, Parganas me. 
			
			- Shiq was under the control of Shiqdar               Parganas ko head karne ke liye, Amil hota tha. 
			
			- Village Headman---> Muqaddam or Chaudhri
			
			- Village accountant ---> Patwari
			
			
		10) Land--> Divided into 3 categories : 
		
			i) Iqta Land : Officials ko unki services ke liye, payments ki jagah, jo land assign kar di jaati thi. 
			
			ii) Khalisa Land : Jo Sultan ke direct control ke under thi ----> Revenue collected was used for maintenance of royal court and royal household
			
			iii) Inam Land : Religious leaders/instis ko jo land assign ki jaati thi. 		  		
		  		
		  		
		 11) Muhammad bin Tughlaq   and   Firoz Tughlaq ---> In dono ne, Agri productivity increase karne ki koshish ki thi----> Extended takkavi loans and Irrigation facilites. 
		 
		 	- Encourage kiya, barley ki jagah wheat ugane ko. 
		 	
		 	- Firoz ne horticulture encourage kiya. 
		 	
		 	- Mo ne, Separate Agri Dept banaya--> Diwani Kohi. 
		 	
		 
		 12) India me sericulture introduce kiya gaya---> Made India less dependent on Import of Raw silk. 
		 
		 13) 14th and 15th CE me, extensive use of paper shuru hua. 
		 
		 14) Coinage ka system develop hua ---> 
		 
		 	Iltutmish - Silver Tanka
		 	
		 	Khalji --> Gold Dinar
		 	
		 	
		 15) Shuru me, Jiz-oh-yeah was collected as part of land tax.   Lekin, Firoz bhai ne, ise as a seperate tax lena shuru kar diya. 
		 
		 16) New Musical instrument : Sarani and Rabab were introduced
		 
		 	- Kusre ne, Ghora and Sanam jaise kai naye, Rag introduce kiye. 
		 	
		 	- Khusre ne hi Qwali introduce ki. 
		 	
		 	- Isi ne hi Sitar banaya. 
		 	
		 	- Firoz ke time, Ragadarpan ko Persian me translate kiya gaya. 
		 	
		 	- Raja Man Singh of Gwalior ne, Man Kautuhal naam ka great musical composition banaya. 
		 	
		 
		 17) Khusre ne, new style of Persian Poetry banai---<> Sabaq-i-Hind or Indian Style. 		 		
		  		
		  	- 'Khazain-ul-Futuh' --> Alauddin's Conquest
		  	
		  	- 'Tughlaq Nama' ---> Rise of Ghiyasuddin Tughlaq
		  	
		  18) Zia Nakshabi- --> 1st to translate, Sanskrit stories into Persian. 
		  
		  	- Tutu Nama --> Book of Parrot
		  	
		  19) Kashmir me, Zain-ul-adbidin ke time, Rajatarangini likhi gai	
		  
		  20) Nusrat Shah--> Patronized the translation of Mahabharath into Bengali. 
		  
		  
		  
		  
	Bhakti Movement : Directly influenced from Islam and Sufism
	
		Sufism==> Started in Persia, and spread in India around 11th CE.
		
		- 1st Saint--> Shaikh Ismail of Lahore
		
		- Most famous --> Khwaja Muinuddin CHishti --> Settled in Ajmer
		
		- Another one --> Bahauddin Zakariya, Farid Zakariya ka chacha. ----> Ye. Shihabuddin Suhrawardi ka chela tha. 
		
		- Ek or tha---> Nizamuddin Auliya---> Belonged to the chisti order. 
		
		
	Bhakti Movement : 
	
		1) 9th CE me Shankara ne start kiya - -> Ye bhai, Kerala me janma tha. 
		
			- Doctrine of Advaita or Monosim   and   Nirgunbrahman
			
		2) 12th CE --> Ramanuj ---> Ye bhai, Chennai me janma tha
		
			- Doctrine of Vishishtadvaita    and   Sagunabrahman
			
			- Also advocated, Prabattimarga ---> Path of Self-Surrender to God
			
		3) 13th CE ---> Madhava ---> Ye bhai, Karnataka me janma tha
		
			- Doctrine of Dviata  or Dualims of Jivatma and Paramatma 
			
			- Iske hisab se, World is not an illusion but a reality. 
			
		4) Isi time, Telugu region me---> Nimbarka and Vallabhacharya preached Vaishnavite Bhakti
		
		5) Surdas---> Vallabhacharya ka disciple tha ----> Popularized Krishna CUlt in North India. 
		
		6) Mirabai ---> Great devotee of Krishna
		
		7) Tulsidas --> Worshipper of Rama  and  COmposed the famous, Ramcharitmanas -0---> Hindi Version of Ramayan.
		
		
		8) 14th and 15th CE ---> Ramananda, Kabir and Nanak. 
		
			- Ye bhai log, rituals, ceremonies wagarh me yakeen nahi karte the. 
			
			- Denounced polytheism and belived in 1 god.
			
			- Denounced all forms of idolatry
			
			- Fundamental unity of all religions
			
		
		9) Ramananda --> Born in Allahbad---> Originally a follower of Ramanuja
		
			- Banaras and Agra me gyan choda. 
			
			- Worshipper of Rama. 
			
			- Ye bhai pehla banda tha, jo local language me gyan chod ta tha. 
			
			- Iske 2 main contri---> Simplification of Worship  and   Emancipation of people from traditional caste rules
			
			- Iske desciples the : Kabir     Raidasa      Sena       Sadhana       Dhanna(Jat boi)      Narahari       Pipa(Rajput Princess)
			
		
		10) Kabir : Ramananda ka most famous chela
		
			- Objective : To reconcile Hindu and Muslim ideas. 
			
			- Denounced idolatry and rituals
			
			- Greatest Mystic saints and his followers are called Kabirpanthis. 
			
			
		
		11) Guru Nanak : Disciple of Kabir
		
		
		12) Chaitanya : Popularised the Krishna Cult. 						  		
		
			- Bhai Bengali tha, lekin pure desh me ghumta tha. 
			
			
		13) Gnandeva : 13th CE Maharashtra ----> Founder of Maharashtra Dharma
		
			- Bhagwat Gita par, Gnaneswari naam ki commentary likhi. 
			
		14) Namdev : Preached Gospel of love.
		
		15) Eknath : 16th CE me
		
		16) Tukaram : Contemporary of Shivaji ---> Maratha Nationalism create kiya bhai ne	
		
		
		
		
		
		
	Vijaynagar Empire : 1336 se 1672 tak 4 dynasties ne rule kiya. : Sangama----> Saluva----> Tuluva ----> Aravidu 
	
		Sources : 1) Krishandevray ki Amukthamalyada
		
			   2) Gangadevi ki, Madurvijayam
			   
			   3) Allasani Peddanna ki Manucharitam
			   
			   4) Copper Plate inscription at Srirangam of Devraya II 
			   
			   5) Hampi Ruins
			   
			   
			   
		Travelers : Ibn Battuta      Venetian - Nicolo de Conti       Persian Abdur Razzak       Portuguese   Domingo Paes
		
			   	
			   	
			   	
		- Founded by---> Harihara and Bukka of Sangama Dynasty		   	
		
			= Ye, originaly, Kakatiya rulers of Warangal ke liye kaam karte the. 
			
			- Ye mulle ban gaye, fir, Vidyaranya ke under Hindu ban gaye. 
			
			- Tungabhadra River ke south bank par ek nai city banai. ---> 'Vijaynagar'
			
			- Hoysala kingdom ke decline ke time, inhone apna kingdom expand kiya----> 1346 tak pura Hoysala kingdom inke under aa gay. 
			
			- Inhone madurai Sultans ko harake, pure South India par kabja kar liya. 
			
			- Raichur Doab and Krishna-Tungabhara ke beech ke area ko lekar, inhone Bahmani kingdom se lode liye. 
			
			- Sangama Dynasty ka greatest ruler tha---> Deva Raya II
			
		
		
	 Krishna Deva Raya (1509 to 1530)
	 
	 	1) Ye bhai, Tuluva Dynasty ka tha, jise, Vira Narasimha ne found kiya tha. 
	 	
	 	2) Iske time tak, Bahamai sultanate was replaced by Deccan sultanates. 
	 	
	 	3) Battle of Diwani me bhai ne sabko hara diya.    Bhai ne fir, Raichur Doab par kabja kiya.    Sultan of Bijapur ko harake, Raichur shahr par kabja kiya, in 1520.  
	 	
	 	4) Bhai yahi nahi ruka or Bidar par kabja kar liya. 
	 	
	 	5) Bhai ne fir, Orissa ke, Gajapathi Ruler ko haraya and conqured the whole of Telangana. 
	 	
	 	6) Bhai ke portuguese se friendly relations the. ----> Albuquerque ne Ambassadors bheje the bhai ke pass. 
	 	
	 	7) Bhai ko log 'Andhra Bhoja' kehte the---> Great patron of lit and Art. 
	 	
	 	8) Bhai ke court me 8 eminent scholars rehte the--> Ashtadiggajas---> Inme sabse awesome tha---> Allasani Peddanna a.k.a Andhrakavita Pitamaha
	 	
	 										- Wrote Manucharitam  and   Harikathasaram
	 										
	 	9) Pingali Suranna   and    TENALI RAMAKRISHNA bhi. 
	 	
	 	10) Bhai ne khudne likha : Amuktamalyada (Telugu)   and  Jambavati Kalyanam and Ushaparinayam (dono Sanskrit)
	 	
	 	11) South India ke temples restore kiye. 
	 	
	 	12) Vittalswamy and Hazara Ramaswamy temple banwaye at Vijaynagar
	 	
	 	13) Apni item, Nagaladevi ki yaad me, Nagalapuram naam ki nayi city banwai.     
	 	
	 	14) Bohot saare, Rayagopurams bhi banwaye. 
	 	
	 	
	 	15) Bhai ki death ke baad--> Achutadeva  and Venkata succeded the throne
	 	
	 	
	 	
	 	
	 		- Rama Raya ke time--> Combined mulla forces defeated him at the Battle of Talaikotta in 1565. ---> 'Rakasa Thangadi'. 
	 		
	 		- Vijayanagar shahar to khatam ho gaya lekin, Aravidu Dynasty ke under, agle 100 saal or chalta raha empire. 
	 		
	 		- Is dynasty ke imp ruler the : Sri Ranga and Venkata II
	 		
	 		- Last Vijaynagar ruler---> Sri Ranga III
	 		
	 		
	 	
	 	Vijaynagar Admin : 
	 	
	 		1) King--> Absolute authoriy in executive, Judicial and legislative matters. 
	 		
	 				- Ye bhai, apne aap ko Highest court of appeal samjhta tha. 
	 				
	 				- Hereditary succession
	 				
	 				
	 		2) Empire was divided into diff admin units : Mandalams, Nadus, Sthalas  and finally into Gramas
	 		
	 			- Mandalam ka governor hota tha: Mandaleshwara or Nayak
	 			
	 			- In logo ko local admin ke liye full power di gayi thi. 
	 			
	 		
	 		3) Top-Grade officers of the army ---> Nayaks or Poligars ---> Inhe service ke badle me, 'Amaram' naam ki jameen di jaati thi. 
	 		
	 			- Lekin, Soldiers were usually paid in Cash. 
	 			
	 		
	 		
	 - Sangama Rulers---> Saivaites the  and  Virupaksha was their family deity. 	
	 	
	 	
	 - Kumarakampana ki Wife, Gangadevi ne---> 'Maduravijayam' naam ki book likhi. 
	 
	 - Nuniz ke hisab se--> Devadasi, Polygami and Sati was prevalant. 
	 
	 - Chief Gold Coin : 'Varaha'.
	 
	 - Inka main port tha : Cannanore
	 
	 - Ye log shipbuilding bhi karte the. 
	 
	 
	 
	 	Arts : 
	 	
	 		1) Main characteristic : Construction of tall Gopurams or Gateways  and  kalyanmandapam with Carved pillars
	 		
	 		2) In pillars par sabse jyada carve kiya gaya tha--> Ghoda. 
	 		
	 		3) BC, kuch bade mandiro me to, Itne bade mandap the, jime 1000 se jyada pillars hote the. 
	 		
	 		4) In mandapo me, festivals ke time, bhagwan ko baithate the. 
	 		
	 		5) Sabse mast temple milte thain--> hampi me ----> Vittalaswamy and Hazara Ramaswamy temples
	 		
	 		6) Varadharaja  and  Ekamaparanatha temples at Kanchipuram
	 		
	 		7) Thiruvannamalai  and  CHidambaram ke, Raya Gopurams--> Kya mast. 
	 		
	 		8) KDR or Bhai ki wife ki, Tirupati me bani Metal images--> Kya mast
	 		
	 		9) Sanskrit and telugu lit ki balle balle. 
	 		
	 		
	 		
	 
	 Bahamani Kingdom : Started by, Alauddin Bahman a.k.a Hasan Gangu in 1347.---> Capital at Gulbarga. 
	 
	 	- Ahamd Wali Shah ne capital shift karke, Bidar kar di. 
	 	
	 	- Muhammad Shah II ke under--> Peak Power.  --> Arabian Sea to BoB.   And Goa to Bombay     
	 	
	 		- Iska minister tha--> Mahmud Gawan--> mastermind. 
	 		
	 			1) Ye bhai ek Persian merchant tha
	 			
	 			2) Persain style me, Bidar me ek College bhi banwaya. 
	 			
	 			3) Isi ke time me, Konkan, Goa  and  K-D Delta conquer hua. 
	 			
	 			4) Isne, har province me royal officers appoint karwaye---> Forts ka control bhi inhi ke under tha. 
	 			
	 			5) Lekin, Nobels ki bhai se gand jalti thi, isliye, bhai ka gala utarwa diya. 
	 			
	 			6) Iske baad, Bahmani kingdom ke lode lag gaye---> Disintegrated in 1526---> Ahmadnagar,  Bijapur,  Berar, Golkonda and Bidar  (Deccan Sultanates)
	 			
	 						
	 						
	 						
	 						
	 						
	 						
	
	
	Babur(1526 to 1530) : Founder of Mughal Empire.    Zahiruddin Muhammad.    Iska baap, Timur ki aulado me se ek tha,  oR iski maa, Chengiz khan ki. 
	
		- Bhai ne, Kabul par kabja kiya, fir India par, 1519 se 1523 tak, 4 baar raid mari.
		
		- Bhai ne, Lahor par kabja kiya by defeating Daulat khan lodi. 
		
		- 21st April, 1526 ko---> Ibrahim Lodi ko loda diya in the 1st battle of Panipat.  						
		
		- Apne bete, Humayun ko Agra par kabja karne ke liye bhej diya. 
		
		- Baad me bhai ne, Rana Sanga (Battle of khanua) and Afghans (Battle of Gogra) ko haraya.
		
		- Bhai, 1530 me expire ho gaya, bas 47 ki age me. 
		
		- Bhai ne khudki, book likhi - 'Tuzuk-i-Baburi' in Turkish. ----> describe the flora and fauna of India. 
		
		
		
	Humayun(1530 to 1540)
	
		- Bahadur Shah se Gujrat capture kiya  and  appointed Askari as its governor. 
		
		- Isi time, Bengal me, Sher Khan powerful ho gaya 
		
		- 1539 me, Battle of Causa me, Bhai ki gand mar di sher khan ne.   BC, fir Agle saal, Battle of Kanauj(a.k.a Battle of Bilgram) me, Dobara gand mari Sher Khan ne. 
		
		
	
	Sur Dynasty(1540 to 1555)
	
		- Sher Shah Suri---> Became ruler of Delhi in 1540. 
		
		- Punjab, Malwa, Sind, Multan and Bundelkhand par kabja kiya. 
		
		- Assam, Nepal, Kashmir and Gujrat ke alawa, pura North India bhai ke under tha. 
		
		
		- Admin : 
		
			1) 4 important ministers appoint kiye : 
				
				i) Diwan-i-Wizarat : a.k.a Wazir---> Revenue and Finance
				
				ii) Diwan-i-Ariz : Army
				
				iii) Diwan-i-Risalat : Foreign Minister
				
				iv) Diwan-i-Insha : Minister of Communications
				
			
			2) Empire ko 47 sarkars me divide kiya. 
			
				- Har sarkar ki admin ke liye, 'Chief Shiqdar' - Law and Order      and      'Chief Munsif' - Judge  appoint kiye. 
				
				- Sab sarkar, kai saare 'Parganas' me divided thi. 
				
				- Har Pargana ki admin ke lie the ---> Shiqdar(Military officer),   Amin(Land Revenue),   Fotedar(Treasurer),   Karkuns(accountants)
				
				- 'Iqtas' naam ke admin units bhi the. 
				
				
			3) Land Survey karwaya  ====> Saari Cultivable land ko, 3 classes me divide kiya---> 'Good', 'middle' and 'bad'. 
			
				- State ka share tha---> 1/3rd of Avg produce. 
				
			
			4) 'Dam' naam ka naya Silver Coin introduce kiya---> Ye, 1835 tak circulation me the. 
			
			5) 4 naye Highways banwayae : 
			
				i) Sonargaon to Sind
				
				ii) Agra to Burhampur
				
				iii) Jodhpur to Chittor
				
				iv) Lahore to Multan				 
				
				
				
				
		- Bhai ne, Yamuna River par ek naya shahar banwaya. 
		
		- Sasaram me Mausoleum banwaya. 
		
		- Malik Muhammad Jayasi ne, 'Padmavat' likhi isi time. 		
		
		


	Return of Humayun (1555 to 1556 lol)
	
		- 1542 me, Akbar ka janam hua tha. 
		
		- 1555 me, Delhi par dobara kabja kar liya. 	
		
		
	
	
	Akbar : 1556 to 1605
	
		- 2nd Battle of Panipat in 1556 me, Hemu ko haraya. 
		
		- Gujrat and Bengal par kabja kiya. 
		
		- Raja Bharmal ki beti chodi. 
		
		- Raja Bhagwan Das   and   Raja Man singh ko senior positions di. 	
		
		- 1576 me, Rana Pratap ko Haldighati me harya. (Mewar)
		
		- Pigrim tax and Jiz-oh-yeah ko abolish kiya (1562) 
		
		- 1575 me,  Ibadat Khana  banwaya at his new capital Fatehpur Sikri. 
		
		- 157 me, 'Infallibility Decree' issue ki---> To say fuck you to Mulla Ulemas. 
		
		- 1582 me bhai ne khudka naya religion hi bana dala===> Din Ilahi
		
		
			Land Revenue Admin : Raja Todar Mal ki madad se kai experiments kiye
			
				1) Zabti or Bandobast System , jise Todar Mal ne improve karke--> Dahsala System bana diya---> Completed in 1580. 
				
					- Uniform system of land measurement. 
					
					- Pichle 10 saal ki annual yield ke basis par---> Revenue fix kar diya. 
					
					- 4 categories me land divide ki : Polaj           Parauti(2 saal me ek baar kheti wali)       Chachar(3-4 saal me ek baar)    and  Banjar(5 se jyada salo me ek baar)
					
					- Cash me payments lete the. 
					
					
				
			
			Mansabdari System : Har officer ko ek rank( Mansab) assign kiya. 
			
				- Lowest rank- 10,  Highest rank- 5000 for nobles.      Royal princes ko is se bhi jyada. 
				
				- Ranks were divided into 2 ---> Zat and Sawar ---> Zar- Personal status of banda.     Sawar----> Number of ghudsawar a banda was requried to maintain. 
				
				- Har sawar ko kamsekam 2 ghode rakhne hote the.   
				
				- Mansabs hereditary nahi the. 
				
				- Saare Appointments or Dismissals, emperor khud karta tha. 
				
				
	Jahangir(1605 to 1627)
	
	
		1) Iske bete , Khusrau ne revolt kiya. 
		
		2) Khusre ke supporter, Guru Arjun--> 5th Guru ka Gala utara. 
		
		3) 1611 me, Mehrunnisa ko chodna shuru kiya, jo khudko, Nur-Jahan kehti thi. 
		
		4) Is randi ke bhai, Asaf khan ki beti ko, baad me, Shah jahan ne, Taj Mahal banwa ke choda. 
		
		5) Shah Jahan ne, 1622 me revolt kar diya, kyunki, Nur Jahan bina kapdo ke court me aa jati thi. 
		
	
	Shah Jahan (1627 to 1658)
	
		1) Bhai ne, Kandhar par kabja karne ki koshih ki, lekin maa chud gayi. 
		
		2) Lekin, bhai ne, Agmadnagar par kabja kar liya--> Carved 4 provinces there---> Khandesh, Berar, Telungana and Daulatabad----> Aurangzeb ke under kar di sab. 
		
		3) Bhai 1666 me expire ho gaya. 
		
		
	
	Aurangzeb (1658 to 1707)
	
		1) Shivaji se lode lene ke liye---> Annexed Bijapur and Golkonda. ----> Lekin, aisa karte karte bhai ne apni treasury empty kar di. 
		
		2) Isne, Muhtasibs appoint kiya---> High powered officers to enforce moral codes 
		
		3) Betichod ne charas par ban laga diya.    Jarokhadarshan ki practice bhi band kar di.      Dasarah and Muharram par ban laga diya. 
		
		4) 1679 me, Jiz-oh-yeah, dobara impose kar diya			
		
		5) 9th Sikh Guru - Tej Bahadur ka Gala utara. 					
					
					
					
	Is Mulle ke marne ke baad, Mughal Empire ki maa chud gayi. ----> Nadir Shah Invaded in 1739. 
	
	
	
	17th CE me---> 2 new crops were added : Tobacco and Maize
	
	18th CE me---> Potato and Red Chillies came				
					
					
					
					
	Mughal Arts : 
	
		1) Gardens : Nishat Bagh in Kashmir,     Shalimar Bagh in Lahore,    Pinjore Garden in Punjab
		
		2) Akbar ne banwaya--> Agra fort.    Fatehpur-Sikri
		
			- Apni Gujrat victory ke baad- --> Buland Darwaja banwaya. 
			
			- Akbar ke time hi bana---> Humayun's Tomb---> Precursor of Taj Mahal. 
			
			- Akbar ka khudka tomb--> Jahangir ne banwaya. 
		
		3) Shah-Jahan ---> Red Fort				
		
	
	
		4) Mughal Paintings : Humayun ne shuru karwaya---> When he was staying in Pesia - ----> Waha se ye 2 bande pakad ke laya---> Mir Sayyid Ali  and  Abdal Samad
		
			- Akbar ke painters : Baswan,  Miskina and Daswant
			
			- Akbar nama me,  and  1200 painting wali Hamzanama me bhi bohot saari miniatrue paintings hain. 
			
			- Reached climax during age of Jahangir : Abdul Hasan,  Bishan Das,  Madhu,  Anant,  Manohar,  Govardhan  and Ustad Mansur. 
			
		
		
		5) Mughal Lit : 
		
			1) Abul Fazl : Ain-i-Akbari   and  Akbar nama
			
			2) Iske bhai, Abul Faizi ke under, Mahabharata ko Persian Language me translate karwaya gaya. 
			
			3) Jahangir ki autobiography : Tuzuk-i-Jahangiri : patronized : Ghiyas Beg,   Naqib khan  and  Niamatullah
			
			4) Shah Jahan : Abdul Hamid Lahori---> Wrote Padshah Nama   and   Inayat Khan---> Wrote Shah Jahan Nama. 
			
			5) Dara Shikoh ---> Translated, Bhagavat Gita and Upanishads into Persain language 
			
			
			
			
			
			
	Marathas : Rose in 16th and 17th CE. 
	
		- Religious unity due to  : Tukkaram,  Ramdas,  Vaman Pandit  and Eknath. 
		
		- Shivaji : 
		
			1) Born in 1627
			
			2) Jagir of Poona in 1637. 
			
			3) Attacked Bijapur Kingdom in 1657. Killed Afzal Khan in 1659. 
			
			4) Got defeated by SHaista Khan---> Mughal Governor of Deccan.  But, in 1663---> Killed his son. 
			
			5) 1664 ---> Attacked Surat. 
			
			6) Signed Treaty of Purander with Jai Singh in 1665. ----> Surrendered 23 forts to Mughals---> Became loyal to Mughals. 
			
			7) His Son Sambhaji---> Granted Mansab of 5000. 
			
			8) Visited Agra in 1666. 
			
			9) 1670 ---> Attacked Surat once more.   Captured all his lost territories. 
			
			10) 1674 ---> Crowned himself at Raigarh and assumed the title, Chatrapathi. 
			
			11) Vellore Campaign
			
			12) Died in 1680. 
			
			
				Sivaji Admin : 
				
					- ASsisted by a council of ministers called---> Ashtapradhan---> All of them directly responsible to Shivaji. 
					
					- Peshwa : Finanve and General Admin. ---> Baad me ye betichod Prime Minister ban gaya. 
					
					- Sar-i-Naubat  or  Senapati ---> Military Commander ---> An honorary post. 
					
					- Amatya --> Accountant General .
					
					- Waqenavis ---> Intelligence, Posts and Household affairs. 
					
					- Sachiv --> Correspondence
					
					- Sumanta---> Master of Ceremonies. 
					
					- Nyayadish --> Justice
					
					- Panditarao ---> Charities and Religious admin. 
					
					
				- Ye sab, Deccan Sultanates par based the---> Peshwa ek Persian Title hai. 
				
				- Revenue System was based on that of Malik Amber of Agmadnagar. 
				
				- 'Kathi' naam ki rod se land measure karte the. 
				
				- Existing deshmukhs and kulkarnis ki power reduce ki. ---> Appointed his own revenue officials called Karkuns. 
				
				- Chauth And Sardeshmukhi ---> Tax collected from neighbouging non-maratha areas. 
				
					- Chauth --> Agar raid hone se bachna hai to 1/4th pay karo. 
					
					- Sardeshmukhi --> Ye jameen hamare baap dada ki hua karti thi, isliye, 10% extra pay karo. 
					
				- 2 divisions of Maratha Cavalry : 1) Bargirs --> Paid by states    2) Silahdars --> Maintained by Nobles. 
				
				- Shivaji ke bete, Shamaji and Rajaram me ladai ho gayi.  Shambaji jeet gaya, lekin, mughalo ne iski gardan utar di. 
				
				- Rajaram bhaag ke Satara aa gaya or mar gaya---> iska beta Shivaji II and Tara Bai started ruling. 
				
				- Inke baad aaya Shahu---> Iske time me, Peshwa Rose to power. 
				
				
		
		Peshwas(1713 to 1818)
		
			1) Balaji Viswanath (1713 to 1720)
			
				- Is bhai ne hi Shahu ko ruler banwaya tha. 
				
				- 1719 me, Farukh Siyar se kuch rights liye, Marathas ke liye. 
				
					Farukh recognized Shahu as Maratha king. 
					
					Allowed them to collect C and S from 6 mughal provinces of Deccan. 
					
			
			2) Baji Rao I (1720 to 1740)
			
				- Eldest son of BV.    20 saal ki umar me peshwa ban gaya bhai. 
				
				- Maratha power reached its zenith under him. 
				
				- Confederacy system start kiya, Marathas chiefs ke among. 
				
					Gaekwad at baroda,     Bhonsle at Nagpur,     Holkars at Indore,   Scindias at Gwalior     Peshwas at Poona
					
			
			
			3) Balaji Baji Rao (1740 to 1761)
			
				- Shahu ke marne ke baad, bhai ne Pure Maratha kingdom par kabja kar liya. 
				
				- 1752 me, Mughals se agreement kiya---> To collect total revenues of Agra and Ajmer provinces in lieu of defence of Mughals. 
				
				- Isiliye, jab Abdali ne invade kiya, tab Marathas ko ladna pada---> 3rd Battle of Panipat in 1761. 							
				
				
				
				
				
				
				
				
				
	Europeans : 
	
		- VDG ne, 1498 me, Cape of Good Hope ke around naya route discover kiya. 
		
			Reached Calicut on 17th May, 1498.   Received by Zamorin. 
			
		
		- Pedro Alvarez Cabral arrived in 1500.    VDG also returned in 1502. 	
		
		- In logo ne, Calicut, Cannanore and Cochin me, Trading Stations bana liye. 
		
		- 1st Governor appoint hua---> Francis de Almeida		
				
		- 1509 me naya gov --> Albuquerque
		
			1) Bhai ne, Goa capture kiya, Sultan of Bijapur se, in 1510. 
			
			2) Iske baad---> Goa became the capital of Portuguese settlement in Indai. 
			
			3) Bhai ne baad me, Malacca and Ceylon par bhi kabja kiya. 
			
			4) Bhai ne, Calicut me ek fort banwaya. 
			
			5) Bhai, 1515 me expire ho gaya--> But left the portuguese as the strongest naval power in India. 
			
		
	
	
	Dutch :  Dutch EIC bani thi, 1602 me 
	
		1) Settlements at---> Masulipattinam  Pulicat, Surat, Karaikal,  Nagapattinam,  Chinsura  and  Kasimbazar. 
		
		2) 17th CE me ports ko haraya---> Became the dominant power in EAst.
		
		3) Inka main centre tha Pulicat---> LAter shifted to Nagapattinam. 
		
		4) Angrezo ke aane ke baad, theri rivalry lasted for 7 decades. 
		
		
		
	Brits : EIC bani thi, 1600 me--> Charter issued by Queen Elizabeth . 
	
		1) Captain Hawkins aaya, at the court of Jahangir in 1609. ---> "Sir, Surat me factory khol le kya". 
		
		2) Lekin, ports ke pressure me aake, Mughlas ne refuse kar diya. 
		
		3) Lekin, 1612 me, Jahangir ne Farman issue kiya--> So they established a factory at surat in 1613. 
		
		4) Sir Thomas Roe---> Came to India as ambassador of James I to Mughal Court in 1615.    India ke different parts me factories kholne ki permission mil gayi. 
		
		5) 1619 tak, Inhone, Agra, Ahmadabad, Baroda and Broach me Factories khol li thi. 
		
		6) Acquired, Bombay from Charles II--> then king of England 
		
		7) 1639 ---> Francis Day ne, Madras city found ki---> Fort St.George was built here. 
		
		8) 1690 me, Job Charnok ne, Sutanati me English Factory setup ki---> Later developed into Calcutta---> Fort Williams was built here. 
		
	
	
	French : French EIC --> Formed in 1664 by Colbert---> Louis XIV ka minister tha ye. 
	
		1) 1st French Factory bani thi, Surat me, By Francis Caron. 	
		
		2) Marcara naam ke bande ne baad me, Masulipattinam me bhi ek factory kholi. 
		
		3) 1673 me, Francois Martin ne, Pondicherry found kiya.  ---> Ye bhai, Pehla Gov tha, Pondicherry ka. 
		
		4) Other French Factories : Chandranagore,  Mahe and Karaikal. 		
		
		
		
	
	Denmark : Settlement at Tranquebar---> Founded in 1620. 
	
		- Another major settlement : Serampore in Bengal ---> Ye inka HQ tha India me. 
		
		- Par, in salo ne, 1845 me, Brits ko apni saari possesions bech di, or kat liye chup chaap. 
		
		
	
	
	Brit Rule : 
	
		- Robert Clive---> 1st Governor of Fort Williams ---> Iske baad aaya, Verelst  and  Cartier. 
		
		- 1772 me, Company ne, Warren Hastings ko, Governor of Fort Williams appoint kiya. 
		
		- Bhai ne, Clive wala Dual System abolish kiya---> EIC decided to act as Diwan, and to undertake the collection of Revenue by its own agent. 
		
		- Bhai ne, Nawab ki allowance bhi aadhi kar di and he also stopped the annual payments of 26 lakhs given to Mughal Emperor. 
		
		- Revenue collect karne ke liye, ek Board of Revenue was established at Calcutta, to supervise the collection of revenue. 
		
		- Sab districts me ENglish collectors appoint kiye. 
		
		- Treasury ko, Murshidabad se Calcutta shift kiya and an accountant general was appointed---> Calcutta became the capital of Bengal in 1772. 
		
		- BoR ne, lands ke auction kiye, 5 saalo ke liye----> Zamindar logo ko, auction me priority di. 
		
		- Inhone, Arbitrary cessed and unreasonable fines ko abolish kiya ---> Rent enhancement par restriction lagai. 
		
		- Lekin, ye system fail ho gaya---> Many zamindars defaulted and the arrears of revenue accumulated. 
		
		
	Warren Hastings ne, Judicial system ko bhi reorganise kiya
	
		-Sab disctricts me-->  Civil court under a collector   and   a criminal court under an Indian Judge. 
		
		- District courts se appeal sun ne ke liye---> 2 appellate courts--> 1 for civil cases and 1 for criminal cases, banai Calcutta me. 
		
		- Highest Civil court of appeal---> Sadar Diwani Adalat. ---> To be presided over by the Governoe  and 2 judges, recruited from among the members of his council. 
		
		- Highest Criminal court of appeal---> Sadar Nizamat Adalat--> Under an Indian jugge, appointed by Governor-in-council. 
		
		- Judges ko assist karne ke liye, experts in Hindu and Muslim laws were provided. 
		
		- Halhed ne---> 'Code of Hindu Laws' publish kiya. 
		
		
	
	Warren Hastings ne---> System of Dastaks(Free Passes) ko abolish kiya---> Regulated the Internal Trade. 
	
		- Reduced the number of custom houses. 
		
		- Indian and Non-Indian goods par, uniform 2.5% tariff lagaya. 
		
		- Lekin, Company ke servants ki private trade chalti rahi. 
		
	
	Bhai ne, ek Pre-Paid postage system bhi introduce kiya. 
	
	Calcutta me ek bank bhi start kiya. 
	
	Calcutta ki Police ko bhi improve kiya. 
	
	
	
	
	1773 me, EIC bankrupt hone wali thi, to wo, Brit sarkar ke pass lone lene gai.   Lord North--> PM---> Appointed a select committee to inquire into the affairs of the company. 
	
		- Iski report par, Regulating act enact kiya gaya. 
		
			1) CoD ke members ka term 1 saal se badhake 4 saal kar diya gaya.    Retiring directors not eligible for re-election
			
			2) GG of Bengal--> 5 year term
			
			3) Council of 4 to assist the GG.     Decision of the majority ke hisab se government chalai jayegi. 
			
				- Lekin, tie ke case me, GG ke pass ek casting vote tha. 
				
			4) And, War and Peace ke matters me--> GG was made supreme
			
			5) SC at Calcutta, consisting of 1 Chief-Justice and 3 junior judges. ---> Independent of GG in council. 
			
			6) Servants of the company---> Can't accept any gifts. 
			
			
		
		Warren Hastings ke time ye saare wars hue : Rohilla War,  1st-Anglo-Maratha war ,    2nd Anglo-Mysore War
		
		
		
		Rohilla War : 1774
		
			1) Oudh and marathas ke beech ek chota sa, kingdom tha---> Ruler was---> Hafiz Rahmat Khan
			
				- Is bhai ne, Marathas ke dar se, Oudh ke Nawab se ek defensive treaty kar li thi. 
				
				- Par koi attack hua hi nahi.   Lekin, Nawab sala fir bhi paise maang raha tha. 
				
				- Mana karne par, Betichod nawab, angrez bula laya. 
				
		
		
		1st Anglo-Maratha War (1775 to 82)
		
			- Madhav Rao and Raghunath Rao ke beech, Peshwa ki post ke liye, ladai ho rahi thi. 
			
			- Bomaby ke Brits ne, RR ke saath, Treaty of Surat kar li, in March 1775. 0----> Brits ko, Bassein and Salsette mil gaya. 
			
			- lekin, RR mukar gaya dene se, To brits ne jabardasti kabja kar liya BC. 
			
			- Leki, 1776 me, Hastings ne is chutiyaap se naraj hokar, Co. Upton ko bheja dispute settle karne ke liye----> To bhai ne, Nana Fadnavis se, Treaty of Purander sign ki. 
			
			- Madhav Rao ko Peshwa accept kar liya gaya and Brits retained Salsette. 
			
			- Lekin, baad me betichod ye fir lad pade, kyunki brits ko ye treaty pasand nahi aai. 
			
			- 1781 me, Captain Popham ne, Mahadaji Scindia ko hara diya and captured Gwalior. 
			
			- 1782 me, Treaty of Salbai sign hui, Hastings or Scindia ke beech. ====> Brits ko aakhrikaar, dono Salsette and Bassein mil gaye. 
			
			
				- Is treaty ki madad se, Brits ka lemon chusne lage maratha agle 20 saalo tak. 
				
				
				
		
		2nd Anglo-Mysore War (1780 to 84)
		
			- Pehli wali me, Haider Ali, jeet gaya tha in 1767-69.---> Dono ne ek Defensive treaty sign ki. 
			
			- Lekin, jab 1771 me, Marathas ne Mysore par attack kiya, to saale brits, apni jhant khujlate rahe, madad karne nahi aaye. 
			
			- American War of independence ki wajah se, French and Brits bhi lad rahe the. 
			
			- Brits ne, Mahe capture kar liya---> a French settlement within Haider's Territories. 
			
			- Haider Ali ne, Nizam and Marathas ke saath ek Grand alliance banaya, in 1779. 
			
			- Ladai shuru hui, jab saale brits, Northern Sarkar me Guntur capture karne ke liye, Haider ke area se hokar ja rahe the, wo bhi bina permission liye. 
			
			- Hastings ne alliance ki maa chod di, or Haider ko akela kar diya----> then he was defeated by Sir Eyre Coote at Porto Novo in March 1781.  Haider mar gaya agle saal. 
			
			
			- 2nd Mysore war came to an end by the Treaty of Mangalore in 1783. 			 		
		
	
	
	Hastings ke baad aaya : Lord Cornawallis the Coward --> in 1786. 
	
		- Surrendered York Town in 1781, Jiske badle bhai ko India ka GG bana diya gaya. 
		
		- 1786 me Pitt's India act ko amend kiya gaya---> Gave him overriding powers. 
		
		- Cornwallis ke saath hi--> Kisi arostracatic family se GG ke post ke liye bande choose karne ki pratha shuru ho gayi. 
		
		- Bhai ki team me the : John Shore,   James Grant    and   Sir Willian Jones. 
		
		
	
	Tipu Sultan and the 3rd Mysore War (1790 to 92)
	
		- Treaty of mangalore- 1784---> Exposed English weakness. 
		
		- Tupu ne, Travancore par kabja karne ki koshish ki. 
		
		- Brits ne, 1789 me, Nizam and marathas ke saath, Tripartite alliance kar liya, against Tipu. 
		
		- 1790 me ladai shuru hui. 
		
		- 1792 me, Tipu ne, Treaty of Srirangapattinam sign kiya with the Brits. 
		
			- Tipu ki aadhi territory cheen li. 
			
			- 3 crore Rs + 2 bete. 
			
			- Prisoners of war ko release karna. 
			
		- Brits ko, Malabar Coast par, large territory mil gayi. 
		
		
		
	
	Cornwallis ke reforms : 
	
		1) Admin Reforms : 
		
			- Cabale and Honest Civil servants appoint kiye---> Purified Civil service. 
			
			- Company ke directors ko bola ki, Company servants ki salary badhao. 
			
			- Bas Merit ke basis par appointments karne ki policy start ki---- Laid the foundation of Indian Civil services. 
			
			- 3 branches of services ko seperate kiya---> Commercial, Judicial and Revenue. 
			
			- Collectors se unki Judicial powers cheen li. ----> their work became merely, collection of revenue. 
			
			
		
		2) Judicial Reforms : With the help of Sir Willian Jones---> Judge and a great scholar. ----> Civil and Criminal courts were completely reorganized. 
		
			- Judicial system ke top par the--> Highest and Civil court of appeals---> At calcutta----> Both were presided by GG and his council. 
			
			- 4 provincial courts of appeals bhi thi---> Calcutta,  Dacca,  Musrhidabad and Patna ===> Under 3 Euro Judges assisted by Indian advisers. 
			
			- District and City courts---> Under an European judge. ---> har district me ek court tha. ---> COllectors se unki power cheen ke hi bhai ne, District judges appoint kiye the. 
			
			- Sab courts me, Indian judges a.k.a Munsiffs bhi appoint kiye gaye the. 
			
			
			- Criminal cases me Mulla law apply hota tha,  Civil wale me seperate. 
			
			- Barbarous punishments ko abolish karwaya. 
			
			
			- George Barlow ki madad se bhai ne, ek Comprehensive code prepare karwaya---> COvering the whole field of admin, Judiciay, Poice, Commercial and fiscal 
			
			- Ye code, Montesquie ke 'Seperation of Powers' ke principle par based tha
			
			- Made, all officials answerable to the courts. 			
	
	
	
	 	Police Reforms : Taki, judicial reforms ko effectively implement kiya ja sake. 
	 	
	 		- District judge, police ko control karta tha. 
	 		
	 		- Har district, Thanas or Police circles me divided thi, each of which was about 20 square miles. ----> Placed under an Indian officer called the daroga.
	 		
	 		- Daroga ko assist karne ke liye, constables hote the. ---> Lekin, ye saale daroga randibaaj the--> Became scourge of the country. 
	 		
	 		
	 		
	 	Board oF trade reforms : BoT--> COmapny ke commercial investments ko manage karta tha. 
	 	
	 		- CHarles Grant ki madad se, bhai ne, corrupt practices ko eradicate karwaya. 
	 		
	 		- Indian workers and weavers ki payment badhai. 	
	
	
	
	- Cornwallis ke baad aaya---> Sir John Shore (1793 to 1798)
	
		- Adopted the Policy of Neutrality---> Is saale ki fat ti thi. 
	
	
	
		Marquess of Wellesley (1798 to 1805) : 'The Bengal Tiger' or 'The Fuck Boy'. 
		
			- Bhai India me aaye the, with a forward policy, to make, 'The british Empire in India' into 'The British Empire of India'. 
			
			- Bhai ne, is kaam ke liye--> "Subsidiary Alliance" ka system adopt kiya. 
	
			- Brits ko, Napolean ke invasion ka dar tha.     Saala Nizam bhi French chodo ka lemon chusne laga tha.   Maraths or Tipu to the hi,  NW me Zaman Shah naam ka choda bhi tha. 
			
		
		
		Subsidiary System : 
		
			- Wellesly se pehel, Awadh and Hyderabas ke chodo ne, alliances kiye the, Brits ke saath. 
			
			- Troops ke badle me brits ko subsidy milti thi.    Fuckboy ne isi system ko enlarge kiya. 
			
		
		- Had to maintai a contingent of Brit Troops.   To be commanded by a Brit Officer.    The Indian state was called 'the Protected State'  and Brits ==> 'THe Paramount Power'. 
		
		- Iske badle me, paisa ya part of territory. 
		
		- Lekin, Saare Euro se connections band, specially French se. 
	
		- Apni army disband karo or, ek British Resident rakho court me. 
		
		- Paramount power, internal affairs me lund nahi adayegi. 
	
		
		
			- Lekin, troops disband karne ki wajah se ye saale daaku ban gaye---> Ex : Pindaris of Central India. 
			
			- Princes---> Apne admin responsibility ko bhi ignore karne lage.  Sala, misadmin and Anarchy badhne par, Brits annex kar liya karte the. 
			
			
		
		1) Hyderabad : 1st State under S A in 1798.   Rs 24 lakh annually.    1800 me, Nizam ne bohot saari territories bhi de di. 
		
		2) Awadh : Afgan Zaman SHah ke invasion ka dar tha---> To Sala Awadh ke nawab ko force kiya, SA accept karne ke lye. 
		
			- Nawab ne diye : Lands in Rohilkhand,   Gorakhpur ka lower Doab. 
			
			- Nawab ke army ki streangth kam hui. 
			
			- Law and Order maintain karne ke naam par, Brits ko, Internal Affairs me lund adane ka moka bhi mil gaya yaaha. 
			
			
		3) Assumed the admin of Tanjore, Surat and Karnataka by concluding treaties with respective rulers. 
		
			- Tanjore me, Maratha Sarbhoji ko Raja maana. 
			
			- Surat ka protection to sala 1759 me hi mil gaya tha, lekin, 1799 me Nawab mar gaya, to Fuckboy ne admin par hi kabja kar liya. 
			
			
	
	4th Anglo-Mysore War : Kyunki Tipu apni defeat ko avenge karna chahta tha. 
	
		- Tipu ne, 1798 me, Revolutionary French Gov se bhi correspondance kiya. 
		
		- Srirangapattinam me ek Jacobin Club bhi shuru kiya. --> Tree of Liberty was planted. 
		
		- Jab Napolean Egypt me tha, tab bhai ko ek letter bhi likha. 
		
		- Ye sab jab ho raha tha, tabi hamara Fuckboy Calcutta aaya tha. 
		
		
			- General Stuart ke under, Bombat army ne, West se attack kiya. 
			
			- Arthur Wellesly ke under, Madras Army ne, East se attack kiya. ---> Ye Fuckboy ka bhai tha. 
			
			
		- Krishnaraja III--> 5 saal ke---> ko Mysore de diya gaya.    Puraniya became Diwan.      Baaki parts, Brits and Nizam ke beech divide ho gaya. 
		
		
		
		- Mysore ke baad, bas Marathas hi akeli power rah gaye the, outside the Subsidiary Alliance. ----> Nana Fadnavis inka leader tha. ---?> But ye 1800 me expire ho gaya. 
		
		- Jaswant Rao Holkar and Daulat Rao Scindia--> Ye chutiye aapas me ladte rahte the, or Peshwa Baji Rao II apni jhant nochta tha baith ke. 
		
		- Holkar ne Peshwa and Scindia, dono ki ek saath maa chod di.   Saale ne Poona shahar ki bhi maa chod di. 
		
		- Peshwa sala dar ke, Brits ka lemon chusne bhaag gaya to Bassein----> Signed the Treaty of Bassein in 1802. --> Ye ek Subsidiary Treaty thi. 
		
		- Is treaty ke baad se, Marathas ki foreign policy brits ke under aa gayi. 
		
		- Is treaty ke baad, Brits troops ki madad se, Peshwa ne, poona khali karwaya(Holkar kat liya chupchaap). 
		
		
	
	2nd Maratha War(1803 to 1805)
	
		- Scindia and Bhonsle ki, Bassein treaty se, Gand me aag lag gayi. 
		
		- Dono ki gand thandi kardi Fuckboy ne, in 1803 near Aurangabad. 
		
		- Bhonsle ko fir uske ghar me ghuske maara at the plains of Argaon---> Signed the Treaty of Deogaon---> signed between Bhonsle and Fuckboy. 
		
			Brits ko, Cuttack in Orissa mil gaya, and Bhonsle ko SA. 
		
		-  Baad me, Lord Lake ne, Delhi ke aaspass, Scindia ki maa chod di----> Signed the Treaty of Surji-Arjungaon. 
		
		- Puri ladai me, sala Holkar gayab tha. 
		
		
		
	- Karnataka and Tanjore annex karke, Fuckboy ne hi Madras Presidency banai thi---->> 'Maker of Madras'. 
	
	- Agra province bhi bhai ne hi banai thi, Bharatpur raja se leke, during the 2nd AM war. 					
	
	
	
	Fuckboy ke baad, 2 saal ke liye aaya---> Gerogre Barlow(1805 to 1807) ----> Vellore Mutiny hui iske time par. 
	
	Barlow ke baad, Lord Minto aaya from 1807 to 1813 
	
		- Signed, Treaty of Amritsar with Ranjit Singh in 1809. 
		
		- Charter Act of 1813 bhi isi ke time pass hua tha. 	
		
		
	Minto ke baad, 1813 me, GG bana---> Lord Hastings. ----> Vigrous Forward policy and waged wars. 
	
		- Is time, Central India me, Pindaris, toofan macha rahe the. 
		
		- Marathas and Gurkhas bhi josh me aa rahe the. 
		
		
			Gurkha Wars (1814 to 1816)
			
				- 1768 me, Nepal emerged as a powerful Gurkha state. 
				
				- 1801 me, Brits ko, Awadh nawab ne, Basti and Gorakhpur de diye the. ---> Boundry with Nepal. 
				
				- 1814 me, Gurkha ne, 18 brit policemen ki gardan utar di. ---> Hastings declared war on Nepal. 	
				
				- March, 1816 me, Treaty of Sagauli sign hui---> Gurkhas ne, Terai region ke claim chod diye    and    Kumaon and Garhwal ka area Brits ko de diya. 
				
				- Brit Resident from kathmandu.    Gurkha sikkim se bhi kat liye. 
				
				- Gurkhas ne kasam khai ki wo brits ke alawa kisi or Euro ka lemon nahi chusenge. Ye parampara aaj tak kayam hai, as evident from r/Nepal posts. 
				
				- Brits ko kai Hill Stations bhi mil gaye : Simla,  Mussoori, Nainital, Ranikhet
				
				- Is war ke baad hi, Hatings becmae---> 'Marquis of Hastings'. 
				
				
		
		1812 me, Pindaris ki gand leni shuru ki.  Their leaders : Karim Khan(Lived in an estate),   Wasil Muhammad(Suicide)   and   Chitu(Ise Tiger kha gaya)
		
			- 1824 tak, Pindaris shaant ho gaye. 
			
			
		
		
		1817 me, Brtish Resident Elphinstone ne, Peshwa Baji Rao II ke saath, Treaty of Poona Sign ki---> over some fucked up shit related to Triambakji
		
		
		
		3rd Maratha War(1817 to 1819)
		
			- Peshwa saale ne, 5th Nov 1817 ko bhang ke nashe me, British Residency par hamla bol diya. 
			
			- Ye haar gaya at Kirkee. 
			
			- Bhonsle bhi haar gaya, in the Battle of Sitabaldi. 
			
			- Holkar bhi sala haar gaya, at Baroda. 
			
			1818 me, Scindia ne bhi treaty kar li. 
			
			
		1818 : The Maratha Dream of Establishing themselves as the paramount power in India was completely destroyed. 
		
		
		- Hastings ke time hi, Ryotwari System introduce hua tha. 
		
			- In Madras Presidency by Sir Thomas Munroe. 
			
		
		- Bhai ne, Bengal ke Police system ko bakli areas me bhi expand kar diya. 
		
		- 1817 me, Calcutta me, Hindu College khulwaya
		
		- Bhai ne, Press freedom ko promote karne ke liye, 1799 wali censorships ko abolish kiya. 
		
		- 1818 me, Marshman naam ke ek missionary ne, 'Samachar Darpan' naam ka Bengali Weekly shuru kiya. 
		
		- Bhai was known as the maker of Bombay Presidency. 
		
		
	
	Hastings ke baad aaya---> Lord Amherst(1823 to 28)
	
	
	
	Lord Willian Bentinck : 1828 to 1835. 
	
		- Appointed the Gov of Madras in 1803.    Sir Thomas Munroe ki madad ki, Revenue admin me. 
		
		- 1806 me Vellore mutiny ka bhanda William ke sar fata --> he was recalled 
		
		- Lekin, 1828 me GG appoint kar diya gaya. 
		
		- Indian States ke against, policy of non-intervention and non-aggression adopt kiya. 
		
		
	
		Mysore ==> 1830 me chutiye raja ki wajah se revolt hua---> To Brits took over the admin. --> Sir Mark Cubbon --> 1834 to 1861---> Good Guy. 
		
		- Treaty of Yandaboo se Cachar mila tha---> iske raja ke assasination ke baad--> 1832 me annex kar liya gaya. 
		
		- Jaintia ko bhi annex kiya, kyunki yaha ka raja, kali mata ke liye, bali chadha raha tha. 
		
		- 1834 me, COorg capture kiya, kyunki Vira Raja lode le raha tha logo se. 
		
		- Bhai ke time, Brits ki pehli baar, Russians se gand fatna shuru hui. 
		
			- 1831 me, Ranjit singh se mulakat hui at the banks of River Sutlej. 
			
			- Indus Navigation Treaty Sign hui. 
			
			- Punjab and Sindh ke saath commercial treaties bhi sign hui. 
			
		
		- 1833 wala charter act pass hua---> Defined the scope and authority of EIC. 
		
			1) Liberal and Utilitarian Philosophy of Bentham was made popular. 
			
			2) EIC ceased to be a commercial agency in India. ---> Aaj ke baad se, it would function as the political agent of the crown. 
			
			3) GG of Fort Williams ban gaya, GG of India. 
			
			4) GG's Council me ek, Law Member appoint kiya gaya---> T.B Macaulay
			
			5) Indianisation of Public Serivces ki neenv rakhi gayi--> no disqualification on basis of color,yada yada 
			
	
		- Bhai ke Financial Reforms : 
		
			- Officers ki salaries kam ki. 
			
			- Military ka Double Batta abolish kiya. 
			
			
		- Admin Reforms : 
		
			- Cornwallis wale, Provincial Courts of Appels abolish kar diye.----> Yaha bhayanak cases atke pade the. 
			
			- Lower courts me Local language intro karwai   and   English in Higher courts in place of Persian. 
			
			- R.M Bird ke under, NW Provinces me, Revenue settlement launch kiya. --> 30 saal ke period ke liye (Either with tillers of soil or with the landowners)
			
			
		- Social Reforms : 
		
			- Sati abolish kiya. ---> Regulation XVII on 4th December 1829. ----> Pehle Bengal me,  baad me, 1830 me Madras and Bombay me bhi. 
			
			- Thugs ki maa chodi. ---> 1830 by Colonel Sleeman. 
			
			- Bhrun hatya, maha paap. 					
			
			
		- English Education : 
		
			- Macaulay ke under ek committee appoint ki---> TO make recommendation on promotion of educatio. 
			
			- Mac ne bola ki sala English medium me padhao. , Euro lit and Science. 
			
			- so, Gov Resoluton in 1835---> Made, English the official and lit language of India. 
			
			- Usi, saal, bhai ne, Calcutta Medical College ki foundation rakhi. 
			
			
			
			
	Bhai ke baad, 1836 me aaya, Lord Auckland, or ye raha 1842 tak. 
	
		- Isi ke time, 1st Afghan War hua, from 1836 to 42. 
		
		- Maa chud jane ki wajah se ise wapas bula liya gaya. 
		
	
	Iske baad---> Lord Ellenborough---> Ended the Afghan war. 
	
		- Also, annexed Sindh. 
		
	
	Iske baad---> Lord Hardinge(1844 to 1848)
	
		- Fought the 1st Anglo-Sikh War (1845 to 46)
		
		- Concluded the treaty of Lahore. 
		
		
		
	Lord Dalhousie (1848 to 1856)
	
		- Bahi ko 36 saal ki umar me hi, GG appoint kiya gaya---> Youngest GG. 
		
		- Annexed---> Punjab,   Lower Burma,   Most of CP  and  Awadh				
		
		
			Punjab--> After the 2nd Anglo-Sikh War in 1849
			
				- Province ko small districts ke under divide kiya---> Under the control of District officers---> "Deputy Commissioners". 
				
				- Overall admin--> Entrusted to Chief Commissioner. 
				
				- 1859 me---> John Lawrence became the Lt Governor of Punjab. 
				
				
			
			Burma : 1852 me commercial disputes ki wajah se---> 2nd Burmese War. 
			
				- Major Arthur Phayre ko, commissioner of the province appoint kar diya gaya. 
				
				- Rangoon became the biggest port in Asia. 	
				
				
			
			Doctrine of Lapse : 1857 mutiny ke baad is policy ko withdraw kar liya gaya tha. 
			
				1) Satara ko khatra in 1848. 
				
				2) Jhansi and Nagpur in 1854. 
					
					
			
			1856 me, Awadh ko annex kiya on the pretext of Misrule. 
			
			
		
		Reforms by Dalhousie : 
		
			- Appointed a Lt Gov to Bengal
			
			- Naye provinces ko, modern centralized states me convert kiya----> Introduced the centralized control called ' Non-Regulation System' 
			
			- Isek under, har acquired territory me, ek commissioner appoint kiya jata tha. 
			
			- Bengal Artillery ka HQ, Calcutta se merut shift kiya.     Simla ko, Army ka perma HQ bana diya. 
			
			
			- Introduced Railway
			
				Guarantee System---> Min. interest of 5% to Railway companies on their investment.  Gov retained the right to buy the railway at the end of period of contract. 
				
				1st Railway line--> Bomaby to Thane in 1853. 
				
				Calcutta to Raniganj coal fields--> 1854
				
				Madras to Arakkonam ---> 1856
				
				
			- Introduction of Telegraph
			
				1852 me, O'Shaughnessy---> Appointed as the Superintendent of Telegraphy department. 
				
				Bombay, Calcutta, Peshawar and Madras were connected. 
				
				Dalhousie ke time, 4000 miles ki Telegraph lines bichai gayi. 
				
			
			- Modern Postal System 
			
				Post OFfice Act--> 1854
				
				Chahe kitna door post bhejna hai---> Uniform rate of 1/2 anna per post card was charged throughout India. 
				
				Postage stamps were introduced for the 1st time.
				
				
			- Charles Wood Dispatch : 1854 ----> "Intellectual Charter of India". 
			
				- Comprehnsive scheme of education at primary, secondary and collegiate levels. 
				
				- Dalhousie--> Organized Dept of Public Instructions. 
				
				- Universities of Calcutta, Bombay and Madras were founded in 1857. 
				
			
			- Public Works Department : PWD ka kaam, Dalhousie ke pehle, Military Board wale karte the. 
			
				- Bhai, ne, Canalas and Roads banane ke liye, PWD naam ka seperate dept banaya. 
				
				- 1854 me, Upper Ganges Canal complete hui.   Kai bridges banaye. 
				
				- Laid the foundation of Engineering Service in India. 
				
				
			- Bhai left India in 1856 and died in 1860. 
			
			
			
	Land Revenue System : 
	\
	
	
		- 19% of Brit India --> Bengal, Bihar, Banaras, North-Western Provinces ke kuch divisions ----> Under, Permanent Settlement a.k.a the Zamindari System. 
		
		-  30% of Brit India --> N-W Provinces ka major part,  CP and Punjab ---> Mahalwari System. 
		
		- 51% --> Under, Ryotwari System ---> Part of Bombay, Assam and Madras presidency. 
									
									
									
	Perma Settlement : 
	
		- Lord Cornwallish the Coward ----> P.S of Bengal---> Jise baad me, Bihar and Orissa me bhi extend kar diya gaya. 
		
		- Is se pehle, Warren Hastngs ne---> Annual lease system of auctioning land to highest bidder shuru kiya tha. 
		
		- BC, is se, Revene admin me chaos aa gaya tha. 
		
		
			Ise banane me bhai ko 3 saal lage the, or isme bhai ki madad ki---> Sir John Shore ne and James Grant ne. 
			
			Decide kiya---> Annual lease system ko abolish karke, Decennial sttlemetn le aaye---> 10 saal wala. 
			
			
		- Zamindaro ko, bola ki tum sala owner ho land ke, jab tak, tum EIC ko rent pay karte rahoge. 
		
		- Ye rent ka rate fix kar diya tha---> Or kasam khai thi ki, sala jo ho jaye rent nahi badhayenge. 
		
		- GoI ko milega 89% of the rent, baaki 11% zamindar ka. 
		
		- Saaalo ne, land ko survey karne ki or iski value assess karne ki koi koshish nahi ki. ----> Bas pichle collections ke rough estimates ke basis par. 
		
			Sala itna high revenue fix kiya ki, landlords default karne lage
			
			Aisa karne par, unki property sieze kar li jaati
			
			Inhone Agent rakhe hue the---> who excted all kinds of illegal taxes besides the legal ones from ryots. 
			
			So, Lord Cornwallis's Idea for building a system for benevolent land-lordism failed. 
										
										
										
										
		1833 me, Punjab, CP and N-W provinces ke kuch parts me Mahalwai system introduce kiya gaya. 
		
			- Basic unit of revenue settlement was the village or the Mahal. 
			
			- Revenue fix karte time, entire land of the village was measured. 								
			
			
			
	Is time, India me, important centres for textile production the : Krishnanagar, Chanderi, Arni and Banaras. 
	
		- Ahmedabad ke dhotis and Dupattas	
		
		- Lucknow ke Chikan
		
		- Nagpur ke silk borders
		
		- Kashmir, Punjab for their woolen garments. 
		
		
		India , textiles ke alawa famous tha---> shipping, Leather and metal industry ke liye. 
		
		- Precious stone polishing ke liye bhi. 
		
		- Carving of ivrory and sandalwood.
		
	
	Moradabad and Banaras---> Famous for Brass, COpper, Bronze utensils. 
	
	Nasik, Poona, Hyderabas and Tanjore---> Famous for other metal works. 
	
	Kutch, Sindh and Punjab---> FOr manufacturing arms
	
	Kolhapur, Satara, Gorakhpue, Agra, CHittor and Palghat ----> Glass industries. 
	
	
	
		1769 ---> COmpany encouraged the cultivation of raw silk in Bengal----> Imposed service restrictions on the sale of its finished products. 
		
		
		1813 ---> Company, ne strategy banai---> TO increase the consumption of Finished goods from Britain. 
		
			- Iske liye, tariff and Octroi policty ko modify kiya----> TO suit the British COmmercial interests. 		
			
			
	
	
	Brit Education policy : 1781 me---> Warren Hastings---> Established a Madrasa in Calcutta----> TO study mulsim law, Arabic and Persian. 
	
		- 1791 : Jonathan Duncan --> Resident of Banaras---> Sanskrit college khola---> To study the Hindu laws and philosophy. 
	
	
			
	Compnay rule ke start ke time----> Bengal akele me, 80,000 traditional institutions of learning the.    Laghbhag har gaanv me ek school tha. 
	
	
		- 1813 : CHarter Act----> 1 lakh Rs per annum for the spread of eduction in India. 
		
			- Lekin, sala agle 20 saalo tak debate ke chakkaro me, ek rupey bhi education par kharch nahi ho paya. 
			
			
			
		- 1829 : William Bentinck : Emphasized on the medium of English language in Indian Education. 
		
		- 1835 me, General Committee on Public Instruction ke 10 members---> Clearly divided into 2 equal groups ---> English or Oriental languages. 
		
			2nd February, 1835 ---> Macaulay's Minute
			
			7th March, 1835 ----> Aaj ke baad gov fund bas, WEstern Education and Science through English language par hi kharch kiya jayega. 
			
			
		- 1854 : Charles Wood Dispatch  : A Grand plan on education
		
			- 5 provinces me, Dept of Public Instructions establish kiye. 
			
			- Grant in Aids ke patterns introduce kiye gaye
			
			- Isme, Technical education, Teacher and Women education par bhi emphasis diya gaya tha. 
			
			- Establishment of University each in Calcutta, Bombay and Madras, on the model of London University. 
			
			- Agle kuch saal ome, Indian education became rapidly westernized. 
			
			
			
		
		J.P Grant----> Member of GG's Council---> 13th July, 1856 me, widow remarriage ke support me, bill pass karwaya ---> Widown Remariage Act, 1856. 
		
		
		
		1870 : Indian Reforms Association bani thi---> By, Keshav CHandra Sen---> Child Marriage ke against. 
		
			- 'Mahapap, Bal Vivah' ---> B.M Malabari ka, journal tha. 
			
			- 1846 me, minimum marriageable age--> FOr a girl was only 10 years. 
			
			- 1891 me, Age of Consent Act ke through, age, 12 saal raise kar di gayi. 
			
			
			- 1930 me, Sharda Act se, ye 14 years kar di gayi. 
			
			- Aajadi ke baad, 1978 me, ye 18 saal kar diya. 
			
			
		
		
		Caste Chutiyaap ke against : 
		
			'The Harijan' ----> Mahatma Gandhi ka ek paper
			
			- Gandhi ne, Harijan Sevak Sangh bhi shuru kiya. 
			
			
		
		B.R.A ne, 1924 me, Bahishkrit Hitkarni Sabha shuru kiya 
		
			- Baad me, Akhil Bharatiya, Dalit Varg Sabha shuru ki----> To fight caste oppression. 
			
	
		- Western India me---> Jyotirao Phule---> Satya Sadhak Samaj
		
		- Kerala me---> Sri Narayan Guru ---> Shri Narayan Dharma Partipalana Yogam
		
		
		- 20th CE - Madras Presidency ---> E.V.R Periyar ki---> Self-Respect movement. 
		
		
		
		
		
		
		
	Poligar Revolt : Palayakkaras against EIC
	
		- Vijaynagar rule ke Tamil nadu me extension ke saath hi, ye Poligar system evolve hua tha. 
		
		- Har poligar ke pass, Palayam naam ki territory hoti thi--> COnsisting of a few Villages---->> Jo ise, Military Service ke badle grant ki jaati thi 
		
		- Lekin, time ke saath ye saale arogant hone lage---> Regarded themselves as independent sovereign authorities within their respective Palayams. 
		
		
		
		- Inke 2 block the : West and East poligars. 
		
			Easten block ka learder tha----> Kattabomman of Panchalamkuruchi (Telugu)
			
			Western block ka leader tha ---> Puli Thevar or Nerkkattumseval (Marava)
			
		- In dono ne, Nawab ko, Kist yani Tribute dene se mana kar diya. ---> Rebelled. 
		
		
		- Mahfuz Khan ne, Brits ki madad se inke against expedition launch karwaya, under, Col Heron in March 175. 
		
			Puli Thevar ne, Madurai capture kar liya, from Mahfuz Khan. ---> Lekin, Yusuf ne baad me ise recapture kar liya. 
			
			Puli shaheed ho gaya. RiP bro. 
			
			
		
		Vir Pandya Kattabomman : Nawab of Arcot ne, Karatic Treaty of 1792 ke under, Tribute collection ke rights EIC ko transfer kar diye the. 
		
			- Katta ne, Brits ko tax pay karne se mana kar diya. 
			
			- Waha ka collector Jackson, Katta ke against army bhejna chahta tha, lekin Madras Gov ne mana kar diya. 
			
			- Ye apne minister, Sivasubramaia pillai ke saath, Jackson se hisab kitab karne gaya tha tab sala koi kaand ho gaya, or Lt Clarke naam ke bande ka sir utar gaya. Katta farar ho gaya.
			
			
			- Jab ye kaand chal raha tha, tab, Marudu Pandyan of Sivaganga ne, South Indian COndederacy of rebels organize kiye, against the Brits. 
			
				Tiruchirappali Proclamation was made. 
				
				Katta bhi isme kood pada. 
				
				
			- 1799 me Fuckboy ne, Madras se army bhej di, to Tirunelveli, under Major Bannerman. 
			
			- October tak, teeno ko latka diya gaya tha. 
			
			
		Marudu Brothers : 1799 me bhayanka repression ke baad bhi , 1800 me dobara, Poligar bhadak uthe. 
		
			- Marudu Pandian of Sivaganga
			
			- Gopala Nayak of Dindugal
			
			- Kerala Verma of Malabar
			
			- Krishnappa Nayak and Dhoondaji of Mysore. 
			
			
			
		- June 1800 me, Coimbator me insurrection shuru hua tha---> Spreak to Ramanathapuram and Madurai
		
			- 1801 tak---> Reached Northern Provinces ---> 'Marudu Pandian and Melapan' provided leadership. 
			
			- Katta ka bhai, Oomathurai ---> Emerged as a key leader. ----> 1801 me, Panchalamkuruchi Fort par kabja kar liya tha. ---> Became the centre of uprising. 
			
			
			- Brits ne, fort ko tod ke, waha castor oil and salt chidak diya---> So that it should never again be inhabited. 	
			
			
			
			
			
	Vellore Mutiny : 
	
		Vellore---> Tamil Nadu ke North Arcot district ka capital tha. 				
		
		
			- Native Sepoys rose in revolt in 1806. 
		
			- Ye pehla rebellion tha, jise Sepoys ne organize kiya tha. ---> Iske pehle ke sare, Native Rulers ke the. 
			
			
		
		Sir John Cradock--> Commander-in-chief---> Vidhwapremi Bentinck (then GOv of Madras) ki madad se, nai form ka turban introduce kiya---> Resembling a European Hat
		
			- Sepyos ko laga ki unhe Christian banaya ja raha hai. 
			
			
		
		May, 1806---> 4th Regiment rose in revolt against the new turban
		
			Fettah Hyder(1st Son of Tipu)---> Tried to form an alliance against the English and sought the help of Marathas and the French. 
			
		10th July---> Revolt start hua in 1st and 23rd regiment. 						
		
		
		Col. Gillespie ne 800 bande mare Vellore FOrt me. 
		
		
		
		
	
	1857 Revolt : Detailed analysis by : R C Majumdar, S.N Sen,  Sir John Lawrence,  Vir Savarkar	
	
	
			1833 : Ek naya planation system introduce kiya : Allowed Enlishmen to acquire land plantations. 
		
			Revolt shuru hua tha--> 29th March, 1857 me at Barrackpore 
			
				- Mangal Pandey --> Refused to use the greased cartridges and killed his officer. 
				
		
		Delhi : General Bakht Khan ----> Nicholson, Wilson, Baith Smith and Neville CHamberlain---> By September 1857. 
		
		
		Kanpur : By Nana Saheb ----> Adopted Son of Baji Rao II ---> Last Peshwa
		
			- Nana Saheb ke lieutants : Tantia Tope and Azimullah
			
			- Sir Hugh Wheeler ---> COmmander of British Garrison at Kanpur---> Surrendered on 27th June, 1857. 
			
			- Recapturecd by Sir Colin Campbell. 
			
			
		
		Lucknow : Begum of Awadh
		
			- Henry Lawrence was killed in a bomb blast. 
			
			- Sir Colin Campbell ne suppress kiya. 
			
		
		Jhansi : Gangadhar Rao ki widow ---> Dalhousie wale Doctrine of Lapse se inhe bhi pareshani hui thi. 
		
			- Tantia Tope ke saath milke, Gwalior capture kiya. 
			
			- Lekin, Sir Hugh Rose ne, Tantia Tope ko harake, Jhansi par kabja kar liya on 3rd April, 1858. 
			
			- Rose wahi nahi ruka or, Gwalior bhi capture kar liya. 
			
			- Rani of Jhansi died on 17th June, 1858. 
			
			- Tantia Tope was captured and Hanged. 
			
			
		
		Bihar : Kunwar Singh : Zamindar of Jagdishpur near Awadh. ----> Chief organiser of Revolt in Bihar. 
		
			- Jagdishpur me, 27th April 1858 ko dam tod diya. 
			
			
			
			
			
	Indian Admin was transfered from EIC to Crown by Queen's Proclamation of 1st November, 1858. 
	
		- GG received a new title of Viceroy. 
		
		- Lord Canning---> GG + 1st Viceroy according to Act of 1858.
		
		
		
	
	Lord Lytton Lundwale (1876 to 1880)
	
		- Is time, Famine aaya hua tha, or NW me halat kharab thi. 
		
		- 1st Famine Commuission appoint ki (1878 to 80) under, Sir Richard Starchey. 
		
		- Iske basis par, 1883 me Famine Code leke aaye. 
		
		
	
	Vernacular Press Act and the Arms Act (1878)
	
		- Ek magistrate, ki vernacular newspaper ke, Editor, Publisher ya Printer se, ek undertaking le sakta hai---> That nothing would be published against the English Government. 
		
		- Offence commit hone par, Press ke equipment hi sieze kar liye jayenge. 
		
		- Is act se, India Press ki freedom crush kar di gayi. 
		
		- Created adverse public opinion against the Brit Govl.  
		
	
	Same saal, bhai ne, Arms act pass kar diya----> Bina license ke, Indians ke Arms rakhne par ban. ---> Violation would be a criminal offence. 
	
		- Lekin, European and Anglo-Indians were exempted from this act. 
		
		
	
	
	Lord Lytton ne hi, Salt Tax introduce kiya tha throughout India. 
	
		- Abolished many import duties and supported the free trade Policy. 
		
		- Lord Mayo ke time, Decentralisation of Finance ka jo system shuru hua tha---> Was continued during the time of Lord Lytton. 
		
		- Lytton---> Wanted to encourage the provinces in collecting the revenue and thereby strength the financial power and position of the provinces. 
		
		
		
	
	1878 me, Statutory Civil Service was established---> Exclusively for Indians---> But this was abolished later. 
	
	
	
	Lytton and 2nd Afghan War : 1878 to 1880
	
		- Afghan policy based thi----> On the threat of Russian Invasion of India. 
		
		- 1st Afghan war(1838 to 1842) ---> Disaster for Brits. 
		
		- Lord Lytton ko ye bola tha ki, jaake, Forward Policy follow karni hai. 
		
	
		Main cause : Russian attempt to send a mission to Afghanistan
		
		
		1878 me Brits ne, Kabul and Kandahar capture kar liya---> 1879 me Sher Ali, desh chor ke bhaag gaya. 
		
			- Brits concluded the Treaty of Gandamak with Sher Ali's son--> Yakub
			
			- Lekin, afghans ne brit resident ko mar diya.  
			
			- 1780 me, Lytton was forced to resign. 
			
			
			
			
	
	
	Lord Rippon (1880 to 84)
	
		- Staunch Liberal Democrat with faith in Self-Government. 
		
		- GLadstone ne appoint kiya tha. 
		
		- Lytton ki afghan policy ko reverse karne ko bola tha ise. 
		
		- Peace was made. 
		
		
		- Restrored, Mysore to its Hindu Ruler. 
		
		- Repealed the Verna Press Act. 
		
		
	
	Introduction of Local Self-Gov (1882)
	
		- Muncis in Towns and Local Boards in Talukas and Villages
		
		- Muncis ki powers badhai. 
		
		- Charimen to be non-officials. 
		
		- Care of local amenities, Sanitation, Drainage and Water-Supply and Also primary education.  
		
		- Bhai ne, District and Taluka boards banae. 
		
		- Boards ke jyadatar members---> Should be elected non-officials. 
		
		- Local bodies----> Were given executive powers with Financial resources of their own. 
		
		- Insisted on election of local bodies against selection by the gov. 			
								
								
								
								
	Educational Reforms : Wanted to review the working of educational system on the basis of recommendations of Wood's Despatch.
	
		- 1882 me, Sir William Hunter ke under ek chairmanship banai. ---> Hunter Commission. 
		
		- Isne recommend kiya---> Expansion and improvement of elementary  education of the masses. 
		
		- 2 channels for Secondary education : 1-> Literary education--> Upto, University    2-> Vocational Education
		
		- Women education ke bure haal hain. 
		
		- Encouraged the local bodies in villages and towns to manage the elementary education. 
		
		- Is wajah se---> Resulted in extraordinary rise in the number of educational instis in India.
		
		
		
	
	
	1st Factory Act - 1881
	
		- Factory workers ki service condition improve karne ke liye. 
		
		- Banned the appointment of children below the age of 7 in factories. 
		
		- Reduced the working hours for CHildren. 
		
		- All dangerous machines in the factories to be properly fenced 
		
		
	
	Ilbert Bill Agitation - 1884
	
		- 1883 me, C.P Ilbert ---> Law Member-> Ek bill introduce kiya to abolish the bullshit system-> ek european ko bas Euro judge hi try kar sakta hai. 
		
		- Euros ne, Defence Association naam ki organisation banai---> and raised a fund of 1 lakh ruppes. 
		
		- Ripon amended the bill to satisfy the English in India and England. 
		
		
		- Is chutiyaap ki wajah se hi--> INC bani thi, 1885---> Ripon ke jaane ke 1 saal baad hi. 									
		
		
		
		
		
		
		
	Lord Curzon (1899 to 1905)
	
		- Educational Reforms : Bhai ke hisab se, Unis, factories ban gayi hain, for producing political revolutionaries. 
		
		- To set the educational system in order---> Instituted in 1902--> A Universities Commission---> To go into the entire question of university eduction in the country. 
		
		- Is basis par, Curzon ne, Indian Uni Act of 1904 leke aaya---> Brought all the unis in India under GoI control. 
		
		
	
	
	Police and Military Reforms : 1902 me ek Police Commission banai---> Under, Sir Andrew Frazer
	
		- Officers and COnstables, sab ke liye, Training Schools banae
		
		- Provincial Police Service introduce ki. 
		
		- Army ki remodeling ki---> By Lord Kitchener---> Commander-in-chief in India. 
		
		
	
	Calcutta Corporation Act (1899)
	
		- Strength of elected members was reduced---> That of official members was increased. 
		
		- Gave more representation to Brits, then Indians in Calcutta Corporation. 
		
		
	
	Preservation of Archeological Objects : 
	
		- Ancient Monuments Act, 1904 ---> Made it obligatory on the part of Gov and Local Authorities to preserve the momuments of Archeological importance and their destruction an offence.
		
		
	
	Partition of Bengal, 1905
	
		- 4th July, 1905.	
		
		- Led to anti-partition agitations all over the country. 			
		
		
		
		
		
	Socio-Religious Reforms Movements : 
	
		- For social and cultural awekening in India. 
		
		
		
		
		
	Raja Rammohan Roy ----> Brahmo Samaj establish kiya, 1828 me. ---> To Purify Hinduism and to preach Monotheism. 
	
		- Considered the 1st Modern Man of India. 
		
		- 1772 me bhai ka janam hua. 
		
		- 1815 me, Atmiya Sabha establish ki. ---> Jo, August, 1828 me, Brahmo Samaj ban gayi.
		
		- Bhai ne, Sab religions ko combine karne ki koshish ki.
		
		- Bhai ke jaane ke baad---> Work carried out by, Maharishi Debendrantah Tagore (Father of Rabindranath Tagore) ---> Renamed it Brahmo Samaj. 
		
		- Lord Bentinck ko, help kiya to declare, Sati illegale in 1829. 
		
		- Bhai ko, Caste System se bhi nafrat thi.  Favoured widow remarriage, female education and women's right to property. 
		
		- 1817 me, David Hare ke saath milke, ek Hindu COllege khola---> Now the presidency COllege, Calcutta. 
		
		- Started the 1st Bengali Weekly---> Samvad Kaumudi 
		
		- Edited a Persian weekly-->  Mirat-ul-akhbar
		
		- Died in Bristol in Enland in 1833. 
		
		
		
		
	
	Young Bengal Movement : By Henry Vivian Derozio : 
	
		- 1809 me, Calcutta me janam hua tha. 
		
		- 1833 me dam tod diya. DUe to CHolera. 
		
		- Derozians, debate karwate the against : Idol Worship,  Casteism  and  Superstitions. 
		
		
	
	
	Swami Dayanand Saraswati and the Arya Samaj
	
		- Swami D S ne, 1875 me, Bombay me, Arya Samaj Banaya tha. 
		
		- A scholor, A patriot, A Social Reformer and a Revivalist. 
		
		- Bhai ko lagta tha ki, Vedas hi, Source of True Knowledge hain. 
		
		- Bhai ka motto tha, " Back to the Vedas". 
		
		- Bhai was against---> Idol Worship, Child Marriage, Caste System based on birth. 
		
		- Bhai ne, Inter-caste and widow remarraige ko encourage kiya. 
		
		- Shuddhi movement start ki. 
		
		- 'Satyartha Prakash' naam ki book publish ki.
		
		
	
	Spread of educatio me bhi bhai ne kafi madad ki : 1st DAV School khula tha, 1886 me in Lahore. 
	
	
	
	Prarthana Samaj : 1867 me bana tha--> In Bombay by Dr Atmaram Pandulund. 
	
		- Ye, Brahmo-Samaj ka hi ek offshoot tha. 
		
		- Hinduism ke andar ek reform movement tha. 
		
		- SOcial reforms jaise ki---> Inter-dining,  Inter-marriage,  Widow Remarriage  and uplift of womenand depressed classes. 
		
		- Justice M.G Ranade   and  R.G Bhandarkar ne, 1870 me join kiya. 
		
		- Justice---> Also promoted the Deccan Educatio Society. 
		
		
		
		
	
	
	Ramakrishna Mission : Swami ji ka original naam tha---> Narendranath Dutta (1863 to 1902)
	
		- Bhai most famous desciple the--> Shri Ramkrishna Paramhamsa ke. 
		
		- Bhai kaafi paise wali party se the---> Was educated in Scottish Church College. 
		
		- 1886 me, narendranath bhai ne, Sanyas le liya---> Jiske baad, bhai ka naam ho gaya--> Vivekananda. 
		
		- Bhai, Vedantic Philosophy padhate the. 
		
		= USA me, 1893 me jo Parliament of Religions hui thi--> Chicago me--> Isme participate kiya tha. \\
		
		- 'Service to manking, is service to God'. 
		
		- 1897 me, Howarah ke Belur me, Ramkrishna Mission shuru kiya. ----> Social Service and Charitable society. 
		
		
		
	
	
	Theosophical Society : 1875 me, New York me bani thi, by Madam H.P Blavatsky--> Russian   and  Henry Steel Olcott--> An American colonel. 
	
		- To form the universal brotherhood of man without any distinction of race, color or creed. 
		
		- Madras ke Adyar me, 1882 me HQ banaya.
