		- 1893 me, Annie Besant arrived in India. ---> took over the leadership after death of Olcott. 
		
		- MMM ke saath milke, Benaras me, Central Hindu College khola. ---> Leter developed into BHU. 
		
		
	
	P.I.C.V : Educator, Humanist and Social Reformer. 
	
		- 1820 me janam hua tha---> Bengal ke Midnapur me. 
		
			- Fort Williams COllege ke Bengali Dept ke Head Pandit the. 
			
			- Girls ke liye bohot saare school khole. 
			
			- J.D.Bethune ki, Bethune Schools kholne me madad  ki. 
			
			- Calcutta me Metropolitan Institute khola. 
			
			- CHild Marriage ke against protest kiya and favoured widow remarriage---> Jise 1856 me, GoI ne bhi legalise kar diya. 			
		
			- APne, Education ko spread karne ke kaamo ki wajah se hi bhai ko, 'Vidyasagar' bulate the log. 
			
			
			
	
	Jyotiba Phule : Maharashtra ki ek low caste family ko belong karte the. 
	
		- Upper Caste domination + Brahmanical Supremacy ke against ke, life-long struggle launch ki. 
		
		- 1873 me, Satyashokhak Samaj found kiya---> TO fight against the caste system. 
		
		- Maharashtra me, Widow Remarriage movement pioneer kiya   and    worked for the education for women. 
		
		- 1851 me, Jyotiba Phule of uski wife ne, Poona me 1st Girl's School establish kiya. 
		
		


	Muslim Reform Movements : 
	
		- Ye kafi baad me shuru hui. 
		
		- 1863 me, Muhammad Literary Society bani, in Calcutta. ---> To popularise the study of English and WEstern Sciences. 
		
		- Bengal me kai sare school khole in logo ne. 
		
		
	
	Aligarh Movement : Sir Syed Ahmad Khan(1817 to 1898)
	
		- Social and Educational advancement of Muslims in India. 
		
		- 1866 me, Mohammadan Educational Conference shuru ki---> General Forum for spreading liberal ideas among the Muslims. 
		
		- 1875---> Founded a modern School at Aligarh ---> To Promote English Education among the Muslims. 
		
		- Baad me yahi grow karke---> Mohammadan Anglo-Oriental college bana---> Then into the Aligarh muslim Uni. 
		
	
	
	Deoband School : Muslim Ulema ke orthodox section ne, organise kiya. 
	
		- Ye ek revivalist movement thi---> Twin objective --> i) Propogate pure teachings of Koran   ii) Keep alive Jihad. 
		
		- Mahmud-ul-Hasan (1851 to 1920) : Leader. 
		
		
		
	
	
	Sikh Reform Movement : 
	
		1) Babad Dayal Das ne found kiya---> Nirankari Movement---> Worship of Formless god. 
		
		
		2) Baba Ram Sing--> Founded the Namdhari Movement----> Harbans wala. 
		
		
		3) 1870 me, Singh Sabha shuru hui---> Sikh society ko reform karne ke liye. 
		
			- Encouraged Gurmukhi and Punjabi Lit. 
			
			- 1892 me, Khalsa COllege khulwaya, Amritsar me. 
		
			- 1920 ---> Corrupt Mahants ko hatane ke liye movement shuru ki. 
		
		
	
	
	Parsi Reform Movement : 1851 me, Bombay me found hua, by Furdunji Naoroji  and S.S Bengalee. 
	
		- Women Education ko spread karne ko advocate kiya. 
		
		- Naoroji---> 'Jagat Mithra' naam ka ek monthly journal publish kiya. 
		
		
		
	
	Saint Ramalinga : 19th CE Tamil Nadu ke foremost saint. 
	
		- 1865 me---> Samrasa Shuddha Sanmargha Sangha  found kiya----> Casteless society ke ideals promote karne ke liye. 
		
		- Logo ko love and compassion preach kiya. 
		
		- 'Tiru Arutpa' compose kiya. 
		
		- Or lit : 'Manu Murai Kanda Vasagam' and 'Jeeva Kaunyam'. 
		
		- 1872 me, Satya Gnana Sabha shuru ki. 
		
		- Introduced the principle that God could be worshipped in the form of light. 
		
		
		
	
	Sri Vaikunda Swamigal : 1809 me janam hua in the Kanyakumari district of Tamil Nadu. 
	
		- Original name : Mudichoodum Perumal---> Caste System and Untouchablity ke against preach kiya. 
		
		- Teachings came to be known as : "Ayyavazhi". 
		
		
		
		
		
	Self-Respect Movement and Periyar E.V.R
	
		- Periyar E.V.Ramaswamy/ 
		
		- 1921 me, Anti-liquor campaign ke time, apne hi farm me lage, 1000 coconut ped, kaat daale. 
		
		- 1924 me, Vaikom Satyagraha me active participation kiya. ----> Kerala ke Vaikom me, Mandir ke paass ek sadak ko, Untouchable log bhi use kar sake. 
		
		- 1920 se 1925 ke beech INC join ki---> Stressed that, Congress should Accept Communal Representation. 
		
		- 1925 me, 'Self-Respect Movemtn' shuru ki---> To uplift the Dravidians and to expose the Brahmanical Tyrany
		
		- Denounced the caste system,  Child marriage and enforced widowhood. 
		
		- Inter-Caste marriages ko encourage kiya,  CHild Marriage, Caste system ko oppose kiya. 
		
		- Bina rituals ke kai shaadia karwai. ---?> "Self-Respect Marriage". 
		
		- Founded many Tamil Journals --> 1) Kudiarasu    2) Puratchi    3) Viduthalai
		
		- 1938 : Tamil Nadu Women's Conference ne "Periyar" ka title diya. 
		
		
		
		
		
		
	Early Political Associations : 
	
		1) British Indian Association : 1851 - Bengal
		
		
		2) Bombay Association : 1852 by Dadabhai Naoroji
		
		
		3) East India Association : 1856 London
		
		
		4) Madras Native Association : 1852. 
		
		
		5) Poona Sarvajanik Sabha : 1870
		
		
		6) Madras Mahajana Sabha : 1884
		
		
		
	
	
	Indian National Congress - 1885. 
	
		- Allan Octavian Hume. 
		
		- 1st Session : Bombay in 1885----> W.C Banerjee--> 1st President---> 72 delegates ne attend kiya. 
		
		- Shuru se hi ye ek, All-India-Secular Movement thi. 
		
		
	
	- 2nd Session : Calcutta in 1886. 
	
	- 3rd Session : Madras in 1887. 
	
	
	
	3 Important Phases of Indian National Movement : 
	
		1) Phase of Moderate Nationalism (1885 to 1905)
		
			- Congress continued to be loyal to the British Crown. 
			
		2) 1906 to 1916 --> Swadeshi Movement ,  Rise of Militant Nationalism  and   The Home Rule Leage. 
			
		3) 1917 to 19147 : Gandhian Era. 
			
		
		
		
	Moderate Nationalism :
	
	    Leading Figures : A.O Hume, W.C Banerjee, Surendra Nath Banerjee,  Dadabhai Naoroji,  Feroze Shah Mehta,  Gopalkrishna Gokhale,  Pandit MMM,  Badruddin Tyabji, Jutice Ranade, G.Subramaniya Aiyar	
		
		 
	    Surendranath Banerjee--> 'Indian Burke'. 
	    
	    	Bengal Partition ko firmly oppose kiya. 
	    	
	    	- 1876 me, Indian Association banai. ---> To agitate for political reforms. 
	    	
	    	- 1883 me, Indian National Conference karwai---> Merged with INC in 1886. 
	    	
	    
	    
	    G.Subramanya Aiyar :---> Preached nationalism through the Madras Mahajan Sabha
	    
	    	- The Hindu and Swadesamitran bhi found kiya. 
	    	
	    	
	    
	    
	    Dadabhai Naoroji---> Grand Old Man of India. ----> India's Unofficial ambassador in England. 
	    
	    	- 1st Indian to become a Member of British House of Commons. 
	    	
	    	
	    
	    Gopal Krishna Gokhale : Political Guru of Gandhi. 
	    
	    	- 1905 me, founded the Servants of India Society----> To train Indians to dedicate their lives to the cause of the country. 
	    	
	    	
	    	
	    1905 tak ye saare Moderates thi, isliye, GoI bhi inhe patronize karti thi. 
	    
	    	Main demands : 
	    	
	    		1) Expansion and Reform of Legislative councils. 
	    		
	    		2) ICS exam ko India me bhi karwao. 
	    		
	    		3) Judiciary ko executive se alag karo. 
	    		
	    		4) Local Bodies ko jyada powers do. 
	    		
	    		5) Land Revenue kam karo and Peasants ko Unjust landlords se bachao. 
	    		
	    		6) Salt Tax and Sugar Duty ko abolish karwao. 
	    		
	    		7) Army ki spending kam karo. 
	    		
	    		8) Freedom of Speech and Expression and Freedom to form associations. 
	    		
	    		
	    	
	    
	    Methods of Moderates : Petitions, Resolutions, Meetings, Leaflets and Pamplets, memorandum and Delegations
	    
	    	
	    	Inki bas ek hi demand mani Brits ne---> Expansion of Councils by the Indian Councils Act of 1892. 
	    	
	    	
	    	
	    	Brits ne, Dadabhai ki 'Poverty and Unbritish Rule in India' me Drain Theory batai----> Brits ne, is se darke--> "Welby Commision"  with Dadabhai is the 1st Indian as its member, appoint ki. 	 		
		 			
		 			
		 			
		 			
	Extremism : 1905 ke baad ka time. 
	
		Leaders : Lala,  BGT,  BC Pal   and   Aurobindo Ghosh. 
		
		Reasons : 
		
			Kyunki moders, wo council act ke alawa kuch nahi ukhaad paye. 
			
			1896-67 me, Plagues and Famines aaye bohot saare. 
			
			Ill-Treatment of Indians in South-Africa
			
			Russo-Japanese War of 1904-05. 
			
			Reactionary rule of Curzon
			
			
		Ye bas Self-Gov se khush nahi the,  Inhe chahiye tha, Swaraj or Complete independence. 
		
		They had no faith in Brit sense of Justice and Fair Play. 
		
		
			Inke methods : Non-Cooperatoin- Boycott Schools, Colleges and courts.     Promote Swadeshi and Boybott of foreign goods.       Intro and Promotion of National Education. 	
		
					 			
		
		BGT : Real founder of popular anti-British movement in India. 	 			
		 			
		 	- 'lokmanya'. 
		 	
		 	- Weeklies- The mahratta   and   Kesari. 
		 	
		 	- 2 baar jail hui ---> 1908 me,Mandalay me depart kar diya gaya, 6 saalo ke liye. 
		 	
		 	- 1916 me, Poona me, Home Rule Leage setup ki and declared----> "Swaraj is my birth right and I will have it". 
		 	
		 	
		 
		 LLR : "Lion of Punjab". 
		 
		 	- US me, 1916 me, Indian Home Rule Leage found ki. 
		 	
		 	- Bhai ko bhi, Sedition ke charges par, Mandalay transport kar diya gaya tha. 
		 	
		 	- 17th Nov, 1928 me, Simon COmmission ke against procession ke time, dande padne se dam tod diya. 		
		 			
		 			
		 			
		 Bipan Chandra Pal : Started as a moderate and turned an extremist. 
		 
		 	- Swadeshi Movement me imp role nibhaya. 
		 	
		 	
		 	
		 	
		 
		 Aurobinda Ghosh : Swadeshi Movement
		 
		 	- Bhai ko bhi jail hui
		 	
		 	- Jail se chutne ke baad, Pondicherry ki Frenc Territory me chale gaye and concentrated on Spiritual activities. 
		 	
		 	
		 	
	Partition of Bengal : 
	
		- 16th October, 1905---> The day, when partition came into effect---> People of Bengal, organised protest meetings and observed a day of mourning. 
		
		- Gandhi ne bola tha ki, " Read awekening in India took place only after the partition of Bengal". 
		
		- Culminated into Swadeshi Movement and spread to other parts of India. 
		
		
	- Extremist logo ke dawab me, 1906 ke Calcutta Congress session me--> Dadabhai Naoroji ne bhi, Swaraj ke baare me bola---> Even though ye ek moderate demand thi hi nahi. 
	
		Adopted the Boycott and Swadeshi resolution. 
		
		Lekin, Moderates ki fat gayi, kyunki wo bas, consti  methods ke through hi, Swaraj achieve karna chahte the. 
		
	
	- Is difference ki wajah se hi, 1907 me, Congress split ho gayi, at the Surat Session. ---> "Surat Split". 
	
	
	Swadeshi Movement : Both a Political and Economic Movement. 
	
		- It was a great success. 
		
		- Bengal me to saala, Landlords tak kood pade. 
		
		- Women and Students took to picketing. 
		
		- GoI ne, "Bande mataram" bolne tak par ban laga diya. 
		
		
		- Jail hui : BGT,  LLR,  BC Pal  and  Aurobindo Ghosh ko, 
		
		
		
		
	Muslim League (1906)
	
		- 1906 me, Muslim delegates from all over India met at Dacca for the Muslim Educational Conference. 
		
			Nawab Salimullah of Dacca ne is meeting ka fayda uthae hue : Proposed, the setting up of an organisation to look after the Muslim interests. 
			
			
		- All-India Mulsim League was finally setup on Dec 30th, 1906. 
		
		- Ye bhi INC ki tarah annual sessions kiya karte the---> to put their demands to the Brit Gov
		
		- Shuru me GoI ne inka khub swagat Satkaar kiya. 
		
		- Their 1st achievments : Separate Electorates for the Muslims in the Morley-Minto Reforms. 
		
		
	
	
	Lucknow Pact (1916)
	
		- Divided congress, unite ho gayi. 
		
		- Joint action against the Brits, ki ek understanding reach ki gayi, by signing the Lucknow Pact by the Congress and Muslim League in 1916--> Imp step for Hindu-Muslim Unity. 
		
		
	
	Home Rule Movement (1916) : To get self-Gov for India within the Brit Empire. 
	
		- 1st by B.G.T at Poona in April 1916. ---> Concentrated on maharashtra. 
		
		- 2nd by Annie at Madras in Sept 1919. ---> Covered the rest of India. 	
		
		
	Both leagues, cooperated with each other and with INC and Mulla League. . 
	
		
	Revived the Swadeshi movement. 
	
	Women joined in Large numbers. 	
		
		
		
	
	20th August, 1917 : Secy of State, Montague ne, Declare kiya in Brit Parliament --> On Gov's Policy towards future political reforms in India. 
	
		- Promised the gradual dvpt of Self-Governing instis in India. 
		
		- August Declaration--> Led to the end of the Home Rule Movement. 
		
		
	
	Revolutionary Movements : Bengal, Maharashtra, Punjab and Madras. 
	
		Bengal : Anushilan Samiti and Jugantar
		
		Maharashtra : Savarkar bhaio ne milke banaya---> Abhinav Bharat. 
		
		Madras : Nilakanta Bramachari ne banaya, Bharathmatha Association. 
		
		Punjab : Ajit Singh ne, ek secret society banai, to spread revolutionary ideas among the Youth. 
		
		London : India House---> Shyamjo Krishna Verma, gathered Young Indian Nationalists like, Madan Lal Dhingra, Savarkar, VVS Iyer and TSS Rajan, 
		
		USA : Lala Hardyal Setup 'Ghadar Party' in USA to organise revolutionary activites from Outside India. 
		
		
		
		
	Gandhi : Non-Violence and Satyagraha. ---> Made, nationalist movement, a mass movement. 
	
		- Bhai ka janam hua at Porbandar in Gujrat on 2nd October, 1869. 
		
		- April, 1893--> Went to South Africa, and struggled against Apartheid for 20 years. 
		
		- Came back in 1915. 
		
		
	
	Champaran : 1917 : Satyagraha against opperessive Indigo planters. 
	
	
	Kehda : 1918 : Satyagraha in support of peasants---> Not able to pay land tax due to failure of crops. 
	
		- Patel emerged as one of the trusted followers of Gandhi. 
		
	
	Ahmedabad : 1918 :--> Fast unto death for the cause of Ahmedabad Mill Workers
	
	
	
	Rowlatt Act(1919)
	
		- 1917 me, Sir Sydney Rowlatt ke under, Militant Nationalist activities ko rokne ke liye ek committee bani. 
		
		- Iski report ke basis par, Rowlatt act pass kiya gaya in 1919 by, Central Legislative Council. 
			
			Kisi bhi bande ko, suspicion ke basis par arrest kar liya jayega. 
			
			Is arrest ke against, koi appeal ya petition tak file nahi kar sakte. 
			
		
		- Is act ko log, Black Act bolne lage--> Widely opposed. 
		
		
		6th April, 1919 ko, All-India Hartal organize kiya .    Pure desh me meetings hue.     Gandhi ko Delhi ke pass arrest kar liya gaya. 
		
			
			Satyapal and Saifuddin Kitchlew ko, Amritsar ke pass arrest kar liya gaya. 
			
			
			
		Jallianwala Bagh Massacre : 13th April, 1919
		
			- Rabindranath Tagore renounced his knighthood
			
			
		Treaty of Sevres (1920)
		
			- After defeat of Turkey in 1st WW. 
			
			- Iske badle, khilafat movement shuru hua. 
			
		
			Prominent leaders of the movement : Maulana Abdul Kalam Azad,   M.A Ansari,   Saifuddin Kitchlew   and  Ali Brothers. 
			
			19th October 1919 ko, Khilafat Day observe kiya gaya. 
			
			23 Nov, 1919 ko, Hindus and Muslim leaders ko joint conference hui, under Chairmanship of Gandhi. 
			
			Iske baad, Khilafat Movement merged with NCM , jise gandhi ne 1920 me launch kiya. 
			
			
		
		
		Non-Cooperation Movement (1920 to 1922)
		
			- Against, Rowlatt Act, Jallianwala Bagh Massacre and Khilafat Movement. 
			
			- INC ne, Dec 1920 ke Nagpur Session me approve kiya. 
			
			
			Iske under kya karne wale the ye log ? 
			
				- Titles and Honorary positions ka Surrender. 
				
				- Local Bodies ki membership se resignation. 
				
				- 1919 act ke under hone wale elections ka boycott. 
				
				- Gov Functions ka boycott
				
				- Courts, Schools, Colleges ka boycott. 
				
				- Foreign Goods ka boycott. 
				
				- National Schools, Colleges, and Private Panchayat COurts ko establish karna\
				
				- Swadeshi Goods and Khadi ko popularize karna. 
				
				
			Sabse pehle, Bapu ne title chore. 
			
			National Schools bane : Kashi Vidyapeeth,  Bihar Vidyapeeth  and  Jamia Millia Islamia
			
			Prominent leaders gave up theri lucarative legal practice. 
			
			Legislatures ka boycott kiya gaya. 
			
			
			1921 me, Prince of Wales India ke tour par aaya--> TO mass demonstrations kiye gaye. 
			
			GoI ne, Congress and Khilafat committees ko illegal declare kar diay or Leaders ko jail bhej diya. 
			
			
			
			11th Feb, 1922---> Movement was called off by, Churi CHaura incident. 
			
			10th March, 1922---> Gandhi was arrested. 	
								
				 				
				 				
				 				
	Non-Cooperation Movement ke suspend hone se Congress party suspend ho gayi---> At Gaya Session in Dec 1922. 
	
		-  Moti and C.R Das =---> Seperate groups bana liye, within Congress, known as Swaraj Party on 1st January 1923. 
		
			Ye log, Gov join karke, use andar se khatam kar dena chahte the. 
			
		
		- Legislative councils ke liye elections hue, Nov 1923 me----> Swaraj Party gained impressive success. 
		
		- Central Legislative Council me---> Motilal Nehru became the leader of the party,     Whereas in Bengal the party was headed by C.R Das
		
		
		Home Member, Alexander Muddiman ke under, jab ek committe ne, Dyarchy ko proper bola to, bhaio ne, Central Legislative Council me iske agaisnt ek resolution pass karwaya. 
		
		- Lekin, C.R Das died in 1925, jiske baad, Swaraj party kamjor ho gayi. 
		
		
	
	
	
	Simon Commission (1927)
	
		- 7 ke 7 members, Brit the. ---> So faced a lot of Criticism. 
		
		- 3rd Feb 1928---> Comission reached Bombay---> General hartal was observed all over the country. 
		
			Lahore me students ne, ek, Anti-Simon commission demostrations kiye. --> on 30th October, 1928---> Under leadership of Lala. 
			
		
		- May 1930 me, Simon Commission ki report publish ki gayi. 
		
			Dyarchy is a sucks. 
			
			Iski jagah, Autonomus governments establish karo. 
			
			Became basis for enacting the GoI Act of 1935. 
			
			
		
		Nehru Report(1928)
		
			1) Secy of State, Lord Dickhead ne, Indians ko khudka constitution banae ke liye challenge kiya. ---> Jo sabko acceptable ho. 
			
				Accepted by Congress---> Convened an all party meeting on 28th Feb, 1928. 
				
				8 logo ki ek committee banai---> To draw up a blueprint for the future consti of India. ---> Headed by Moti. 
				
				Iski report ko, Nehru Report kehte hain. 
				
					1) Dominon status do, as an immediate next step. 
					
					2) Centre me, fully responsible gov banao. 
					
					3) Provinces ko autonomy do
					
					4) Centre of Provinces ke beech, clear cut division of power karo. 
					
					5) Centre me ek bicameral legislature banao. 
					
				
				Mulla League ke leader, Jinnah ne bola ki ye, Mullo ke interests ke agianst hai. 
				
				Jinnah ne, All India Conference of Muslims bulai---> Drew up a list of 14 points as Muslim League Demand. 
				
				
		
		
		Civil Disobedience Movement (1930 to 1934)
		
			- December 1929 ko, INC ka Lahore Session hua----> Presided over by Jawaharlal Nehru----> "Poorna Swaraj" resolution was passed. 
			
			- GoI ne, Nehru Report se apni gand ponch li thi, to, COngress gave a call to launch CDM. 
			
			- Observed 26th January, 1930 as the Day of Independence. 
			
			
			
	
	Dandi March :  12th March, 1930 
	
		- 79 followers ke saath---> To break the Salt law. ---> Reached Dandi on 5th April, 1930.   or agle din, Salt Law break karke, CDM launch kiya. 
		
		- 9th April ko--> Bapu laid out the programme of the movement---> 
		
			1) Making Salt in every village in violation of the existing salt laws. 
			
			2) Picketing by women before the shops selling liquor, Opium and Foreign Clothes. 
			
			3) Organising the bonfires of Foreign clothes
			
			4) Spinning clothes using Charkha fighting untouchability
			
			5) Boycott of schools and colleges
			
			6) Resigning from Gov Jobs
			
			7) Stop paying taxes
			
			
		GoI ne, imp Congress leaders ko arrest kar liya. 
		
		
		
	Round Table Conference : 
	
		1) 1st RTC : Nov 1930 at London---> Boycotted by INC. 
		
			
			1931 me, GoI lifted ban on Congress party and released its leaders from prison. 
			
			
			8th March, 1931 ko--> Gandhi-Irwin Pack sign hua
			
				Bapu agreed to suspend the CDM  and   to participate in the 2nd RTC. 
				
				
		
		
		2) 2nd RTC : Sept 1931 at London. 
		
			No agreement could be reached on the demand of complete independence and on the communal question. 
			
			
			Jan 1932--> CDM was resumed. 
			
				GoI arrested Gandhi and Sardar Patel  and   Reimposed ban on INC. 
				
				
		
		
		
		Poona Pact (1932)
		
			- 1930 tak, BRA had become, leaders of the depressed people of the country. 
			
			- 1st RTC me bhai ne, depressed classes ki halat sabko batai and asked for separate electorates for them. 
			
			
			- 16th August---> Ramsay MacDonald---> "Communal Award" wali announcement. 
			
				Depressed classes were considered a separate community    and    as such they were given separate electorates. 
				
				
			- Bapu went mad and stopped eating, on 20th September 1932, while he was in Yeravada Jail. 
			
			
			- Finally he reached an agreement with BRA---> "Poona Pact". ---> Even the Brits approved it. 
			
				71 ki jagah, ab 148 seats, Depressed classes ke liye, reserved hongi.
				
				
	
	3rd RTC---> 1932 me hua.   INC stayed away. 
	
	March 1933---> Brit Gov--> Issued a White Paper---> Became basis for the enactment of the GOI Act, 1935. 
	
	
		1937--> GoI Act, 1935 ke provisions ke under, elections hue. 
		
		
			Congress formed Ministries in 7 states of India. 
			
		
		
		1st Sept 1939 ko, 2nd World War shuru ho gaya. 
	
	
			12th December, 1939---> Congress Ministries resigned. ===> Muslim Leage celebrated this day as 'Deliverance Day'. 
			
			March, 1940 ---> Mulla Leage, demanded for Creation of Pakistan. 
	
	
	
	Individual Satyagraha : 
	
		8th August, 1940 ---> 'August Offer' was made, to secure cooperation of Indian in WW II. 
		
			War ke baad, Indians ki ek representative body bana denge. ---> To Frame the new constitution. 
			
			Gandhi was not satisfied and decided to launch Individual Satyagraha 
			
				Limited,  Symbolic  and Non-Violent in Nature. 
				
				Left to Bapu to choose the Satyagrahis. 
				
				
				Acharya Vinoba Bhave---> 1st to offer Satyagraha ---> 3 mahine ki jail hui. 
				
				Jawahar Lal Nehru ---> 2nd to offer ----> 4 mahine ki Jail hui. 
				
				Agle 15 mahine ye chutiyaap  chalta raha. 
				
				
	
	Cripps mission (1942)
	
		July 1941 me, Viceroy Linlithgow ne apni Executive council ko expand kar diya, by including 5 more Indians. 
		
		Isi time, Brits ne, Indian coop ke liye, Sir Stafford Cripps ko Indian bheja on, 23rd March, 1942. ---> Cripps Mission. 
		
		
		
			1) Indian ko Dominion Status de denge
			
			2) Minorities ko protection do
			
			3) Conti Assembly banayenge, jisme, British Provinces and Princely States, dono ke represntative honge. 
			
			4) Jis Brit province ko, naya consti nahi pasand aayega, wo present wala continue kar legi, ya BC khudka naya consti bana legi. 
			
			
		
		Major Political Parties ne ye reject kar diya. 
		
		Gandhi ne bola ki, "Ye sala to ek post-dated cheque ki tarah hai". 
		
		Congress ko nahi pasand tha, ki PRincly state wale bhi apne represntative bheje or agar na pasand aaye consti to khudka bana le, BC ye kya bakwas hai. 
		
		
		Mulla League bhi gusse me thi, kyunki Demand for Pakistan had not been conceded in the proposal. 
		
		
		
		
	Quit India Movement (1942 to 1944)
	
		- Gandhi ko laga ki sala Japs invade karne wale hain or Cripps Mission bhi fail ho gaya tha. 
		
		- Bapu ko lagta tha ki ek Interim GOv bas tabhi ban sakti hai jab, Brits chor ke nikal lenge or jab, Hindu-Muslim chutiyaap bhi khatam ho jayega. 
		
		- All India Congress Committee---> Met in Bombay on 8th August, 1942. ----> Passed the 'Quit India Resoluton'. ---> Isi din Bapu ne bola, 'Do or Die' and then he died...OH I'm just kidding. 
		
		- 8th and 9th August, 1942 ko---> GoI Arrested, all prominent leaders of the Congress. 
		
		
			Indians were left leaderless. 
			
			Bapu ko Poona me,      Nehru , Azad or Baki leaders ko,, Ahmednagar Fort me,  Jail me rakha. 
			
			
			
		- So, leadership was provided by : Ram Manohar Lohiya,   Achyuta  and  S.M Joshi		
		
		
		- 1943 me, Gov ki buildings par bhi attack hona shuru ho gaye the. 
		
		- 1944 ---> Gandhi ko jail se chor diya. 
		
		
		Total,60k logo ko jail hui.      7000 log mare gaye. 
		
		
	
	
	Indian National Army : 
	
		- 2nd July, 1943 ---> Bose gave the call of Delhi Chalo.    Was made the President of Indian Independence League. 
		
		- Soon became the Supreme Commaner of INA> 
		
		- Jai Hind ka slogan diya. 
		
		- 18th August, 1945 ---> Died during a plane crash. 
		
		
		
		
	
	Cabinet Mission (1946)
	
		- 2nd WW ke baad----> Atlee became the PM of England. 
		
		- 15th March, 1946---> Right to self-determination was conceded. 
		
		- Brit Cabinet ke 3 members---> Pahick Lawerence,  Sir Stafford Cripps and A.V.Alexander ko India bheja gaya.  ---> Yahi tha Cabinet Mission. 
		
		
		
			Consti Problem ke liye unhone ek plan diya. 
			
			3 groups of provinces to posses their separte constis. 
			
			Union of India bana denge---> Comprising both the British India and Princley States. 
			
			- Union charge me hoga of--> Foreign Affairs,  Defence  and communications----> Residuary powers to be vested in the provinces. 
			
			
			Interim Gov ke liye ek proposal diya gaya---> Would remain in office till a new Gov was elected on the basis of the new consti, framed by the COnsti Assembly. 
			
			
			
			Muslim league and COngress, both accepted the plan. 
			
			
		
		July 1846 me, elections hue. 
		
			Congress ko 214 General Seats me se 205 mili. 
			
			Muslim League ko, 78 muslim seats ki 73 seats mili. 
			
			
		Interim Gov was formed under the leadership of Jawaharlal Nehru on 2nd September, 1946. 
		
		
		
		
	Moutbatten Plan (1947)
	
		- 20th Feb, 1947----> Atlee ne, House of Commons me announce kiya---> June 1948 tak power transfer kar denge. 
		
		- Mountabatten ko, 24th March, 1947 ko, India ka Viceroy bana diya gaya. 
		
		
		
		- 3rd June, 1947 ko, Partition of India ka plan rakha gaya. 
		
		- COngress and Muslim league---> Approved the Mountbatten Plan.
